import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/shops_model.dart';

class ModernShopGallery extends StatelessWidget {
  final int imageCount;
  final String shopTitle;

  const ModernShopGallery({
    super.key,
    required this.imageCount,
    required this.shopTitle,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final imageUrls = ShopData.getShopGalleryImages(shopTitle);
    final displayCount = imageCount > imageUrls.length
        ? imageUrls.length
        : imageCount;

    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Gallery',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          _buildMasonryLayout(displayCount, imageUrls),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMasonryLayout(int count, List<String> imageUrls) {
    if (count == 0) return _buildEmptyState();

    return Container(
      height: 350,
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // Left column - taller images
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                _buildImageCard(imageUrls[0], height: 200),
                if (count > 2) SizedBox(height: 8),
                if (count > 2) _buildImageCard(imageUrls[2], height: 142),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(width: 8),
          // Right column - shorter images
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                if (count > 1) _buildImageCard(imageUrls[1], height: 142),
                if (count > 1) SizedBox(height: 8),
                if (count > 3) _buildOverlayImageCard(count - 3, imageUrls[3], height: 200),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildImageCard(String imagePath, {required double height}) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Color(0x42000000),
              blurRadius: 8,
              offset: Offset(0, 4),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          child: Image.asset(
            imagePath,
            fit: BoxFit.cover,
            width: double.infinity,
            height: height,
            errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
              return Container(
                height: height,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Color(0xFF424242),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                ),
                child: Icon(Icons.error, color: Color(0xFF9E9E9E)),
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOverlayImageCard(int remainingCount, String imagePath, {required double height}) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Stack(
        fit: StackFit.expand,
        children: [
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            child: Image.asset(
              imagePath,
              fit: BoxFit.cover,
              width: double.infinity,
              height: height,
            ),
          ),
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              color: Color(0x8A000000),
            ),
            child: Center(
              child: Text(
                '+$remainingCount',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                  fontSize: 28,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyState() {
    return Container(
      height: 200,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        color: Color(0xFF424242),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0xFF616161)),
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.photo_library, size: 48, color: Color(0xFF9E9E9E)),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'No images available',
              style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF9E9E9E)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class Animated3DIcon extends StatefulWidget {
  final double size;
  final IconData icon;
  final dynamic  unselectedColor;
  final dynamic  selectedColor;
  final Duration duration;
  final double rotation;
  final double scale;
  final bool enableShadows;
  final bool enableHighlights;

  const Animated3DIcon({
    Key? key,
    required this.size,
    required this.icon,
    this.unselectedColor = Colors.grey,
    this.selectedColor = Colors.red,
    this.duration = const Duration(milliseconds: 800),
    this.rotation = 2 * 3.14159, // Full rotation in radians
    this.scale = 1.3,
    this.enableShadows = true,
    this.enableHighlights = true,
  }) : super(key: key);



Color _parseColor(dynamic color) {
  if (color is Color) {
    return color;
  } else if (color is String) {
    return _hexToColor(color);
  } else {
    return Colors.grey; // Fallback color
  }
}

Color _hexToColor(String hexColor) {
  hexColor = hexColor.replaceAll('#', '');

  // Handle different hex formats
  if (hexColor.length == 6) {
    hexColor = 'FF$hexColor'; // Add full opacity if not provided
  } else if (hexColor.length == 8) {
    // Already has alpha channel
  } else {
    throw FormatException('Invalid hex color format: $hexColor');
  }

  return Color(int.parse(hexColor, radix: 16));
}

  @override
  Animated3DIconState createState() => Animated3DIconState();
}

class Animated3DIconState extends State<Animated3DIcon> {
  bool _isSelected = false;
  bool _isAnimating = false;

  Color get _unselectedColor => widget._parseColor(widget.unselectedColor);
  Color get _selectedColor => widget._parseColor(widget.selectedColor);

  void _toggleIcon() async {
    if (_isAnimating) return;

    setState(() {
      _isAnimating = true;
      _isSelected = !_isSelected;
    });

    await Future.delayed(widget.duration);

    setState(() {
      _isAnimating = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: _toggleIcon,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: widget.duration,
        curve: Curves.elasticOut,
        transform: _isAnimating
            ? _createCoinFlipTransform()
            : Matrix4.identity(),
        transformAlignment: Alignment.center,
        child: _build3DIcon(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Matrix4 _createCoinFlipTransform() {
    final double progress = _isAnimating ? 1.0 : 0.0;

    return Matrix4.identity()
      ..setEntry(3, 2, 0.001) // Perspective
      ..rotateY(progress * 3.14159); // 180 degrees around Y axis
  }

  Widget _build3DIcon() {
    final currentColor = _isSelected ? _selectedColor : _unselectedColor;
    final shadowOffset = widget.size * 0.04;
    final highlightOffset = widget.size * -0.025;

    return SizedBox(
      width: widget.size + (shadowOffset * 2),
      height: widget.size + (shadowOffset * 2),
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          // Shadow layers
          if (widget.enableShadows) ...[
            _buildShadowLayer(offset: Offset(shadowOffset, shadowOffset), opacity: 0.25),
            _buildShadowLayer(offset: Offset(shadowOffset * 0.6, shadowOffset * 0.6), opacity: 0.15),
          ],

          // Main icon
          Icon(
            widget.icon,
            color: currentColor,
            size: widget.size,
          ),

          // Highlight layer
          if (widget.enableHighlights)
            _buildHighlightLayer(offset: Offset(highlightOffset, highlightOffset)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildShadowLayer({required Offset offset, required double opacity}) {
    return Transform.translate(
      offset: offset,
      child: Icon(
        widget.icon,
        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: opacity),
        size: widget.size,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHighlightLayer({required Offset offset}) {
    return Transform.translate(
      offset: offset,
      child: Icon(
        widget.icon,
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
        size: widget.size,
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

// Reusable Faded Background Widget
class FadedBackground extends StatelessWidget {
  final Color startColor;
  final Color endColor;
  final AlignmentGeometry begin;
  final AlignmentGeometry end;
  final Widget? child;

  const FadedBackground({
    super.key,
    required this.startColor,
    required this.endColor,
    this.begin = const Alignment(0.50, -0.40),
    this.end = const Alignment(0.50, 0.50),
    this.child,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: begin,
          end: end,
          colors: [startColor, endColor],
        ),
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

class ThirdPage extends StatelessWidget {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: FadedBackground(
        startColor: const Color(0xffffffff),
        endColor: const Color(0xB3DFA8A4),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Text(
                "Third Page",
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 28, color: Colors.black),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 20),
              ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () {},
                child: Text("Button"),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:ui';

class BgCircle extends StatefulWidget {
  final Color color;
  final double blurLevel;
  final double size;
  final double rotationDegree;
  final Duration animationDuration;
  final Offset startPosition;
  final Offset endPosition;

  const BgCircle({
    Key? key,
    required this.color,
    required this.blurLevel,
    required this.size,
    required this.rotationDegree,
    required this.animationDuration,
    required this.startPosition,
    required this.endPosition,
  }) : super(key: key);

  @override
  BgCircleState createState() => BgCircleState();
}

class BgCircleState extends State<BgCircle> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<Offset> _positionAnimation;
  late Animation<double> _rotationAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _controller = AnimationController(
      duration: widget.animationDuration,
      vsync: this,
    )..repeat(reverse: true);

    _positionAnimation = Tween<Offset>(
      begin: widget.startPosition,
      end: widget.endPosition,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    ));

    _rotationAnimation = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0,
      end: widget.rotationDegree,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    ));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _controller,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Transform.translate(
          offset: _positionAnimation.value,
          child: Transform.rotate(
            angle: _rotationAnimation.value * (pi / 180),
            child: Container(
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                color: widget.color,
              ),
              child: BackdropFilter(
                filter: widget.blurLevel > 0
                    ? ImageFilter.blur(
                  sigmaX: widget.blurLevel,
                  sigmaY: widget.blurLevel,
                )
                    : ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 0, sigmaY: 0),
                child: Container(
                  color: Colors.transparent,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}


import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:ui';

class BgIcon extends StatefulWidget {
  final String iconPath;
  final double blurLevel;
  final double size;
  final double rotationDegree;
  final Duration animationDuration;
  final Offset startPosition;
  final Offset endPosition;

  const BgIcon({
    Key? key,
    required this.iconPath,
    required this.blurLevel,
    required this.size,
    required this.rotationDegree,
    required this.animationDuration,
    required this.startPosition,
    required this.endPosition,
  }) : super(key: key);

  @override
  BgIconState createState() => BgIconState();
}

class BgIconState extends State<BgIcon> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<Offset> _positionAnimation;
  late Animation<double> _rotationAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _controller = AnimationController(
      duration: widget.animationDuration,
      vsync: this,
    )..repeat(reverse: true);

    _positionAnimation = Tween<Offset>(
      begin: widget.startPosition,
      end: widget.endPosition,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    ));

    _rotationAnimation = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0,
      end: widget.rotationDegree,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    ));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _controller,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Transform.translate(
          offset: _positionAnimation.value,
          child: Transform.rotate(
            angle: _rotationAnimation.value * (pi / 180),
            child: Container(
              width: widget.size,
              height: widget.size,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                image: DecorationImage(
                  image: widget.iconPath.startsWith('http')
                      ? NetworkImage(widget.iconPath)
                      : AssetImage(widget.iconPath) as ImageProvider,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                ),
              ),
              child: BackdropFilter(
                filter: widget.blurLevel > 0
                    ? ImageFilter.blur(
                  sigmaX: widget.blurLevel,
                  sigmaY: widget.blurLevel,
                )
                    : ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 0, sigmaY: 0),
                child: Container(
                  color: Colors.transparent,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'bg_icon.dart';
import 'bg_circle.dart';
import 'dart:math';

class FloatingBackground extends StatefulWidget {
  final int objectCount;
  final List<BgObjectConfig>? objectConfigs;
  final Widget child;
  final String objectType; // 'icons', 'circles', or 'mixed'
  final List<String>? iconPaths; // List of icon paths for auto-generation
  final List<Color>? colors; // List of colors for auto-generation
  final double minSize; // Minimum size for random generation
  final double maxSize; // Maximum size for random generation

  const FloatingBackground({
    Key? key,
    required this.objectCount,
    this.objectConfigs,
    required this.child,
    required this.objectType,
    this.iconPaths,
    this.colors,
    this.minSize = 30.0,
    this.maxSize = 150.0,
  }) : super(key: key);

  @override
  FloatingBackgroundState createState() => FloatingBackgroundState();
}

class FloatingBackgroundState extends State<FloatingBackground> {
  late List<BgObjectConfig> _objectConfigs;
  final Random _random = Random();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _objectConfigs = widget.objectConfigs ?? _generateDefaultObjects(widget.objectCount);
  }

  List<BgObjectConfig> _generateDefaultObjects(int count) {
    final List<BgObjectConfig> configs = [];

    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      if (widget.objectType == 'icons') {
        final iconPath = _getRandomIconPath(widget.iconPaths);
        final size = _getRandomSize();
        final (startPosition, endPosition) = _getRandomPositions();

        configs.add(BgObjectConfig(
          objectType: 'icons',
          iconPath: iconPath,
          color: Colors.transparent,
          blurLevel: _random.nextDouble() * 5.0,
          size: size,
          rotationDegree: _random.nextDouble() * 360,
          animationDuration: Duration(seconds: 10 + _random.nextInt(20)),
          startPosition: startPosition,
          endPosition: endPosition,
        ));
      } else if (widget.objectType == 'circles') {
        final color = _getRandomColor(widget.colors);
        final size = _getRandomSize();
        final (startPosition, endPosition) = _getRandomPositions();

        configs.add(BgObjectConfig(
          objectType: 'circles',
          iconPath: '',
          color: color,
          blurLevel: _random.nextDouble() * 5.0,
          size: size,
          rotationDegree: _random.nextDouble() * 360,
          animationDuration: Duration(seconds: 10 + _random.nextInt(20)),
          startPosition: startPosition,
          endPosition: endPosition,
        ));
      } else if (widget.objectType == 'mixed') {
        final isIcon = _random.nextBool();
        final size = _getRandomSize();
        final (startPosition, endPosition) = _getRandomPositions();

        if (isIcon) {
          final iconPath = _getRandomIconPath(widget.iconPaths);
          configs.add(BgObjectConfig(
            objectType: 'icons',
            iconPath: iconPath,
            color: Colors.transparent,
            blurLevel: _random.nextDouble() * 5.0,
            size: size,
            rotationDegree: _random.nextDouble() * 360,
            animationDuration: Duration(seconds: 10 + _random.nextInt(20)),
            startPosition: startPosition,
            endPosition: endPosition,
          ));
        } else {
          final color = _getRandomColor(widget.colors);
          configs.add(BgObjectConfig(
            objectType: 'circles',
            iconPath: '',
            color: color,
            blurLevel: _random.nextDouble() * 5.0,
            size: size,
            rotationDegree: _random.nextDouble() * 360,
            animationDuration: Duration(seconds: 10 + _random.nextInt(20)),
            startPosition: startPosition,
            endPosition: endPosition,
          ));
        }
      }
    }
    return configs;
  }

  double _getRandomSize() {
    return widget.minSize + _random.nextDouble() * (widget.maxSize - widget.minSize);
  }

  (Offset, Offset) _getRandomPositions() {
    // Generate random positions within the screen bounds
    // Using values between -0.5 and 0.5 to allow objects to go partially off-screen
    final startX = (_random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2.0; // -1.0 to 1.0
    final startY = (_random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2.0;
    final endX = (_random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2.0;
    final endY = (_random.nextDouble() - 0.5) * 2.0;

    return (
    Offset(startX, startY),
    Offset(endX, endY)
    );
  }

  String _getRandomIconPath(List<String>? customIconPaths) {
    if (customIconPaths != null && customIconPaths.isNotEmpty) {
      return customIconPaths[_random.nextInt(customIconPaths.length)];
    }

    final defaultIcons = [
      "https://placehold.co/100x100/FF6B6B/FFFFFF?text=1",
      "https://placehold.co/100x100/4ECDC4/FFFFFF?text=2",
      "https://placehold.co/100x100/45B7D1/FFFFFF?text=3",
      "https://placehold.co/100x100/96CEB4/FFFFFF?text=4",
      "https://placehold.co/100x100/FFEAA7/FFFFFF?text=5",
    ];
    return defaultIcons[_random.nextInt(defaultIcons.length)];
  }

  Color _getRandomColor(List<Color>? customColors) {
    if (customColors != null && customColors.isNotEmpty) {
      return customColors[_random.nextInt(customColors.length)];
    }

    final defaultColors = [
      Colors.red.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.purple.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.teal.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
      Colors.pink.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
    ];
    return defaultColors[_random.nextInt(defaultColors.length)];
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
    final screenHeight = MediaQuery.of(context).size.height;

    return Stack(
      children: [
        // Background layer with floating objects
        SizedBox(
          width: double.infinity,
          height: double.infinity,
          child: Stack(
            children: _objectConfigs.map((config) {
              // Convert fractional positions to absolute positions
              final startX = screenWidth / 2 + config.startPosition.dx * screenWidth;
              final startY = screenHeight / 2 + config.startPosition.dy * screenHeight;

              return Positioned(
                left: startX - config.size / 2,
                top: startY - config.size / 2,
                child: _buildObject(config),
              );
            }).toList(),
          ),
        ),

        // Your content on top
        widget.child,
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildObject(BgObjectConfig config) {
    if (config.objectType == 'icons') {
      return BgIcon(
        iconPath: config.iconPath,
        blurLevel: config.blurLevel,
        size: config.size,
        rotationDegree: config.rotationDegree,
        animationDuration: config.animationDuration,
        startPosition: config.startPosition,
        endPosition: config.endPosition,
      );
    } else if (config.objectType == 'circles') {
      return BgCircle(
        color: config.color,
        blurLevel: config.blurLevel,
        size: config.size,
        rotationDegree: config.rotationDegree,
        animationDuration: config.animationDuration,
        startPosition: config.startPosition,
        endPosition: config.endPosition,
      );
    } else {
      return BgIcon(
        iconPath: config.iconPath,
        blurLevel: config.blurLevel,
        size: config.size,
        rotationDegree: config.rotationDegree,
        animationDuration: config.animationDuration,
        startPosition: config.startPosition,
        endPosition: config.endPosition,
      );
    }
  }
}

class BgObjectConfig {
  final String objectType; // 'icons' or 'circles'
  final String iconPath; // Used for icons
  final Color color; // Used for circles
  final double blurLevel;
  final double size;
  final double rotationDegree;
  final Duration animationDuration;
  final Offset startPosition;
  final Offset endPosition;

  BgObjectConfig({
    required this.objectType,
    required this.iconPath,
    required this.color,
    required this.blurLevel,
    required this.size,
    required this.rotationDegree,
    required this.animationDuration,
    required this.startPosition,
    required this.endPosition,
  });
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppRulesOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppRulesOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
      // shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
    return Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 500),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    _buildRuleCategory(
                      'User Conduct',
                      Icons.person,
                      [
                        'Be respectful to other users',
                        'No harassment or hate speech',
                        'No spamming or flooding',
                        'No impersonation of others',
                        'Respect privacy of other users',
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    _buildRuleCategory(
                      'Content Guidelines',
                      Icons.content_copy,
                      [
                        'No illegal content',
                        'No explicit or adult content',
                        'No copyrighted material without permission',
                        'No false or misleading information',
                        'No commercial spam',
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    _buildRuleCategory(
                      'Account Usage',
                      Icons.account_circle,
                      [
                        'One account per person',
                        'You are responsible for your account',
                        'No sharing of account credentials',
                        'Report lost or stolen accounts immediately',
                        'Accounts must not be bought or sold',
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    _buildRuleCategory(
                      'Prohibited Activities',
                      Icons.warning,
                      [
                        'No hacking or exploiting vulnerabilities',
                        'No automated bots or scripts',
                        'No reverse engineering',
                        'No data scraping',
                        'No unauthorized commercial use',
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    _buildConsequencesSection(),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Color(0x80ffffff),
                  side: BorderSide(color: Color(0x80c25858)),
                  shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                ),
                child: const Text(
                  'I Agree',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Color(0xffc25858)),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRuleCategory(String title, IconData icon, List<String> rules) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: Color(0x80c25858), size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(
              title,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                fontSize: 16,
                color: Color(0xffc25858),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ...rules.map((rule) => _buildRuleItem(rule)).toList(),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRuleItem(String rule) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.circle, size: 8, color: Colors.grey),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              rule,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConsequencesSection() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Violation Consequences:',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.red,
              fontSize: 14,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _buildConsequenceItem('Warning and content removal'),
          _buildConsequenceItem('Temporary suspension'),
          _buildConsequenceItem('Permanent account ban'),
          _buildConsequenceItem('Legal action for severe violations'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConsequenceItem(String consequence) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.red, size: 14),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              consequence,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.red),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ContactUsOverlay extends StatefulWidget {
  const ContactUsOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  State<ContactUsOverlay> createState() => _ContactUsOverlayState();
}

class _ContactUsOverlayState extends State<ContactUsOverlay> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _subjectController = TextEditingController();
  final _messageController = TextEditingController();

  String _selectedCategory = 'General Inquiry';
  final List<String> _categories = [
    'General Inquiry',
    'Technical Support',
    'Billing Issue',
    'Feature Request',
    'Bug Report',
    'Partnership',
    'Other',
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _subjectController.dispose();
    _messageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submitForm() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      // Implement form submission
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Message sent successfully!')),
      );
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 700),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildContactInfo(),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      _buildFormFields(),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _submitForm,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Color(0x80806C99),
                  shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                  side: BorderSide(color: Color(0xff806C99)),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                ),
                child: const Text(
                  'Send Message',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildContactInfo() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: const Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.email, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('support@yourapp.com', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.phone, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('+1 (555) 123-4567', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.access_time, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('Mon-Fri 9:00 AM - 6:00 PM', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFormFields() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
          initialValue: _selectedCategory,
          items: _categories.map((String category) {
            return DropdownMenuItem(
              value: category,
              child: Text(category),
            );
          }).toList(),
          onChanged: (String? newValue) {
            setState(() {
              _selectedCategory = newValue!;
            });
          },
          dropdownColor: Color(0xffffffff),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(20.0)),
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            labelText: 'Category',
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _nameController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Your Name',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your name';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _emailController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Email Address',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your email';
            }
            if (!value.contains('@')) {
              return 'Please enter a valid email';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _subjectController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Subject',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.subject, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter a subject';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _messageController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Message',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            alignLabelWithHint: true,
          ),
          maxLines: 5,
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your message';
            }
            if (value.length < 10) {
              return 'Message must be at least 10 characters';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class FeedbackOverlay extends StatefulWidget {
  const FeedbackOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  State<FeedbackOverlay> createState() => _FeedbackOverlayState();
}

class _FeedbackOverlayState extends State<FeedbackOverlay> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _subjectController = TextEditingController();
  final _messageController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _subjectController.dispose();
    _messageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submitForm() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      // Implement form submission
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Message sent successfully!')),
      );
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 700),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildContactInfo(),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      _buildFormFields(),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _submitForm,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Color(0x80806C99),
                  shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                  side: BorderSide(color: Color(0xff806C99)),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                ),
                child: const Text(
                  'Send Message',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildContactInfo() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: const Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.email, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('support@yourapp.com', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.phone, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('+1 (555) 123-4567', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.access_time, size: 16, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('Mon-Fri 9:00 AM - 6:00 PM', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFormFields() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        TextFormField(
          controller: _nameController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Your Name',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your name';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _emailController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Email Address',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your email';
            }
            if (!value.contains('@')) {
              return 'Please enter a valid email';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _subjectController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Subject',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.subject, color: Color(0xFF806C99)),
          ),
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter a subject';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        TextFormField(
          controller: _messageController,
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            labelText: 'Message',
            border: OutlineInputBorder(),
            alignLabelWithHint: true,
          ),
          maxLines: 5,
          validator: (value) {
            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
              return 'Please enter your message';
            }
            if (value.length < 10) {
              return 'Message must be at least 10 characters';
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class InviteFriendsOverlay extends StatefulWidget {
  const InviteFriendsOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  State<InviteFriendsOverlay> createState() => _InviteFriendsOverlayState();
}

class _InviteFriendsOverlayState extends State<InviteFriendsOverlay> {
  final String inviteLink = 'https://yourapp.com/invite/ABC123XYZ';
  bool _copied = false;

  void _copyToClipboard() {
    // Implement clipboard functionality
    setState(() {
      _copied = true;
    });
    Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _copied = false;
        });
      }
    });
  }

  void _shareLink() {
    // Implement share functionality
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        // const Divider(),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        const Text(
          'Share with your friends and earn rewards!',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.grey),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.grey.shade50,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80c25858)),
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              const Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.card_giftcard, color: Color(0x80c25858)),
                  SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    'Invitation Rewards',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 16,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              _buildRewardItem('You get:', '50 points per friend'),
              _buildRewardItem('Friend gets:', '100 bonus points'),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Color(0x4dffffff),
            border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  inviteLink,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              IconButton(
                icon: Icon(
                  _copied ? Icons.check : Icons.copy,
                  color: _copied ? Colors.green : Colors.grey,
                ),
                onPressed: _copyToClipboard,
                iconSize: 20,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        if (_copied)
          const Text(
            'Copied to clipboard!',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.green, fontSize: 12),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                onPressed: _shareLink,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.share, size: 18, color: Colors.black),
                label: const Text('Share'),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Text(
          'Your referral code: ABC123XYZ',
          style: TextStyle(
            color: Colors.grey.shade600,
            fontSize: 12,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRewardItem(String title, String value) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey),
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          Text(
            value,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class LogoutConfirmationOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onConfirm;

  const LogoutConfirmationOverlay({super.key, required this.onConfirm});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Are you sure you want to logout?',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 16,
                color: Color(0x80000000),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: OutlinedButton(
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                      side: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                    ),
                    child: const Text(
                      'Cancel',
                      style: TextStyle(color: Color(0x80000000)),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                Expanded(
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: () {
                      Navigator.of(context).pop();
                      onConfirm();
                    },
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Color(0xffab1f1f),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                    ),
                    child: const Text(
                      'Logout',
                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }
}

                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],

      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,

          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text(
          content,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, height: 1.5),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ReportProblemOverlay extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReportProblemOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  State<ReportProblemOverlay> createState() => _ReportProblemOverlayState();
}

class _ReportProblemOverlayState extends State<ReportProblemOverlay> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _shopNameController = TextEditingController();
  final _shopUrlController = TextEditingController();
  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();

  String _selectedIssueType = 'Fake Products';
  final List<String> _issueTypes = [
    'Fake Products',
    'Poor Quality',
    'Wrong Description',
    'Late Delivery',
    'No Delivery',
    'Rude Behavior',
    'Overpricing',
    'Safety Concern',
    'Other',
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _shopNameController.dispose();
    _shopUrlController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submitReport() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      // Implement report submission
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Report submitted successfully!')),
      );
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      // decoration: (RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 650),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Help us maintain a safe marketplace by reporting shops that violate our policies.',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 14),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                        initialValue: _selectedIssueType,
                        items: _issueTypes.map((String type) {
                          return DropdownMenuItem(
                            value: type,
                            child: Text(type),
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                        onChanged: (String? newValue) {
                          setState(() {
                            _selectedIssueType = newValue!;
                          });
                        },
                        dropdownColor: Color(0xffffffff),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(20.0)),
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'Issue Type',
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                          prefixIcon: Icon(
                              Icons.warning,
                              color: Color(0xb3c25858)
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      // const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      // TextFormField(
                      //   controller: _shopNameController,
                      //   decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      //     labelText: 'Problem Name',
                      //     border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                      //     prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.store, color: Color(0xb3c25858)),
                      //   ),
                      //   validator: (value) {
                      //     if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                      //       return 'Please enter shop name';
                      //     }
                      //     return null;
                      //   },
                      // ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _descriptionController,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'Issue Description',
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                          alignLabelWithHint: true,
                        ),
                        maxLines: 5,
                        validator: (value) {
                          if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                            return 'Please describe the issue';
                          }
                          if (value.length < 20) {
                            return 'Please provide more details (at least 20 characters)';
                          }
                          return null;
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      _buildEvidenceSection(),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _submitReport,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Color(0x80c25858),
                  shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                ),
                child: const Text(
                  'Submit Report',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEvidenceSection() {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Evidence (Optional but helpful)',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          const Text(
            'Screenshots, order numbers, or any other evidence that supports your report.',
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Color(0x80000000)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () {}, // Implement image picker
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.photo, color: Color(0xb3c25858), size: 18),
                  label: const Text('Add Screenshot'),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () {}, // Implement file picker
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.attach_file, color: Color(0xb3c25858), size: 18),
                  label: const Text('Add File'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SecurityPolicyOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  const SecurityPolicyOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 500),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    _buildSecurityFeature(
                      'Data Encryption',
                      Icons.lock,
                      'All data is encrypted in transit and at rest using industry-standard AES-256 encryption.',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildSecurityFeature(
                      'Two-Factor Authentication',
                      Icons.enhanced_encryption,
                      'Enable 2FA for an extra layer of security on your account.',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildSecurityFeature(
                      'Regular Security Audits',
                      Icons.security_update_good,
                      'We conduct regular security audits and penetration testing.',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    _buildSecurityFeature(
                      'Secure Payment Processing',
                      Icons.payment,
                      'All payments are processed through PCI-DSS compliant providers.',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    const Text(
                      'Security Tips:',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 16,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Color(0xffc25858),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    _buildSecurityTip('Use a strong, unique password'),
                    _buildSecurityTip('Enable two-factor authentication'),
                    _buildSecurityTip('Keep your app updated'),
                    _buildSecurityTip('Log out from shared devices'),
                    _buildSecurityTip('Be cautious of phishing attempts'),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Color(0xFF806C99),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                ),
                child: const Text(
                  'Got It',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.white),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSecurityFeature(String title, IconData icon, String description) {
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: Color(0xFF806C99), size: 24),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  fontSize: 16,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                description,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Color(0x80000000)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSecurityTip(String tip) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Color(0xFF806C99), size: 16),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(child: Text(tip, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12))),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../models/report_model.dart';

class UserReportsOverlay extends StatelessWidget {
  const UserReportsOverlay({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final List<ReportModel> reports = [
      ReportModel(
        id: '1',
        reporterName: 'User A',
        description: 'Spam behavior in comments section',
        date: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 2)),
        type: ReportType.user,
      ),
      ReportModel(
        id: '2',
        reporterName: 'Shop B',
        description: 'Inappropriate content in product review',
        date: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 5)),
        type: ReportType.content,
      ),
      ReportModel(
        id: '3',
        reporterName: 'User C',
        description: 'Suspicious activity detected',
        date: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
        type: ReportType.user,
      ),
    ];

    return Container(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 500),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            _buildStatsSection(reports),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            Expanded(
              child: _buildReportsList(context, reports),
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatsSection(List<ReportModel> reports) {
    final resolvedCount = reports.where((r) => r.date.isAfter(DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))).length;
    final pendingCount = reports.length - resolvedCount;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x80F3E0FF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
        children: [
          _buildStatItem('Total', reports.length.toString(), Colors.blue),
          _buildStatItem('Resolved', resolvedCount.toString(), Colors.green),
          _buildStatItem('Pending', pendingCount.toString(), Colors.orange),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatItem(String label, String value, Color color) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(
          value,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 20,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: color,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: Color(0xffffffff)),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportsList(BuildContext context, List<ReportModel> reports) { // Accept context as parameter
    return ListView.builder(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      itemCount: reports.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final report = reports[index];
        return _buildReportItem(context, report); // Pass context to _buildReportItem
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportItem(BuildContext context, ReportModel report) { // Accept context as parameter
    return Card(
      color: Color(0x4dffffff),
      shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: _getReportIcon(report.type),
        title: Text(
          report.description,
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
          maxLines: 2,
          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
        ),
        subtitle: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              'From: ${report.reporterName}',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
            ),
            Text(
              '${_formatDate(report.date)} • ${_getTypeText(report.type)}',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.grey),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        trailing: _getStatusWidget(report.date),
        onTap: () => _showReportDetails(context, report), // Pass context to _showReportDetails
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _getReportIcon(ReportType type) {
    switch (type) {
      case ReportType.user:
        return const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.blue);
      case ReportType.shop:
        return const Icon(Icons.store, color: Colors.green);
      case ReportType.content:
        return const Icon(Icons.content_copy, color: Colors.orange);
    }
  }

  Widget _getStatusWidget(DateTime date) {
    final isRecent = date.isAfter(DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 1)));
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isRecent ? Colors.green.shade100 : Colors.orange.shade100,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Text(
        isRecent ? 'New' : 'Pending',
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 10,
          color: isRecent ? Colors.green : Colors.orange,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.day}/${date.month}/${date.year}';
  }

  String _getTypeText(ReportType type) {
    switch (type) {
      case ReportType.user:
        return 'User Report';
      case ReportType.shop:
        return 'Shop Report';
      case ReportType.content:
        return 'Content Report';
    }
  }

  void _showReportDetails(BuildContext context, ReportModel report) { // Accept context as parameter
    showDialog(
      context: context, // Now context is available
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('Report Details - ${_getTypeText(report.type)}'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Text('Reporter: ${report.reporterName}'),
              Text('Date: ${_formatDate(report.date)}'),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text('Description:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              Text(report.description),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              const Text('Status:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              Text(report.date.isAfter(DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))
                  ? 'New - Under Review'
                  : 'Pending - Investigation in progress'),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Close'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              // Implement mark as resolved
              Navigator.of(context).pop();
            },
            child: const Text('Mark Resolved'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:na3eeman/features/sidebar/widgets/animated_sidebar_item.dart';
import '../../Models/menu_item_model.dart';
import 'sidebar_controller.dart';
import 'widgets/sidebar_background.dart';
import 'widgets/user_profile_section.dart';

class Sidebar extends StatelessWidget {
  final SidebarController controller;
  final double maxWidth;
  final double minWidth;
  final double widthPercentage;

  const Sidebar({
    super.key,
    required this.controller,
    this.maxWidth = 320,
    this.minWidth = 250,
    this.widthPercentage = 0.45,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Calculate width inside build method where context is available
    final double calculatedWidth = _calculateWidth(context);

    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: controller.animation,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Visibility(
          visible: controller.animation.value > 0,
          child: GestureDetector(
            onTap: controller.closeSidebar,
            child: Container(
              color: Colors.transparent,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  SizedBox(
                    width: calculatedWidth,
                    child: GestureDetector(
                      onTap: () {},
                      child: Container(
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.transparent,
                        ),
                        child: _buildSidebarContent(context),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Expanded(
                    child: GestureDetector(
                      onTap: controller.closeSidebar,
                      child: Container(
                        color: Colors.transparent,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  double _calculateWidth(BuildContext context) {
    final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
    final calculatedWidth = screenWidth * widthPercentage;
    return calculatedWidth.clamp(minWidth, maxWidth);
  }

  Widget _buildSidebarContent(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        // Bubble Background
        const SidebarBackground(),

        // Sidebar Content
        Column(
          children: [
            SizedBox(height: 50),
            // User Profile Section
            UserProfileSection(controller: controller),

            // Divider
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16.0),
              child: Divider(color: Color(0x80c25858)),
            ),

            // Menu Items
            Expanded(
              child: _buildMenuItems(context),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMenuItems(BuildContext context) {
    final menuItems = [
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Settings',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/setting.png', // Use asset image
        delay: 0,
        onTap: controller.navigateToSettings,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Favorites',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/heart.png',
        delay: 4,
        onTap: controller.navigateToFavorites,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Invite Friends',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/plus.png',
        delay: 5,
        onTap: controller.showInviteFriends,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Privacy',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/lock.png',
        delay: 1,
        onTap: controller.showPrivacyPolicy,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Security',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/secure.png',
        delay: 2,
        onTap: controller.showSecurityPolicy,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Rules',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/star.png',
        delay: 3,
        onTap: controller.showAppRules,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Contact Us',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/mail_view.png',
        delay: 6,
        onTap: controller.showContactUs,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Report Shop',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/report.png',
        delay: 7,
        onTap: controller.showReportShop,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'User Reports',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/file_star.png',
        delay: 8,
        onTap: controller.showUserReports,
      ),
      MenuItem(
        title: 'Logout',
        assetIcon: 'assets/icons/exit.png',
        delay: 9,
        onTap: controller.showLogoutConfirmation,
      ),
    ];

    return ListView.builder(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      itemCount: menuItems.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final item = menuItems[index];
        return AnimatedSidebarItem(
          item: item,
          animation: controller.animation,
          delay: item.delay,
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Services/overlay_service.dart';
import '../../Services/sidebar_navigation_service.dart';
// import '../../services/navigation_service.dart';
// import '../../services/overlay_service.dart';
import '../../Services/auth_service.dart';

class SidebarController with ChangeNotifier {
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> animation;

  bool get isOpen => _animationController.isCompleted;

  void init(TickerProvider vsync) {
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
      vsync: vsync,
    );

    animation = CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    );
  }

  void toggleSidebar() {
    if (isOpen) {
      closeSidebar();
    } else {
      openSidebar();
    }
  }

  void openSidebar() {
    _animationController.forward();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void closeSidebar() {
    _animationController.reverse();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // @override
  // void dispose() {
  //   _animationController.dispose();
  // }

  // Navigation Methods
  void navigateToSettings() {
    closeSidebar();
    SideNavigationService.navigateToSettings();
  }

  void navigateToFavorites() {
    closeSidebar();
    SideNavigationService.navigateToFavorites();
  }

  // Overlay Methods
  void showPrivacyPolicy() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showPrivacyPolicy();
  }

  void showSecurityPolicy() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showSecurityPolicy();
  }

  void showAppRules() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showAppRules();
  }

  void showInviteFriends() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showInviteFriends();
  }

  void showContactUs() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showContactUs();
  }

  void showReportShop() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showReportShop();
  }

  void showUserReports() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showUserReports();
  }

  void showLogoutConfirmation() {
    closeSidebar();
    OverlayService.showLogoutConfirmation(
      onConfirm: () {
        AuthService.logout();
        SideNavigationService.navigateToLogin();
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:na3eeman/features/sidebar/widgets/sidebar_item.dart';
import '../../../Helpers/animations.dart';
import '../../../Models/menu_item_model.dart';

class AnimatedSidebarItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final MenuItem item;
  final Animation<double> animation;
  final int delay;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final Color textColor;
  final double iconSize;

  const AnimatedSidebarItem({
    super.key,
    required this.item,
    required this.animation,
    required this.delay,
    this.backgroundColor = Colors.transparent,
    this.textColor = Colors.black,
    this.iconSize = 30.0,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: animation,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Transform.translate(
          offset: Offset(
            -100 + (animation.value * 100),
            0,
          ),
          child: Opacity(
            opacity: animation.value,
            child: child,
          ),
        );
      },
      child: DelayedWidget(
        delay: Duration(milliseconds: 200 + (delay * 50)),
        child: SidebarItem(
          title: item.title,
          icon: item.icon,
          assetIcon: item.assetIcon,
          onTap: item.onTap,
          backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
          textColor: textColor,
          iconSize: iconSize,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class SidebarBackground extends StatelessWidget {
  const SidebarBackground({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        // Main background
        Container(
          color: Colors.transparent,
        ),

        // Bubbles
        ..._generateBubbles(),
      ],
    );
  }

  List<Widget> _generateBubbles() {
    final bubbles = <Widget>[];
    final positions = [
      const Alignment(-0.8, -0.9),
      const Alignment(-0.2, -0.7),
      const Alignment(0.9, -0.4),
      const Alignment(0.7, 0.1),
      const Alignment(-0.3, 0.3),
      const Alignment(0.4, 0.8),
      const Alignment(-0.7, 0.9),
    ];

    final colors = [
      Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.2),
      Colors.purple.withOpacity(0.2),
      Colors.orange.withOpacity(0.2),
      Colors.green.withOpacity(0.2),
    ];

    for (int i = 0; i < positions.length; i++) {
      bubbles.add(
        Align(
          alignment: positions[i],
          child: Container(
            width: 80 + (i * 10),
            height: 80 + (i * 10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              color: colors[i % colors.length],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return bubbles;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class SidebarItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final IconData? icon;
  final String? assetIcon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final Color textColor;
  final double iconSize;

  const SidebarItem({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.icon,
    this.assetIcon,
    required this.onTap,
    this.backgroundColor = Colors.transparent,
    this.textColor = Colors.black,
    this.iconSize = 24.0,
  }) : assert(icon != null || assetIcon != null, 'Either icon or assetIcon must be provided');

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      // margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      elevation: 2,
      shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
      color: backgroundColor,
      child: ListTile(
        leading: _buildLeadingIcon(),
        title: Text(
          title,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            color: textColor,
            fontSize: 16, // You can adjust text size
          ),
        ),
        // trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: trailingColor, size: 20),
        onTap: onTap,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 4), // Control padding
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLeadingIcon() {
    if (assetIcon != null) {
      return Image.asset(
        assetIcon!,
        width: iconSize,
        height: iconSize,
        errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
          // Fallback to icon if image fails to load
          return Icon(
            icon ?? Icons.error_outline,
            size: iconSize,
          );
        },
      );
    } else {
      return Icon(
        icon,
        size: iconSize,
      );
    }
  }
}
