class UserProfileSection extends StatelessWidget {

            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class DelayedWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final Duration delay;

  const DelayedWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    required this.delay,
  });

  @override
  State<DelayedWidget> createState() => _DelayedWidgetState();
}

class _DelayedWidgetState extends State<DelayedWidget>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<double> _animation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      vsync: this,
    );

    _animation = CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.easeOut,
    );

    Future.delayed(widget.delay, () {
      if (mounted) {
        _controller.forward();
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _animation,
      builder: (context, child) {
        return Opacity(
          opacity: _animation.value,
          child: Transform.translate(
            offset: Offset(0, (1 - _animation.value) * 20),
            child: child,
          ),
        );
      },
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }
}

  static const double sidebarWidth = 280;
  static const double sidebarAnimationDuration = 400;
  static const double homePageScale = 0.9;
  static const double homePageTranslate = 200;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../Services/theme_service.dart';
import '../Services/language_service.dart';

class DialogHelper {
  static void showLanguageBottomSheet(
      BuildContext context, LanguageService languageService) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) {
        return Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const Text(
                'Select Language',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Text('🇺🇸'),
                title: const Text('English'),
                trailing: languageService.languageNotifier.value == 'English'
                    ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green)
                    : null,
                onTap: () {
                  languageService.changeLanguage(const Locale('en', 'US'));
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Text('🇸🇦'),
                title: const Text('العربية'),
                trailing: languageService.languageNotifier.value == 'العربية'
                    ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green)
                    : null,
                onTap: () {
                  languageService.changeLanguage(const Locale('ar', 'AR'));
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  static void showThemeBottomSheet(
      BuildContext context, ThemeService themeService) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) {
        return Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const Text(
                'Select Theme',
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.light_mode),
                title: const Text('Light Mode'),
                trailing: themeService.themeModeNotifier.value == 'Light'
                    ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green)
                    : null,
                onTap: () {
                  themeService.changeTheme(ThemeMode.light);
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.dark_mode),
                title: const Text('Dark Mode'),
                trailing: themeService.themeModeNotifier.value == 'Dark'
                    ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green)
                    : null,
                onTap: () {
                  themeService.changeTheme(ThemeMode.dark);
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.phone_iphone),
                title: const Text('System Default'),
                trailing: themeService.themeModeNotifier.value == 'System'
                    ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green)
                    : null,
                onTap: () {
                  themeService.changeTheme(ThemeMode.system);
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}
import '../Models/inventory_item.dart';

class InventoryHelpers {
  static String getStockStatusMessage(int count) {
    if (count == 0) {
      return 'Out of Stock';
    } else if (count < 3) {
      return 'Low Stock';
    } else if (count < 10) {
      return 'In Stock';
    } else {
      return 'Well Stocked';
    }
  }

  static String getStockEmoji(int count) {
    if (count == 0) {
      return '🔴';
    } else if (count < 3) {
      return '🟡';
    } else {
      return '🟢';
    }
  }

  static bool shouldShowLowStockAlert(List<InventoryItem> items) {
    return items.any((item) => item.isLowStock);
  }

  static String formatLowStockAlert(List<InventoryItem> lowStockItems) {
    if (lowStockItems.isEmpty) return '';

    final itemNames = lowStockItems.map((item) => item.name).join(', ');
    return 'Low stock alert: $itemNames';
  }
}

class ShopAppointment {
  final String id;
  final String imagePath;
  final String title;
  final String status;
  final double totalPrice;
  final String serviceTime;
  final String date;
  final String barberName;
  final String barberNumber;
  final String barberShopName;

  ShopAppointment({
    required this.id,
    required this.imagePath,
    required this.title,
    required this.status,
    required this.totalPrice,
    required this.serviceTime,
    required this.date,
    required this.barberName,
    required this.barberNumber,
    required this.barberShopName,
  });

  factory ShopAppointment.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ShopAppointment(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      imagePath: json['imagePath']?.toString() ?? '',
      title: json['title']?.toString() ?? '',
      status: json['status']?.toString() ?? '',
      totalPrice: (json['totalPrice'] is int ? (json['totalPrice'] as int).toDouble() : json['totalPrice'] ?? 0.0),
      serviceTime: json['serviceTime']?.toString() ?? '',
      date: json['date']?.toString() ?? '',
      barberName: json['barberName']?.toString() ?? '',
      barberNumber: json['barberNumber']?.toString() ?? '',
      barberShopName: json['barberShopName']?.toString() ?? '',
    );
  }
}

import '../Models/staff_models.dart';

class Barber extends StaffMember {
  final double rating;
  final int totalAppointments;
  final double totalEarnings;
  final List<String> specialties;
  final Map<String, int> servicesCount;
  final DateTime? lastActive;

  Barber({
    required String id,
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String phone,
    required DateTime joinDate,
    String? profileImage,
    String? branchId,
    bool isActive = true,
    required this.rating,
    required this.totalAppointments,
    required this.totalEarnings,
    required this.specialties,
    required this.servicesCount,
    this.lastActive,
    double? hourlyRate,
  }) : super(
          id: id,
          name: name,
          email: email,
          phone: phone,
          role: StaffRole.barber,
          joinDate: joinDate,
          profileImage: profileImage,
          branchId: branchId,
          isActive: isActive,
          hourlyRate: hourlyRate,
          specialties: specialties,
          performanceMetrics: {
            'rating': rating,
            'totalAppointments': totalAppointments,
            'totalEarnings': totalEarnings,
            'servicesCount': servicesCount,
          },
        );

  factory Barber.fromStaffMember(StaffMember staff, {
    required double rating,
    required int totalAppointments,
    required double totalEarnings,
    required List<String> specialties,
    required Map<String, int> servicesCount,
    DateTime? lastActive,
  }) {
    return Barber(
      id: staff.id,
      name: staff.name,
      email: staff.email,
      phone: staff.phone,
      joinDate: staff.joinDate,
      profileImage: staff.profileImage,
      branchId: staff.branchId,
      isActive: staff.isActive,
      rating: rating,
      totalAppointments: totalAppointments,
      totalEarnings: totalEarnings,
      specialties: specialties,
      servicesCount: servicesCount,
      lastActive: lastActive,
      hourlyRate: staff.hourlyRate,
    );
  }

  double get averageServiceTime {
    final totalServices = servicesCount.values.reduce((a, b) => a + b);
    return totalServices > 0 ? totalAppointments / totalServices : 0;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    final json = super.toJson();
    json.addAll({
      'rating': rating,
      'totalAppointments': totalAppointments,
      'totalEarnings': totalEarnings,
      'specialties': specialties,
      'servicesCount': servicesCount,
      'lastActive': lastActive?.toIso8601String(),
    });
    return json;
  }
}
class Branch {
  final int id;
  final String name;
  final String address;
  final String phone;
  final bool isOpen;
  final int todayAppointments;
  final double todayRevenue;

  // New fields for shop owner dashboard
  final String? manager;
  final double? monthlyRevenue;
  final int? totalSales;
  final int? lowStockItems;
  final String? email;
  final DateTime? openingDate;
  final int? totalEmployees;

  Branch({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.address,
    required this.phone,
    required this.isOpen,
    required this.todayAppointments,
    required this.todayRevenue,
    this.manager,
    this.monthlyRevenue,
    this.totalSales,
    this.lowStockItems,
    this.email,
    this.openingDate,
    this.totalEmployees,
  });

  factory Branch.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Branch(
      id: json['id'] ?? 0,
      name: json['name'] ?? '',
      address: json['address'] ?? '',
      phone: json['phone'] ?? '',
      isOpen: json['is_open'] ?? false,
      todayAppointments: json['today_appointments'] ?? 0,
      todayRevenue: (json['today_revenue'] ?? 0).toDouble(),
      // New fields with null safety
      manager: json['manager']?.toString(),
      monthlyRevenue: json['monthly_revenue'] != null
          ? (json['monthly_revenue'] is double
              ? json['monthly_revenue']
              : double.tryParse(json['monthly_revenue'].toString()) ?? 0.0)
          : null,
      totalSales: json['total_sales'] is int ? json['total_sales'] : int.tryParse(json['total_sales']?.toString() ?? '0'),
      lowStockItems: json['low_stock_items'] is int ? json['low_stock_items'] : int.tryParse(json['low_stock_items']?.toString() ?? '0'),
      email: json['email']?.toString(),
      openingDate: json['opening_date'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(json['opening_date'].toString())
          : null,
      totalEmployees: json['total_employees'] is int ? json['total_employees'] : int.tryParse(json['total_employees']?.toString() ?? '0'),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'address': address,
      'phone': phone,
      'is_open': isOpen,
      'today_appointments': todayAppointments,
      'today_revenue': todayRevenue,
      // New fields
      'manager': manager,
      'monthly_revenue': monthlyRevenue,
      'total_sales': totalSales,
      'low_stock_items': lowStockItems,
      'email': email,
      'opening_date': openingDate?.toIso8601String(),
      'total_employees': totalEmployees,
    };
  }

  Branch copyWith({
    int? id,
    String? name,
    String? address,
    String? phone,
    bool? isOpen,
    int? todayAppointments,
    double? todayRevenue,
    String? manager,
    double? monthlyRevenue,
    int? totalSales,
    int? lowStockItems,
    String? email,
    DateTime? openingDate,
    int? totalEmployees,
  }) {
    return Branch(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      address: address ?? this.address,
      phone: phone ?? this.phone,
      isOpen: isOpen ?? this.isOpen,
      todayAppointments: todayAppointments ?? this.todayAppointments,
      todayRevenue: todayRevenue ?? this.todayRevenue,
      manager: manager ?? this.manager,
      monthlyRevenue: monthlyRevenue ?? this.monthlyRevenue,
      totalSales: totalSales ?? this.totalSales,
      lowStockItems: lowStockItems ?? this.lowStockItems,
      email: email ?? this.email,
      openingDate: openingDate ?? this.openingDate,
      totalEmployees: totalEmployees ?? this.totalEmployees,
    );
  }

  // Helper methods for the shop owner dashboard
  String get status => isOpen ? 'Open' : 'Closed';

  String get performanceLevel {
    if (monthlyRevenue == null) return 'Unknown';
    if (monthlyRevenue! > 50000) return 'Excellent';
    if (monthlyRevenue! > 25000) return 'Good';
    if (monthlyRevenue! > 10000) return 'Average';
    return 'Needs Attention';
  }

  bool get isActive {
    // If you don't have an isActive field, you can determine based on other logic
    // For now, let's assume all branches are active
    return true;
  }

  double get safeMonthlyRevenue {
    return monthlyRevenue ?? todayRevenue * 30; // Estimate if null
  }

  String get formattedMonthlyRevenue {
    return safeMonthlyRevenue.toStringAsFixed(2);
  }

  String get formattedTodayRevenue {
    return todayRevenue.toStringAsFixed(2);
  }

  bool get hasLowStockAlerts => (lowStockItems ?? 0) > 0;

  double get getMonthlyRevenue => monthlyRevenue ?? todayRevenue * 30; // Estimate if not available

  int get getTotalSales => totalSales ?? todayAppointments * 30; // Estimate if not available

  int get totalStaff {
    // You can calculate this based on your data or return a field
    return totalEmployees ?? 5; // Fallback to 5 if null
  }
}
class CartItem {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final double price;
  final String imageUrl;
  int quantity;

  CartItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.price,
    required this.imageUrl,
    this.quantity = 1,
  });

  double get totalPrice => price * quantity;
}

class InventoryItem {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final int count;
  final String? description;
  final DateTime? lastUpdated;
  final String? category; // Make optional
  final int? minStockLevel; // Make optional
  final double? price; // Make optional
  final String? imageUrl; // Make optional

  InventoryItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.count,
    this.description,
    this.lastUpdated,
    this.category, // Optional
    this.minStockLevel, // Optional
    this.price, // Optional
    this.imageUrl, // Optional
  });

  factory InventoryItem.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return InventoryItem(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      name: json['name']?.toString() ?? '',
      count: json['count'] is int ? json['count'] : int.tryParse(json['count']?.toString() ?? '0') ?? 0,
      description: json['description']?.toString(),
      lastUpdated: json['lastUpdated'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(json['lastUpdated']?.toString() ?? '')
          : null,
      category: json['category']?.toString(), // Optional
      minStockLevel: json['minStockLevel'] is int ? json['minStockLevel'] : int.tryParse(json['minStockLevel']?.toString() ?? '5'), // Optional
      price: json['price'] != null
          ? (json['price'] is double ? json['price'] : double.tryParse(json['price']?.toString() ?? '0.0'))
          : null, // Optional
      imageUrl: json['imageUrl']?.toString(), // Optional
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'count': count,
      'description': description,
      'lastUpdated': lastUpdated?.toIso8601String(),
      'category': category,
      'minStockLevel': minStockLevel,
      'price': price,
      'imageUrl': imageUrl,
    };
  }

  InventoryItem copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    int? count,
    String? description,
    DateTime? lastUpdated,
    String? category,
    int? minStockLevel,
    double? price,
    String? imageUrl,
  }) {
    return InventoryItem(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      count: count ?? this.count,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      lastUpdated: lastUpdated ?? this.lastUpdated,
      category: category ?? this.category,
      minStockLevel: minStockLevel ?? this.minStockLevel,
      price: price ?? this.price,
      imageUrl: imageUrl ?? this.imageUrl,
    );
  }

  bool get isLowStock => count < 3;
  bool get isCriticalStock => count <= ((minStockLevel ?? 5) * 0.3).ceil();
  int get currentStock => count;
}

class MenuItem {

class Offer {
  final String id;
  final String title;
  final String description;
  final double discountPercentage;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final bool isActive;
  final List<String> applicableProducts;

  Offer({
    required this.id,
    required this.title,
    required this.description,
    required this.discountPercentage,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    required this.isActive,
    required this.applicableProducts,
  });

  factory Offer.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Offer(
      id: json['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      title: json['title'] ?? '',
      description: json['description'] ?? '',
      discountPercentage: (json['discountPercentage'] is double ? json['discountPercentage'] : double.tryParse(json['discountPercentage']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      startDate: DateTime.parse(json['startDate']),
      endDate: DateTime.parse(json['endDate']),
      isActive: json['isActive'] ?? true,
      applicableProducts: List<String>.from(json['applicableProducts'] ?? []),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'title': title,
      'description': description,
      'discountPercentage': discountPercentage,
      'startDate': startDate.toIso8601String(),
      'endDate': endDate.toIso8601String(),
      'isActive': isActive,
      'applicableProducts': applicableProducts,
    };
  }

  int get daysRemaining => endDate.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
  bool get isExpired => DateTime.now().isAfter(endDate);
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

enum PaymentMethod { cash, cliq, visa }

extension PaymentMethodExtension on PaymentMethod {
  String get displayName {
    switch (this) {
      case PaymentMethod.cash:
        return 'Cash';
      case PaymentMethod.cliq:
        return 'CliQ';
      case PaymentMethod.visa:
        return 'Visa';
    }
  }
  
  IconData get icon {
    switch (this) {
      case PaymentMethod.cash:
        return Icons.money;
      case PaymentMethod.cliq:
        return Icons.account_balance_wallet;
      case PaymentMethod.visa:
        return Icons.credit_card;
    }
  }
}

class PointsModel {
  final int totalPoints;
  final List<PointsTransaction> transactions;
  final Set<PointsType> completedOneTimeActions;

  PointsModel({
    required this.totalPoints,
    required this.transactions,
    required this.completedOneTimeActions,
  });

  PointsModel copyWith({
    int? totalPoints,
    List<PointsTransaction>? transactions,
    Set<PointsType>? completedOneTimeActions,
  }) {
    return PointsModel(
      totalPoints: totalPoints ?? this.totalPoints,
      transactions: transactions ?? this.transactions,
      completedOneTimeActions: completedOneTimeActions ?? this.completedOneTimeActions,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'totalPoints': totalPoints,
      'transactions': transactions.map((t) => t.toJson()).toList(),
      'completedOneTimeActions': completedOneTimeActions.map((e) => e.toString()).toList(),
    };
  }

  factory PointsModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PointsModel(
      totalPoints: json['totalPoints'] ?? 0,
      transactions: (json['transactions'] as List? ?? [])
          .map((t) => PointsTransaction.fromJson(t))
          .toList(),
      completedOneTimeActions: (json['completedOneTimeActions'] as List? ?? [])
          .map((e) => PointsType.values.firstWhere((type) => type.toString() == e))
          .toSet(),
    );
  }
}

class PointsTransaction {
  final PointsType type;
  final int points;
  final DateTime date;
  final String description;

  PointsTransaction({
    required this.type,
    required this.points,
    required this.date,
    required this.description,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'type': type.toString(),
      'points': points,
      'date': date.toIso8601String(),
      'description': description,
    };
  }

  factory PointsTransaction.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PointsTransaction(
      type: PointsType.values.firstWhere((type) => type.toString() == json['type']),
      points: json['points'],
      date: DateTime.parse(json['date']),
      description: json['description'],
    );
  }
}

enum PointsType {
  inviteFriend,
  rateShop,
  arriveOnTime,
  shopFeedback,
  rateApp,
  appFeedback,
  makeAppointment,
}

    this.hasAccess,
    this.isOwner,
    this.isWorker,
    this.isSubscribed,
    this.hasBranches,

  });

    List<String>? specialties,

    bool? isOwner,

      location: location ?? this.location,
      lat: lat ?? this.lat,
      lng: lng ?? this.lng,
      vrfidEmail: vrfidEmail ?? this.vrfidEmail,
      vrfidPhone: vrfidPhone ?? this.vrfidPhone,
      isDeleted: isDeleted ?? this.isDeleted,
      deletedAt: deletedAt ?? this.deletedAt,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      bio: bio ?? this.bio,
      specialties: specialties ?? this.specialties,
      contactPhone: contactPhone ?? this.contactPhone,
      hasAccess: hasAccess ?? this.hasAccess,
      isOwner: isOwner ?? this.isOwner,
      isWorker: isWorker ?? this.isWorker,
      isSubscribed: isSubscribed ?? this.isSubscribed,
      hasBranches: hasBranches ?? this.hasBranches,
      hasOffers: hasOffers ?? this.hasOffers,
    );
  }
}

class ProfitData {
  final DateTime date;
  final double revenue;
  final double profit;
  final double expenses;

  ProfitData({
    required this.date,
    required this.revenue,
    required this.profit,
    required this.expenses,
  });

  factory ProfitData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ProfitData(
      date: DateTime.parse(json['date']),
      revenue: (json['revenue'] is double ? json['revenue'] : double.tryParse(json['revenue']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      profit: (json['profit'] is double ? json['profit'] : double.tryParse(json['profit']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      expenses: (json['expenses'] is double ? json['expenses'] : double.tryParse(json['expenses']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'date': date.toIso8601String(),
      'revenue': revenue,
      'profit': profit,
      'expenses': expenses,
    };
  }
}

class ProfitReport {
  final List<ProfitData> monthlyData;
  final List<ProfitData> yearlyData;
  final double totalRevenue;
  final double totalProfit;
  final double totalExpenses;

  ProfitReport({
    required this.monthlyData,
    required this.yearlyData,
    required this.totalRevenue,
    required this.totalProfit,
    required this.totalExpenses,
  });

  factory ProfitReport.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ProfitReport(
      monthlyData: List<ProfitData>.from(
        (json['monthlyData'] ?? []).map((x) => ProfitData.fromJson(x))
      ),
      yearlyData: List<ProfitData>.from(
        (json['yearlyData'] ?? []).map((x) => ProfitData.fromJson(x))
      ),
      totalRevenue: (json['totalRevenue'] is double ? json['totalRevenue'] : double.tryParse(json['totalRevenue']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      totalProfit: (json['totalProfit'] is double ? json['totalProfit'] : double.tryParse(json['totalProfit']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      totalExpenses: (json['totalExpenses'] is double ? json['totalExpenses'] : double.tryParse(json['totalExpenses']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'monthlyData': monthlyData.map((x) => x.toJson()).toList(),
      'yearlyData': yearlyData.map((x) => x.toJson()).toList(),
      'totalRevenue': totalRevenue,
      'totalProfit': totalProfit,
      'totalExpenses': totalExpenses,
    };
  }
}

class ReportModel {
  final String id;
  final String reporterName;
  final String description;
  final DateTime date;
  final ReportType type;

  ReportModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.reporterName,
    required this.description,
    required this.date,
    required this.type,
  });
}

enum ReportType {
  user,
  shop,
  content,
}

enum ReportTime {
  daily,
  weekly,
  monthly,
  annually
}

enum ReportFormat {
  pdf,
  excel,
}

class ReportRequest {
  final ReportTime time;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final String? branchId;
  final ReportFormat format;
  final String fileName;

  ReportRequest({
    required this.time,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    this.branchId,
    this.format = ReportFormat.pdf,
    required this.fileName,
  });
}

  ReportData({
    required this.id,
    required this.time,
    required this.generatedAt,
    required this.data,
    required this.downloadUrl,
  });
}
import 'shop_service_model.dart';

class ServiceSelection {
  final ShopService mainService;
  final List<ShopService> additionalServices;

  const ServiceSelection({
    required this.mainService,
    this.additionalServices = const [],
  });

  List<ShopService> get allServices => [mainService, ...additionalServices];

  ServiceSelection copyWith({
    ShopService? mainService,
    List<ShopService>? additionalServices,
  }) {
    return ServiceSelection(
      mainService: mainService ?? this.mainService,
      additionalServices: additionalServices ?? this.additionalServices,
    );
  }

  ServiceSelection addService(ShopService service) {
    return copyWith(
      additionalServices: [...additionalServices, service],
    );
  }

  ServiceSelection removeService(ShopService service) {
    return copyWith(
      additionalServices: additionalServices.where((s) => s != service).toList(),
    );
  }

  bool containsService(ShopService service) {
    return additionalServices.contains(service);
  }
}

  // Get gallery images for a specific shop
  static List<String> getShopGalleryImages(String shopTitle) {
    final shop = getMockShops().firstWhere(
          (shop) => shop['title'] == shopTitle,
      orElse: () => getMockShops().first,
    );
    return shop['galleryImages'] ?? ['assets/images/default_shop.jpg'];
  }

  // Get services for a specific shop
  static List<Map<String, String>> getShopServices(String shopTitle) {
    final shop = getMockShops().firstWhere(
          (shop) => shop['title'] == shopTitle,
      orElse: () => getMockShops().first,
    );
    return List<Map<String, String>>.from(shop['services'] ?? []);
  }

  // Helper method to create BarbershopCard widgets from shop data
  static List<BarbershopCard> createShopCards({
    required Size cardSize,
    VoidCallback? onCardTap,
    Function(bool)? onFavoriteChanged,
  }) {
    return getMockShops().map((shopData) {
      return BarbershopCard(
        title: shopData['title'],
        workingHours: shopData['workingHours'],
        bottomLeftIconPath: shopData['bottomLeftIconPath'],
        bottomRightIconPath: shopData['bottomRightIconPath'],
        middleRightIconPath: shopData['middleRightIconPath'],
        middleRightActiveIconPath: shopData['middleRightActiveIconPath'],
        profilePicture: shopData['profilePicture'],
        size: cardSize,
        isClickable: shopData['isClickable'],
        shopLocation: shopData['shopLocation'],
        shopRating: shopData['shopRating'],
        onCardTap: onCardTap,
        onFavoriteChanged: onFavoriteChanged,
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  // Method to get shops as ListView
  static Widget createShopListView({
    required Size cardSize,
    ScrollController? scrollController,
    VoidCallback? onCardTap,
    Function(bool)? onFavoriteChanged,
    EdgeInsets padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
  }) {
    return ListView.builder(
      controller: scrollController,
      padding: padding,
      itemCount: getMockShops().length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final shop = getMockShops()[index];
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16.0),
          child: BarbershopCard(
            title: shop['title'],
            workingHours: shop['workingHours'],
            bottomLeftIconPath: shop['bottomLeftIconPath'],
            bottomRightIconPath: shop['bottomRightIconPath'],
            middleRightIconPath: shop['middleRightIconPath'],
            middleRightActiveIconPath: shop['middleRightActiveIconPath'],
            profilePicture: shop['profilePicture'],
            size: cardSize,
            isClickable: shop['isClickable'],
            shopLocation: shop['shopLocation'],
            shopRating: shop['shopRating'],
            onCardTap: onCardTap,
            onFavoriteChanged: onFavoriteChanged,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'shop_service_model.dart';

class ShopScheduleSlot {
  final DateTime date;
  final TimeOfDay time;
  final ShopService shopService;

  const ShopScheduleSlot({
    required this.date,
    required this.time,
    required this.shopService,
  });

  DateTime get dateTime {
    return DateTime(
      date.year,
      date.month,
      date.day,
      time.hour,
      time.minute,
    );
  }
}

class ShopScheduleData {
  final Map<String, dynamic> data;
  final List<ShopService> shopServices;

  const ShopScheduleData({
    required this.data,
    required this.shopServices,
  });

  factory ShopScheduleData.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    final shopServices = (map['services'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [])
        .map((service) => ShopService.fromMap(service as Map<String, dynamic>))
        .toList();

    return ShopScheduleData(
      data: map,
      shopServices: shopServices,
    );
  }
}

class ShopService {
  final String name;
  final String price;
  final String duration;

  const ShopService({
    required this.name,
    required this.price,
    required this.duration,
  });

  factory ShopService.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return ShopService(
      name: map['name'] as String,
      price: map['price'] as String,
      duration: map['duration'] as String,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'name': name,
      'price': price,
      'duration': duration,
    };
  }

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      identical(this, other) ||
      other is ShopService &&
          runtimeType == other.runtimeType &&
          name == other.name;

  @override
  int get hashCode => name.hashCode;
}

class SoonestAppointment {
  final String id;
  final DateTime dateTime;
  final double totalPrice;
  final String shopName;
  final String shopPhoneNumber;
  final String shopId;
  final String customerName;
  final List<String> services;

  SoonestAppointment({
    required this.id,
    required this.dateTime,
    required this.totalPrice,
    required this.shopName,
    required this.shopPhoneNumber,
    required this.shopId,
    required this.customerName,
    required this.services,
  });

  SoonestAppointment copyWith({
    DateTime? dateTime,
    double? totalPrice,
    String? shopName,
    String? shopPhoneNumber,
  }) {
    return SoonestAppointment(
      id: id,
      dateTime: dateTime ?? this.dateTime,
      totalPrice: totalPrice ?? this.totalPrice,
      shopName: shopName ?? this.shopName,
      shopPhoneNumber: shopPhoneNumber ?? this.shopPhoneNumber,
      shopId: shopId,
      customerName: customerName,
      services: services,
    );
  }
}

class SoonestAppointmentData {
  static List<SoonestAppointment> getMockAppointments() {
    return [
      SoonestAppointment(
        id: '1',
        dateTime: DateTime.now().add(Duration(hours: 2)),
        totalPrice: 45.00,
        shopName: 'Modern Cuts Barbershop',
        shopPhoneNumber: '+1 (555) 123-4567',
        shopId: 'shop1',
        customerName: 'John Doe',
        services: ['Haircut', 'Beard Trim'],
      ),
      SoonestAppointment(
        id: '2',
        dateTime: DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 1, hours: 3)),
        totalPrice: 60.00,
        shopName: 'Elite Barber Studio',
        shopPhoneNumber: '+1 (555) 987-6543',
        shopId: 'shop2',
        customerName: 'John Doe',
        services: ['Haircut', 'Shave', 'Facial'],
      ),
      SoonestAppointment(
        id: '3',
        dateTime: DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 2, hours: 1)),
        totalPrice: 35.00,
        shopName: 'Classic Barbers',
        shopPhoneNumber: '+1 (555) 456-7890',
        shopId: 'shop3',
        customerName: 'John Doe',
        services: ['Haircut'],
      ),
    ];
  }

  static SoonestAppointment? getSoonestAppointment(List<SoonestAppointment> appointments) {
    if (appointments.isEmpty) return null;

    DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    List<SoonestAppointment> upcomingAppointments = appointments
        .where((appointment) => appointment.dateTime.isAfter(now))
        .toList();

    if (upcomingAppointments.isEmpty) return null;

    upcomingAppointments.sort((a, b) => a.dateTime.compareTo(b.dateTime));
    return upcomingAppointments.first;
  }
}

import 'report_model.dart';
import 'user_model.dart';


enum StaffRole {
  owner,
  subOwner,
  branchManager,
  barber,
  assistant,
  receptionist,
  cleaner
}

class StaffMember {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String phone;
  final StaffRole role;
  final DateTime joinDate;
  final String? profileImage;
  final String? branchId;
  final bool isActive;
  final double? hourlyRate;
  final List<String>? specialties;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? performanceMetrics;

  StaffMember({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.phone,
    required this.role,
    required this.joinDate,
    this.profileImage,
    this.branchId,
    this.isActive = true,
    this.hourlyRate,
    this.specialties,
    this.performanceMetrics,
  });

  String get roleString {
    switch (role) {
      case StaffRole.owner: return 'Owner';
      case StaffRole.subOwner: return 'Sub Owner';
      case StaffRole.branchManager: return 'Branch Manager';
      case StaffRole.barber: return 'Barber';
      case StaffRole.assistant: return 'Assistant';
      case StaffRole.receptionist: return 'Receptionist';
      case StaffRole.cleaner: return 'Cleaner';
    }
  }

  factory StaffMember.fromUser(User user, {StaffRole? role, String? branchId}) {
    return StaffMember(
      id: user.id.toString(),
      name: user.name,
      email: user.email,
      phone: '', // You might want to add phone to your User model
      role: role ?? StaffRole.barber,
      joinDate: DateTime.now(),
      profileImage: user.profilePic,
      branchId: branchId,
      isActive: true,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'email': email,
      'phone': phone,
      'role': role.toString(),
      'joinDate': joinDate.toIso8601String(),
      'profileImage': profileImage,
      'branchId': branchId,
      'isActive': isActive,
      'hourlyRate': hourlyRate,
      'specialties': specialties,
      'performanceMetrics': performanceMetrics,
    };
  }
}

class BarberPerformance {
  final String staffId;
  final DateTime date;
  final int appointmentsCompleted;
  final double totalRevenue;
  final double tips;
  final double rating;
  final Map<String, int> servicesBreakdown;

  BarberPerformance({
    required this.staffId,
    required this.date,
    required this.appointmentsCompleted,
    required this.totalRevenue,
    required this.tips,
    required this.rating,
    required this.servicesBreakdown,
  });

  double get earnings => totalRevenue + tips;
}

enum StaffReportType {
  daily,
  weekly,
  monthly,
  annually,
  performance,
  payroll,
  attendance
}

class StaffReportRequest {
  final ReportTime time;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final String? branchId;
  final String? staffId;
  final String fileName;

  StaffReportRequest({
    required this.time,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    this.branchId,
    this.staffId,
    required this.fileName,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'time': time.toString(),
      'startDate': startDate.toIso8601String(),
      'endDate': endDate.toIso8601String(),
      'branchId': branchId,
      'staffId': staffId,
      'fileName': fileName,
    };
  }
}
class Subscription {
  final String planName;
  final double price;
  final DateTime startDate;
  final DateTime endDate;
  final String status;
  final List<String> features;

  Subscription({
    required this.planName,
    required this.price,
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    required this.status,
    required this.features,
  });

  factory Subscription.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Subscription(
      planName: json['planName'] ?? '',
      price: (json['price'] is double ? json['price'] : double.tryParse(json['price']?.toString() ?? '0.0')) ?? 0.0,
      startDate: DateTime.parse(json['startDate']),
      endDate: DateTime.parse(json['endDate']),
      status: json['status'] ?? 'Active',
      features: List<String>.from(json['features'] ?? []),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'planName': planName,
      'price': price,
      'startDate': startDate.toIso8601String(),
      'endDate': endDate.toIso8601String(),
      'status': status,
      'features': features,
    };
  }

  int get daysRemaining => endDate.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
}

class ShopSubscription {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String description;
  final double price;
  final Duration duration;
  final List<String> features;
  final bool isPopular;

  const ShopSubscription({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.description,
    required this.price,
    required this.duration,
    required this.features,
    this.isPopular = false,
  });

  String get formattedPrice => '\$${price.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  String get formattedDuration {
    if (duration.inDays >= 30) {
      final months = duration.inDays ~/ 30;
      return '$months month${months > 1 ? 's' : ''}';
    }
    return '${duration.inDays} day${duration.inDays > 1 ? 's' : ''}';
  }

  ShopSubscription copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? description,
    double? price,
    Duration? duration,
    List<String>? features,
    bool? isPopular,
  }) {
    return ShopSubscription(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      price: price ?? this.price,
      duration: duration ?? this.duration,
      features: features ?? this.features,
      isPopular: isPopular ?? this.isPopular,
    );
  }
}

class User {
  final int id;
  final String email;
  final String role;
  final String name;
  final String? profilePic; // Make profilePic optional

  User({
    required this.id,
    required this.email,
    required this.role,
    required this.name,
    this.profilePic, // Make it optional in constructor
  });

  factory User.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return User(
      id: json['id'],
      email: json['email'],
      role: json['role'],
      name: json['name'],
      profilePic: json['profilePic'], // No need for default value here since it's optional
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'email': email,
      'role': role,
      'name': name,
      'profilePic': profilePic,
    };
  }

  bool get isCustomer => role.toLowerCase() == 'customer';
  bool get isBarber => role.toLowerCase() == 'barber';
  bool get isOwner => role.toLowerCase() == 'owner';
  bool get isAdmin => role.toLowerCase() == 'admin';

  // Getter for profile picture with guaranteed non-null return
  String get profilePicture {
    return profilePic ?? 'assets/mojies/image10.png';
  }

  // Static method to get current user
  static User getCurrentUser() {
    return User(
      id: 1,
      name: 'John Doe',
      email: 'john.doe@example.com',
      role: 'customer',
      profilePic: 'assets/mojies/image11.png',
    );
  }
}

class WorkingHours {
  final DateTime date;
  final DateTime scheduledStart;
  final DateTime scheduledEnd;
  final DateTime? actualStart;
  final DateTime? actualEnd;
  final bool isWorking;

  WorkingHours({
    required this.date,
    required this.scheduledStart,
    required this.scheduledEnd,
    this.actualStart,
    this.actualEnd,
    required this.isWorking,
  });

  Duration get scheduledDuration => scheduledEnd.difference(scheduledStart);
  Duration get workedDuration {
    if (actualStart != null && actualEnd != null) {
      return actualEnd!.difference(actualStart!);
    }
    return Duration.zero;
  }

  Duration get remainingTime {
    if (!isWorking || actualStart == null) return scheduledDuration;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return scheduledEnd.difference(now);
  }

  factory WorkingHours.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return WorkingHours(
      date: DateTime.parse(json['date']),
      scheduledStart: DateTime.parse(json['scheduled_start']),
      scheduledEnd: DateTime.parse(json['scheduled_end']),
      actualStart: json['actual_start'] != null ? DateTime.parse(json['actual_start']) : null,
      actualEnd: json['actual_end'] != null ? DateTime.parse(json['actual_end']) : null,
      isWorking: json['is_working'] ?? false,
    );
  }
}

      body: const Center(child: Text('Welcome Admin!')),
    );
  }

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Models/user_model.dart';
import '../../Models/branch_model.dart';
import '../../Models/pending_confirmation_model.dart';
import '../../Models/working_hours_model.dart';
import '../../Theme/glass_appbar.dart';
// import '../../Theme/sphere_neomorphic.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/bd_faded.dart';
import '../../features/sidebar/sidebar.dart';
import '../../features/sidebar/sidebar_controller.dart';
import '../Widgets/appointments_list.dart';
import '../Widgets/custom_navigation_bar.dart';
import '../Widgets/earnings_chart.dart';
import '../Widgets/stats_row.dart';
import '../Widgets/branches_overview.dart';
import '../Widgets/pending_confirmations.dart';
import '../Widgets/working_hours_tracker.dart';

class BarberHomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BarberHomePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BarberHomePage> createState() => _BarberHomePageState();
}

class _BarberHomePageState extends State<BarberHomePage> with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  late SidebarController sidebarController;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  // Simple data holders
  Map<String, dynamic> _homeData = {
    'stats': {'completed': 0, 'pending': 0},
    'appointments': [],
    'earnings': [],
  };
  List<Branch> _branches = [];
  List<PendingConfirmation> _pendingConfirmations = [];
  WorkingHours? _workingHours;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    sidebarController = SidebarController();
    sidebarController.init(this);
    _loadAllData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadAllData() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _error = null;
    });

    try {
      print('Loading all data...');

      // Load all data in parallel
      final results = await Future.wait([
        ApiService.fetchHomeData(),
        ApiService.fetchBranches(),
        ApiService.fetchPendingConfirmations(),
        ApiService.fetchTodayWorkingHours(),
      ]);

      print('Data loaded successfully');

      setState(() {
        _homeData = results[0] as Map<String, dynamic>;
        _branches = results[1] as List<Branch>;
        _pendingConfirmations = results[2] as List<PendingConfirmation>;
        _workingHours = results[3] as WorkingHours;
        _isLoading = false;
      });

    } catch (e) {
      print('Error loading data: $e');
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _isLoading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    sidebarController.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = User(
      id: 1,
      email: 'customer@example.com',
      role: 'owner',
      name: 'John Doe',
    );

    return FadedBackground(
      startColor: const Color(0xffffffff),
      endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        extendBody: true,
        body: Stack(
          children: [
            // Main Content with Animation
            AnimatedBuilder(
              animation: sidebarController.animation,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return Transform(
                  transform: Matrix4.identity()
                    ..translate(
                      sidebarController.animation.value * 250,
                      sidebarController.animation.value * 25,
                    )
                    ..scale(1 - sidebarController.animation.value * 0.3),
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: 1 - sidebarController.animation.value * 0.1,
                    child: Container(
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
                          topLeft: Radius.circular(20),
                          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(20),
                        ),
                        boxShadow: [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(
                                sidebarController.animation.value * 0.2),
                            blurRadius: 20,
                            spreadRadius: 5,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      child: Scaffold(
                        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                        extendBody: true,
                        body: _buildContent(),
                        bottomNavigationBar: CustomNavigationBar(
                          user: user,
                          currentRoute: ModalRoute.of(context)?.settings.name ?? '/',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),

            // Sidebar Overlay
            Sidebar(controller: sidebarController),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildContent() {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    }

    if (_error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Text('Error: $_error'),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _loadAllData,
              child: const Text('Retry'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    final stats = _homeData['stats'] ?? {'completed': 0, 'pending': 0};
    final appointments = _homeData['appointments'] ?? [];
    final earnings = _homeData['earnings'] ?? [];

    return CustomScrollView(
      controller: _scrollController,
      slivers: [
        GlassSliverAppBar(
          title: 'Barber Dash',
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: Image.asset(
              'assets/icons/menu.png',
              width: 30,
              height: 30,
            ),
            onPressed: () => sidebarController.toggleSidebar(),
          ),
          showBackButton: false,
        ),

        // Stats Row
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: StatsRow(
            completedOrders: (stats['completed'] ?? 0) as int,
            pendingOrders: (stats['pending'] ?? 0) as int,
          ),
        ),

        // Working Hours Tracker
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: _workingHours != null
              ? WorkingHoursTracker(workingHours: _workingHours!)
              : _buildPlaceholder('No working hours data'),
        ),

        // Shop Branches
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: _branches.isNotEmpty
              ? BranchesOverview(branches: _branches)
              : _buildPlaceholder('No branches data'),
        ),

        // Pending Confirmations
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: _pendingConfirmations.isNotEmpty
              ? PendingConfirmations(pendingConfirmations: _pendingConfirmations)
              : _buildPlaceholder('No pending confirmations'),
        ),

        // Upcoming Appointments Header
        const SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
            child: Text(
              'Upcoming Appointments',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 20,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),

        // Appointments List
        AppointmentsList(appointments: appointments),

        // Earnings Chart Header
        const SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
            child: Text(
              "Today's Earnings",
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 20,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),

        // Earnings Chart
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: EarningsChart(earningsData: earnings),
        ),

        // Bottom padding
        const SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: SizedBox(height: 20),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPlaceholder(String text) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Text(
          text,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            color: Colors.grey,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class CustomerHomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CustomerHomePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CustomerHomePage> createState() => _CustomerHomePageState();
}

class _CustomerHomePageState extends State<CustomerHomePage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late SidebarController sidebarController;
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController(); // Add scroll controller

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    sidebarController = SidebarController();
    sidebarController.init(this);
    _loadPoints();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    sidebarController.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose(); // Dispose scroll controller
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _loadPoints() {
    // Implement your points loading logic here
  }

  void _handleCardTap(Map<String, dynamic> shopData) {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (context) => ShopDetailsPage(shopData: shopData),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _handleFavoriteChanged(bool isFavorite) {
    print('Favorite status changed: $isFavorite');
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = User(
      id: 1,
      email: 'customer@example.com',
      role: 'customer',
      name: 'John Doe',
    );

    return FadedBackground(
      startColor: const Color(0xFF806C99),
      endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        extendBody: true,
        body: Stack(
          children: [
            // Main Content with Animation
            AnimatedBuilder(
              animation: sidebarController.animation,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return Transform(
                  transform: Matrix4.identity()
                    ..translate(
                      sidebarController.animation.value * 250,
                      sidebarController.animation.value * 25,
                    )
                    ..scale(1 - sidebarController.animation.value * 0.3),
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: 1 - sidebarController.animation.value * 0.1,
                    child: Container(
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        // borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                          topLeft: Radius.circular(20),
                          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(20),
                        ),
                        boxShadow: [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha:
                                sidebarController.animation.value * 0.2),
                            blurRadius: 20,
                            spreadRadius: 5,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      child: Scaffold(
                        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                        extendBody: true,
                        body: CustomScrollView(
                          controller: _scrollController,
                          slivers: [
                            // Use the fixed GlassSliverAppBar directly
                            GlassSliverAppBar(
                              title: 'Customer Home',
                              backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                              leading: IconButton( // Provide leading directly
                                icon: Image.asset(
                                  'assets/icons/menu.png',
                                  width: 30,
                                  height: 30,
                                  // color: Colors.white, // Optional: tint the icon
                                ),
                                onPressed: () => sidebarController.toggleSidebar(),
                              ),
                              showBackButton: false, // We're providing custom leading
                            ),

                            // Fixed horizontal section
                            SliverToBoxAdapter(
                              child: SizedBox(
                                width: double.infinity,
                                child: Column(
                                  children: [
                                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                    HorizontalShopScroll(),

                                    SoonestAppointmentCard(
                                      appointments: SoonestAppointmentData.getMockAppointments(),
                                      onAppointmentDelayed: (delayedAppointment) {
                                        print('Appointment delayed to: ${delayedAppointment.dateTime}');
                                      },
                                      onAppointmentRescheduleNeeded: (appointment) {
                                        print('Reschedule needed for: ${appointment.shopName}');
                                      },
                                    ),

                                    PointsTrackerWidget(),

                                    // NearBy shops link
                                    ClipRect(
                                      child: BackdropFilter(
                                        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 5, sigmaY: 5),
                                        child: Container(
                                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16.0),
                                          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                            color: Color(0x0dffffff),
                                          ),
                                          child: Padding(
                                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 25.0),
                                            child: Row(
                                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                              children: [
                                                Text(
                                                  'Shops near by',
                                                  style: TextStyle(
                                                    fontSize: 18,
                                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                                    color: Colors.grey.shade800,
                                                  ),
                                                ),
                                                const TextLink(
                                                  link: '/shops',
                                                  text: 'See all',
                                                  color: Colors.blue,
                                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                                  fontSize: 14,
                                                ),
                                              ],
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),

                            // Scrollable list section
                            SliverList(
                              delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                                (context, index) {
                                  final shop = ShopData.getMockShops()[index];
                                  return Padding(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16.0, vertical: 8.0),
                                    child: BarbershopCard(
                                      title: shop['title'] as String,
                                      workingHours: shop['workingHours'] as String,
                                      bottomLeftIconPath: shop['bottomLeftIconPath'] as String,
                                      bottomRightIconPath: shop['bottomRightIconPath'] as String,
                                      middleRightIconPath: shop['middleRightIconPath'] as String,
                                      middleRightActiveIconPath: shop['middleRightActiveIconPath'] as String,
                                      profilePicture: shop['profilePicture'] as String,
                                      size: const Size(330, 115),
                                      isClickable: shop['isClickable'] as bool,
                                      shopLocation: shop['shopLocation'] as String,
                                      shopRating: shop['shopRating'] as double,
                                      onCardTap: () => _handleCardTap(shop),
                                      onFavoriteChanged: _handleFavoriteChanged,
                                    ),
                                  );
                                },
                                childCount: min(5, ShopData.getMockShops().length),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        bottomNavigationBar: CustomNavigationBar(
                          user: user,
                          currentRoute: ModalRoute.of(context)?.settings.name ?? '/',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),

            // Sidebar Overlay
            Sidebar(controller: sidebarController),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:logger/logger.dart';
// import '../../Services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../Services/navigation_service.dart'; // Uncomment this
import '../../components/common/fields/text_field.dart';
import '../../Utils/text_field_types.dart';
import '../../Utils/box_sizes.dart';
import '../../components/common/buttons/custom_button.dart';
import '../../components/common/links/text_links.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/floating_bg.dart';

class LoginPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginPage> createState() => _LoginPageState();
}

class _LoginPageState extends State<LoginPage> {
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final Logger _logger = Logger();
  // final AuthService _authService = AuthService();

  // Add this for testing
  String _selectedRole = 'customer';
  final List<String> _availableRoles = ['customer', 'barber', 'owner', 'admin'];

  Future<void> _handleLogin(BuildContext context) async {
    final currentContext = context;

    try {
      // For testing: Use selected role instead of API response
      final userRole = _selectedRole; // Use dropdown selection

      // For production: Use actual API response
      // final response = await _authService.login(
      //   _emailController.text,
      //   _passwordController.text,
      // );
      // final userRole = response['user']['role'];

      if (currentContext.mounted) {
        NavigationService.navigateToHomeBasedOnRole(currentContext, userRole);
      }

    } catch (e) {
      _logger.e('Login error: $e');
      if (currentContext.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(currentContext).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Login failed: ${e.toString()}')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  // Simple login for testing specific roles
  void _handleTestLogin(BuildContext context, String role) {
    _logger.d('Test login as $role');
    NavigationService.navigateToHomeBasedOnRole(context, role);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    Size size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;

    return Scaffold(
      body: FloatingBackground(
        objectCount: 8,
        objectType: 'circles',
        colors: [
          Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.purple.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.teal.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.teal.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.purple.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
        ],
        minSize: 40.0,
        maxSize: 120.0,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              CustomTextField(
                placeholder: 'Email Address',
                type: TextFieldType.email,
                boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                controller: _emailController,
                onChanged: (value) {
                  _logger.d('Email: $value');
                },
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              CustomTextField(
                placeholder: 'Password',
                type: TextFieldType.password,
                boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                controller: _passwordController,
                onChanged: (value) {
                  _logger.d('Password: $value');
                },
              ),

              // Add role selection dropdown for testing
              if (const bool.fromEnvironment('DEBUG', defaultValue: true))
                _buildRoleSelector(),

              const SizedBox(height: 30),
              const Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                child: TextLink(
                  link: '/forgot-password',
                  text: 'Forgot Password?',
                  color: Colors.grey,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 30),
              CustomNeumorphicButton(
                color: Colors.blue.shade100,
                width: size.width * 0.8,
                text: 'Login',
                showText: true,
                leftIcon: Icons.login,
                showLeftIcon: true,
                showRightIcon: false,
                isActive: true,
                onTap: () => _handleLogin(context),
              ),

              // Sign up section
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'Don\'t have an account? ',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const TextLink(
                    link: '/signup',
                    text: 'Signup',
                    color: Colors.blue,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  _SocialButton(
                    onTap: () => _handleSocialLogin('Google'),
                    icon: Icons.g_mobiledata,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    iconColor: Colors.red,
                    borderColor: Colors.grey.shade300,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 20),
                  _SocialButton(
                    onTap: () => _handleSocialLogin('Facebook'),
                    icon: Icons.facebook,
                    color: const Color(0xFF1877F2),
                    iconColor: Colors.white,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 20),
                  _SocialButton(
                    onTap: () => _handleSocialLogin('Apple'),
                    icon: Icons.apple,
                    color: Colors.black,
                    iconColor: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              // Quick test buttons for different roles
              if (const bool.fromEnvironment('DEBUG', defaultValue: true))
                _buildTestButtons(context),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRoleSelector() {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
        child: DropdownButton<String>(
          value: _selectedRole,
          isExpanded: true,
          items: _availableRoles.map((String role) {
            return DropdownMenuItem<String>(
              value: role,
              child: Text(
                'Test as: ${role.toUpperCase()}',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.grey.shade700,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            );
          }).toList(),
          onChanged: (String? newValue) {
            setState(() {
              _selectedRole = newValue!;
            });
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTestButtons(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const Text(
          'Quick Test:',
          style: TextStyle(
            color: Colors.grey,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Wrap(
          spacing: 8,
          runSpacing: 8,
          children: _availableRoles.map((role) {
            return CustomNeumorphicButton(
              color: _getRoleColor(role),
              width: 100,
              text: role.toUpperCase(),
              showText: false,
              leftIcon: _getRoleIcon(role),
              showLeftIcon: true,
              showRightIcon: false,
              isActive: true,
              onTap: () => _handleTestLogin(context, role),
            );
          }).toList(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Color _getRoleColor(String role) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'customer':
        return Colors.green.shade100;
      case 'barber':
        return Colors.blue.shade100;
      case 'owner':
        return Colors.orange.shade100;
      case 'admin':
        return Colors.red.shade100;
      default:
        return Colors.grey.shade100;
    }
  }

  IconData _getRoleIcon(String role) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'customer':
        return Icons.person;
      case 'barber':
        return Icons.content_cut;
      case 'owner':
        return Icons.business;
      case 'admin':
        return Icons.admin_panel_settings;
      default:
        return Icons.person;
    }
  }

  void _handleSocialLogin(String provider) {
    _logger.d('$provider LogIn clicked');
  }
}

class _SocialButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;
  final Color iconColor;
  final Color? borderColor;

  const _SocialButton({
    required this.onTap,
    required this.icon,
    required this.color,
    required this.iconColor,
    this.borderColor,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        width: 50,
        height: 50,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color,
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          border: borderColor != null ? Border.all(color: borderColor!) : null,
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
              blurRadius: 4,
              offset: const Offset(0, 2),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          color: iconColor,
          size: 24,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
