import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:logger/logger.dart';
import '../../Services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../Services/navigation_service.dart';
import '../../components/common/fields/text_field.dart';
import '../../Utils/text_field_types.dart';
import '../../Utils/box_sizes.dart';
import '../../components/common/buttons/custom_button.dart';
import '../../components/common/links/text_links.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/floating_bg.dart';

class SignupPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SignupPage({Key? key}) : super(key: key);

  @override
  State<SignupPage> createState() => _SignupPageState();
}

class _SignupPageState extends State<SignupPage> {
  final _firstNameController = TextEditingController();
  final _secondNameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPasswordController = TextEditingController();
  final Logger _logger = Logger();
  final AuthService _authService = AuthService();

  String? _selectedGender;
  String? _selectedRole;

  Future<void> _handleSignup(BuildContext context) async {
    final currentContext = context;

    // Basic validation
    if (_passwordController.text != _confirmPasswordController.text) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(currentContext).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Passwords do not match')),
      );
      return;
    }

    if (_selectedGender == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(currentContext).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Please select a gender')),
      );
      return;
    }

    if (_selectedRole == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(currentContext).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Please select a role')),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      // Now using the signup method instead of login
      final response = await _authService.signup(
        _firstNameController.text,
        _secondNameController.text,
        _emailController.text,
        _phoneController.text,
        _selectedGender!,
        _passwordController.text,
        _selectedRole!,
      );

      final userRole = response['user']['role'];

      if (currentContext.mounted) {
        NavigationService.navigateToHomeBasedOnRole(currentContext, userRole);
      }

    } catch (e) {
      _logger.e('Signup error: $e');
      if (currentContext.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(currentContext).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Signup failed: ${e.toString()}')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _handleSocialSignup(String provider) {
    _logger.d('$provider signup clicked');
    // Implement social signup logic here
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    Size size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;

    return Scaffold(
      body: FloatingBackground(
        objectCount: 8,
        objectType: 'circles',
        colors: [
          Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.purple.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.teal.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.teal.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.orange.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
          Colors.purple.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
        ],
        minSize: 40.0,
        maxSize: 120.0,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // First Name
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'First Name',
                  type: TextFieldType.text, // Changed from TextFieldType.name to TextFieldType.text
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _firstNameController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('First Name: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Second Name
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'Second Name',
                  type: TextFieldType.text, // Changed from TextFieldType.name to TextFieldType.text
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _secondNameController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('Second Name: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Email
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'Email Address',
                  type: TextFieldType.email,
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _emailController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('Email: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Phone Number
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'Phone Number',
                  type: TextFieldType.phone,
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _phoneController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('Phone: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Gender Dropdown
                Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                  ),
                  child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                    child: DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedGender,
                      hint: Text(
                        'Select Gender',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.grey.shade500,
                          fontSize: 16,
                        ),
                      ),
                      isExpanded: true,
                      items: ['Male', 'Female', 'Other']
                          .map((gender) => DropdownMenuItem(
                        value: gender,
                        child: Text(gender),
                      ))
                          .toList(),
                      onChanged: (value) {
                        setState(() {
                          _selectedGender = value;
                        });
                        _logger.d('Gender: $value');
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Role Selection
                Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                  ),
                  child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                    child: DropdownButton<String>(
                      value: _selectedRole,
                      hint: Text(
                        'Select Role',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.grey.shade500,
                          fontSize: 16,
                        ),
                      ),
                      isExpanded: true,
                      items: ['customer', 'barber', 'owner', 'admin']
                          .map((role) => DropdownMenuItem(
                        value: role,
                        child: Text(role[0].toUpperCase() + role.substring(1)),
                      ))
                          .toList(),
                      onChanged: (value) {
                        setState(() {
                          _selectedRole = value;
                        });
                        _logger.d('Role: $value');
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Password
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'Password',
                  type: TextFieldType.password,
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _passwordController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('Password: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Confirm Password
                CustomTextField(
                  placeholder: 'Confirm Password',
                  type: TextFieldType.password,
                  boxSize: BoxSize.full,
                  controller: _confirmPasswordController,
                  onChanged: (value) {
                    _logger.d('Confirm Password: $value');
                  },
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 30),

                // Signup Button
                CustomNeumorphicButton(
                  color: Colors.blue.shade100,
                  width: size.width * 0.8,
                  text: 'Sign Up',
                  showText: true,
                  leftIcon: Icons.person_add,
                  showLeftIcon: true,
                  showRightIcon: false,
                  isActive: true,
                  onTap: () => _handleSignup(context),
                ),

                // Back to login section
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Already have an account? ',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                        fontSize: 14,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const TextLink(
                      link: '/login',
                      text: 'Back to Login',
                      color: Colors.blue,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),

                // Social signup section
                const SizedBox(height: 30),
                Text(
                  'Or sign up with',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    // Google button
                    _SocialButton(
                      onTap: () => _handleSocialSignup('Google'),
                      icon: Icons.g_mobiledata,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      iconColor: Colors.red,
                      borderColor: Colors.grey.shade300,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 20),

                    // Facebook button
                    _SocialButton(
                      onTap: () => _handleSocialSignup('Facebook'),
                      icon: Icons.facebook,
                      color: const Color(0xFF1877F2),
                      iconColor: Colors.white,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 20),

                    // Apple button
                    _SocialButton(
                      onTap: () => _handleSocialSignup('Apple'),
                      icon: Icons.apple,
                      color: Colors.black,
                      iconColor: Colors.white,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SocialButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;
  final Color iconColor;
  final Color? borderColor;

  const _SocialButton({
    Key? key,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.icon,
    required this.color,
    required this.iconColor,
    this.borderColor,
  }) : super(key: key);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        width: 50,
        height: 50,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color,
          shape: BoxShape.circle,
          border: borderColor != null ? Border.all(color: borderColor!) : null,
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
              blurRadius: 4,
              offset: const Offset(0, 2),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          color: iconColor,
          size: 24,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/appointment_model.dart';
import '../../Services/appointment_service.dart';
import '../../Theme/glass_appbar.dart';
import '../../Theme/sphere_neomorphic.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/bd_faded.dart';
import '../widgets/appointment_accordion.dart';

class AppointmentsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const AppointmentsPage({Key? key}) : super(key: key);

  @override
  _AppointmentsPageState createState() => _AppointmentsPageState();
}

class _AppointmentsPageState extends State<AppointmentsPage> {
  late Future<List<ShopAppointment>> _appointmentsFuture;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  bool _homeActive = false;
  bool _settingsActive = false;
  bool _profileActive = false;
  bool _infoActive = true;
  bool _searchActive = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _appointmentsFuture = AppointmentService.fetchAppointments();
  }

  Future<void> _refreshAppointments() async {
    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
    });

    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));

    setState(() {
      _appointmentsFuture = AppointmentService.fetchAppointments();
      _isLoading = false;
    });
  }

  Widget _buildContent(List<ShopAppointment> appointments) {
    return SliverList(
      delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
        (context, index) {
          return AppointmentAccordion(
            appointment: appointments[index],
          );
        },
        childCount: appointments.length,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLoading() {
    return const SliverFillRemaining(
      child: Center(
        child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildError(String error) {
    return SliverFillRemaining(
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.error_outline,
              size: 64,
              color: Colors.grey[400],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'Error loading appointments',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.grey[600],
                fontSize: 16,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _refreshAppointments,
              child: const Text('Try Again'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmpty() {
    return SliverFillRemaining(
      child: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.calendar_today,
              size: 64,
              color: Colors.grey[400],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'No appointments found',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.grey[600],
                fontSize: 16,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      children: [
        // FadedBackground covering the entire screen
        Positioned.fill(
          child: FadedBackground(
            startColor: Color(0x80806C99),
            endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
          ),
        ),
        // Scaffold on top with transparent background
        Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          body: RefreshIndicator(
            onRefresh: _refreshAppointments,
            child: CustomScrollView(
              slivers: [
                GlassSliverAppBar(
                  title: 'My Appointments',
                  showBackButton: true,
                  textColor: Colors.white,
                  pinned: true,
                ),
                FutureBuilder<List<ShopAppointment>>(
                  future: _appointmentsFuture,
                  builder: (context, snapshot) {
                    if (_isLoading) {
                      return _buildLoading();
                    }

                    if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                      return _buildLoading();
                    }

                    if (snapshot.hasError) {
                      return _buildError(snapshot.error.toString());
                    }

                    if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.isEmpty) {
                      return _buildEmpty();
                    }

                    return _buildContent(snapshot.data!);
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          bottomNavigationBar: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            child: Container(
              height: 90,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFFC25858),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                    blurRadius: 10,
                    offset: const Offset(5, 5),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                children: [
                  NeomorphicSphere(
                    activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_view.png',
                    inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_front.png',
                    size: 80,
                    navigationRoute: '/customer_home',
                    isActive: _homeActive,
                    onTap: () {
                      print('Home sphere tapped');
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = true;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    },
                    onActiveChanged: (active) {
                      if (active) {
                        setState(() {
                          _homeActive = true;
                          _settingsActive = false;
                          _profileActive = false;
                          _infoActive = false;
                          _searchActive = false;
                        });
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  NeomorphicSphere(
                    activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash_view.png',
                    inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash.png',
                    size: 80,
                    navigationRoute: '/settings',
                    isActive: _settingsActive,
                    onTap: () {
                      print('Settings sphere tapped');
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = true;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    },
                    onActiveChanged: (active) {
                      if (active) {
                        setState(() {
                          _homeActive = false;
                          _settingsActive = true;
                          _profileActive = false;
                          _infoActive = false;
                          _searchActive = false;
                        });
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  NeomorphicSphere(
                    activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire_view.png',
                    inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire.png',
                    size: 80,
                    navigationRoute: '/profile',
                    isActive: _profileActive,
                    onTap: () {
                      print('Profile sphere tapped');
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = true;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    },
                    onActiveChanged: (active) {
                      if (active) {
                        setState(() {
                          _homeActive = false;
                          _settingsActive = false;
                          _profileActive = true;
                          _infoActive = false;
                          _searchActive = false;
                        });
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  NeomorphicSphere(
                    activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock_view.png',
                    inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock.png',
                    size: 80,
                    isActive: _infoActive,
                    onTap: () {
                      Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/appointments');
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = true;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    },
                    onActiveChanged: (active) {
                      if (active) {
                        setState(() {
                          _homeActive = false;
                          _settingsActive = false;
                          _profileActive = false;
                          _infoActive = true;
                          _searchActive = false;
                        });
                      }
                    },
                  ),
                  NeomorphicSphere(
                    activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male_view.png',
                    inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male.png',
                    size: 80,
                    isActive: _searchActive,
                    onTap: () {
                      Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/profile');
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = true;
                      });
                    },
                  )
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        )
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/shops_model.dart';
import '../../components/cards/barbershop_card.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/bd_faded.dart';
import '../../Theme/glass_appbar.dart';
import '../Navigations/shop_details.dart';

class ShopListPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ShopListPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ShopListPage> createState() => _ShopListPageState();
}

class _ShopListPageState extends State<ShopListPage> {
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  void _handleCardTap(Map<String, dynamic> shopData) {
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (context) =>
            ShopDetailsPage(
              shopData: shopData,
            ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _handleFavoriteChanged(bool isFavorite) {
    print('Favorite status: $isFavorite');
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final allShops = ShopData.getMockShops();

    return Stack(
      children: [
        // FadedBackground covering the entire screen
        Positioned.fill(
          child: FadedBackground(
            startColor: Color(0x80806C99),
            endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
          ),
        ),
        // Scaffold on top with transparent background
        Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          body: CustomScrollView(
            controller: _scrollController,
            slivers: [
              // Expanded GlassSliverAppBar with gradient and dynamic size
              GlassSliverAppBar(
                title: 'Navigate Barbershops',
                showBackButton: true,
                textColor: Colors.white,
                pinned: true,
              ),
              // Shop list
              SliverList(
                delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                      (context, index) {
                    final shopData = allShops[index];
                    return Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 16.0,
                        vertical: 8.0,
                      ),
                      child: BarbershopCard(
                        title: shopData['title'] as String,
                        workingHours: shopData['workingHours'] as String,
                        bottomLeftIconPath: shopData['bottomLeftIconPath'] as String,
                        bottomRightIconPath: shopData['bottomRightIconPath'] as String,
                        middleRightIconPath: shopData['middleRightIconPath'] as String,
                        middleRightActiveIconPath: shopData['middleRightActiveIconPath'] as String,
                        profilePicture: shopData['profilePicture'] as String,
                        size: const Size(330, 115),
                        isClickable: shopData['isClickable'] as bool,
                        shopLocation: shopData['shopLocation'] as String,
                        shopRating: shopData['shopRating'] as double,
                        onCardTap: () => _handleCardTap(shopData),
                        onFavoriteChanged: _handleFavoriteChanged,
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                  childCount: allShops.length,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/barber.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../components/common/custom_app_bar.dart';
import '../../components/common/loading_indicator.dart';
import '../Widgets/barber_card.dart';

class BarbersListPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BarbersListPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BarbersListPage> createState() => _BarbersListPageState();
}

class _BarbersListPageState extends State<BarbersListPage> {
  late Future<List<Barber>> _barbersFuture;
  // final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _barbersFuture = ApiService.getBarbers();
  }

  void _refreshData() {
    setState(() {
      _barbersFuture = ApiService.getBarbers();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: CustomAppBar(
        title: 'Barbers',
        showBackButton: true,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: _refreshData,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: FutureBuilder<List<Barber>>(
        future: _barbersFuture,
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading barbers...');
          }

          if (snapshot.hasError) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.error, size: 64, color: AppColors.errorColor),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  const Text(
                    'Failed to load barbers',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _refreshData,
                    child: const Text('Retry'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          final barbers = snapshot.data!;

          if (barbers.isEmpty) {
            return const Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.people, size: 64, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    'No barbers found',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          return RefreshIndicator(
            onRefresh: () async => _refreshData(),
            child: ListView.builder(
              itemCount: barbers.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final barber = barbers[index];
                return BarberCard(
                  barber: barber,
                  onTap: () => _showBarberDetails(barber),
                  onEdit: () => _editBarber(barber),
                  onDelete: () => _deleteBarber(barber),
                );
              },
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _addNewBarber,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showBarberDetails(Barber barber) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(barber.name),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('Email: ${barber.email}'),
            Text('Phone: ${barber.phone}'),
            Text('Rating: ${barber.rating}'),
            Text('Total Appointments: ${barber.totalAppointments}'),
            Text('Specialties: ${barber.specialties.join(', ')}'),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Close'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addNewBarber() {
    // Implement add new barber functionality
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Add new barber functionality')),
    );
  }

  void _editBarber(Barber barber) {
    // Implement edit barber functionality
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text('Edit ${barber.name}')),
    );
  }

  void _deleteBarber(Barber barber) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Delete Barber'),
        content: Text('Are you sure you want to delete ${barber.name}?'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              // Implement delete functionality
              Navigator.of(context).pop();
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(content: Text('Deleted ${barber.name}')),
              );
            },
            child: const Text('Delete', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/barber.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../components/common/custom_app_bar.dart';
import '../../components/common/loading_indicator.dart';
import '../Widgets/barber_performance_chart.dart';

class BarberPerformancePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BarberPerformancePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BarberPerformancePage> createState() => _BarberPerformancePageState();
}

class _BarberPerformancePageState extends State<BarberPerformancePage> {
  late Future<List<Barber>> _barbersFuture;
  // final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _barbersFuture = ApiService.getBarbers();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: CustomAppBar(title: 'Performance', showBackButton: true),
      body: FutureBuilder<List<Barber>>(
        future: _barbersFuture,
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading performance data...');
          }

          if (snapshot.hasError || snapshot.data == null) {
            return const Center(
              child: Text('Failed to load performance data'),
            );
          }

          final barbers = snapshot.data!;

          return SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                BarberPerformanceChart(barbers: barbers),
                _buildPerformanceList(barbers),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPerformanceList(List<Barber> barbers) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Detailed Performance',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 18,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ...barbers.map((barber) => _buildPerformanceItem(barber)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPerformanceItem(Barber barber) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppColors.backgroundColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          CircleAvatar(
            backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
            child: Text(barber.name[0]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  barber.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '${barber.totalAppointments} appointments • \$${barber.totalEarnings.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Chip(
            label: Text(barber.rating.toString()),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/branch_model.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../components/common/custom_app_bar.dart';
import '../../components/common/loading_indicator.dart';
import '../Widgets/branch_card_2.dart';

class BranchesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const BranchesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<BranchesPage> createState() => _BranchesPageState();
}

class _BranchesPageState extends State<BranchesPage> {
  late Future<List<Branch>> _branchesFuture;
  // final ApiService _apiService = ApiService();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _branchesFuture = ApiService.getBranches();
  }

  void _refreshData() {
    setState(() {
      _branchesFuture = ApiService.getBranches();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: CustomAppBar(
        title: 'Branches',
        showBackButton: true,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: _refreshData,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: FutureBuilder<List<Branch>>(
        future: _branchesFuture,
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading branches...');
          }

          if (snapshot.hasError) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.error, size: 64, color: AppColors.errorColor),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  const Text('Failed to load branches'),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _refreshData,
                    child: const Text('Retry'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          final branches = snapshot.data!;

          if (branches.isEmpty) {
            return const Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.store, size: 64, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('No branches found'),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          return RefreshIndicator(
            onRefresh: () async => _refreshData(),
            child: ListView.builder(
              itemCount: branches.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final branch = branches[index];
                return BranchCard2(
                  branch: branch,
                  onTap: () => _showBranchDetails(branch),
                  onAssignManager: () => _assignManager(branch),
                );
              },
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _addNewBranch,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
        child: const Icon(Icons.add, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showBranchDetails(Branch branch) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(branch.name),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('Address: ${branch.address}'),
            Text('Phone: ${branch.phone}'),
            Text('Staff: ${branch.totalStaff}'), // Using getter
            Text('Monthly Revenue: \$${branch.formattedMonthlyRevenue}'),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Close'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addNewBranch() {
    // Implement add new branch functionality
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Add new branch functionality')),
    );
  }

  void _assignManager(Branch branch) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Assign Manager'),
        content: const Text('Select a staff member to assign as branch manager:'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              // Implement manager assignment
              Navigator.of(context).pop();
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(content: Text('Manager assigned for ${branch.name}')),
              );
            },
            child: const Text('Assign'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/cart_model.dart';
import '../../Services/cart_helpers.dart';
import '../../Theme/glass_appbar.dart';
import '../../Theme/sphere_neomorphic.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/bd_faded.dart';
import '../loadings/cart_mock_data.dart';
import '../widgets/cart_item_widget.dart';
import '../widgets/suggested_product_widget.dart';

class CartPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CartPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CartPage> createState() => _CartPageState();
}

class _CartPageState extends State<CartPage> {
  List<CartItem> cartItems = MockData.getCartItems();
  List<Product> suggestedProducts = MockData.getSuggestedProducts();

  bool _homeActive = false;
  bool _settingsActive = true;
  bool _profileActive = false;
  bool _infoActive = false;
  bool _searchActive = false;

  void _increaseQuantity(int index) {
    setState(() {
      CartHelpers.increaseQuantity(cartItems[index]);
    });
  }

  void _decreaseQuantity(int index) {
    setState(() {
      CartHelpers.decreaseQuantity(cartItems[index]);
    });
  }

  void _removeItem(int index) {
    setState(() {
      cartItems.removeAt(index);
    });
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Item removed from cart')),
    );
  }

  void _addSuggestedToCart(Product product) {
    setState(() {
      CartHelpers.addToCart(cartItems, product);
    });
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text('${product.name} added to cart')),
    );
  }

  void _checkout() {
    if (cartItems.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Your cart is empty')),
      );
      return;
    }

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Checkout'),
        content: Text(
            'Total: ${CartHelpers.formatPrice(CartHelpers.calculateTotalPrice(cartItems))}'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              setState(() {
                cartItems.clear();
              });
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(content: Text('Order placed successfully!')),
              );
            },
            child: const Text('Confirm'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final totalPrice = CartHelpers.calculateTotalPrice(cartItems);
    final mediaQuery = MediaQuery.of(context);

    return FadedBackground(
      startColor: const Color(0xffffffff),
      endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        body: CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            GlassSliverAppBar(
              title: 'Shopping Cart',
              backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
            ),
            SliverToBoxAdapter(
              child: ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: BoxConstraints(
                  minHeight: mediaQuery.size.height -
                            mediaQuery.padding.top -
                            kToolbarHeight,
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    // Cart Items Section
                    if (cartItems.isEmpty)
                      SizedBox(
                        height: mediaQuery.size.height * 0.4,
                        child: const Center(
                          child: Column(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                            children: [
                              Icon(Icons.shopping_cart_outlined,
                                  size: 64, color: Colors.grey),
                              SizedBox(height: 16),
                              Text(
                                'Your cart is empty',
                                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: Colors.grey),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    else
                      Column(
                        children: [
                          ...cartItems.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                            final index = entry.key;
                            final item = entry.value;
                            return CartItemWidget(
                              item: item,
                              onIncrease: () => _increaseQuantity(index),
                              onDecrease: () => _decreaseQuantity(index),
                              onRemove: () => _removeItem(index),
                            );
                          }),

                          // Checkout Section
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 16),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: Color(0x80ffffff),
                              boxShadow: [
                                BoxShadow(
                                  color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.2), // Fixed: withOpacity instead of withValues
                                  blurRadius: 4,
                                  offset: const Offset(0, -2),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            child: Column(
                              children: [
                                Row(
                                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                  children: [
                                    const Text(
                                      'Total:',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 18,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    Text(
                                      CartHelpers.formatPrice(totalPrice),
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 20,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        color: Color(0xffc25858),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                SizedBox(
                                  width: double.infinity,
                                  child: ElevatedButton(
                                    onPressed: _checkout,
                                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                      backgroundColor: Color(0x802ffda2),
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    child: const Text(
                                      'Checkout',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        fontSize: 16,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),

                    // Suggested Products Section
                    const Padding(
                      padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
                      child: Align(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                        child: Text(
                          'Suggested Items',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 18,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    SizedBox(
                      height: 250,
                      child: ListView.builder(
                        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                        itemCount: suggestedProducts.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                          final product = suggestedProducts[index];
                          return SuggestedProductWidget(
                            product: product,
                            onAddToCart: () => _addSuggestedToCart(product),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16), // Bottom padding
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        bottomNavigationBar: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
          child: Container(
            height: 90,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFFC25858),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                  blurRadius: 10,
                  offset: const Offset(5, 5),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
              children: [
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_front.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/home',
                  isActive: _homeActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Home sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = true;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = true;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/settings',
                  isActive: _settingsActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Settings sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = true;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = true;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/profile',
                  isActive: _profileActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Profile sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = true;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = true;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock.png',
                  size: 80,
                  isActive: _infoActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/appointments');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = true;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = true;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male.png',
                  size: 80,
                  isActive: _searchActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/profile');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = true;
                    });
                  },
                )
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import '../../Models/user_model.dart';

class NavItem {
  final String id;
  final String activeIconPath;
  final String inactiveIconPath;
  final String route;
  final String label;

  const NavItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.activeIconPath,
    required this.inactiveIconPath,
    required this.route,
    required this.label,
  });
}

class NavigationConfig {
  static String getHomeRoute(User user) {
    if (user.isCustomer) {
      return '/customer_home';
    } else {
      return '/barber_home';
    }
  }

  static List<NavItem> getNavItems(User user, String currentRoute) {
    final allItems = [
      NavItem(
        id: 'home',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_front.png',
        route: getHomeRoute(user),
        label: 'Home',
      ),
      NavItem(
        id: 'search',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/search_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/search.png',
        route: '/search',
        label: 'Search',
      ),
      NavItem(
        id: 'history',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock.png',
        route: '/history',
        label: 'History',
      ),
      NavItem(
        id: 'admin',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/admin_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/admin.png',
        route: '/owner',
        label: 'Admin',
      ),
      NavItem(
        id: 'staff',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/staff_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/staff.png',
        route: '/staff',
        label: 'Staff',
      ),
      NavItem(
        id: 'profile',
        activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male_view.png',
        inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male.png',
        route: '/profile',
        label: 'Profile',
      ),
    ];

    // Filter items based on user role
    return allItems.where((item) {
      switch (item.id) {
        case 'search':
          return user.isCustomer;
        case 'admin':
        case 'staff':
          return user.isOwner;
        default:
          return true;
      }
    }).toList();
  }

  static bool isItemActive(NavItem item, String currentRoute, User user) {
    if (item.id == 'home') {
      final homeRoute = getHomeRoute(user);
      return currentRoute == homeRoute || currentRoute == '/';
    }
    return currentRoute == item.route;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/profile_model.dart';
import '../../Models/user_model.dart';
import '../../Theme/glass_appbar.dart';
import '../../features/Backgrounds/bd_faded.dart';
import '../Profiles/profile_widgets.dart';
import '../Profiles/profile_helper_functions.dart';
import '../Widgets/custom_navigation_bar.dart';

class ProfilePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProfilePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ProfilePage> createState() => _ProfilePageState();
}

class _ProfilePageState extends State<ProfilePage> {
  UserProfile? _userProfile;
  UserProfile? _originalProfile;
  bool _isEditing = false;
  bool _isLoading = true;
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  // Controllers for editable fields
  final TextEditingController _firstNameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _lastNameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _usernameController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _bioController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _locationController = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController _specialtiesController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchUserProfile();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchUserProfile() async {
    try {
      // Simulate API call - replace with your actual API call
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));

      // Mock data - replace with actual API response
      final mockProfile = UserProfile(
        firstName: 'John',
        lastName: 'Doe',
        email: 'john.doe@example.com',
        username: 'johndoe',
        phone: '+1234567890',
        role: 'Customer',
        gender: 'Male',
        profilePic: 'assets/mojies/image65.png',
        location: 'New York, USA',
        bio: 'Professional barber with 5 years of experience',
        specialties: ['Haircut', 'Beard Trim', 'Shaving'],
        vrfidEmail: true,
        vrfidPhone: true,
        isSubscribed: true,
        isOwner: false,
        isWorker: false,
        createdAt: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 365)),
        updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
      );

      setState(() {
        _userProfile = mockProfile;
        _originalProfile = mockProfile;
        _isLoading = false;
      });

      _initializeControllers();
    } catch (e) {
      print('Error fetching profile: $e');
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
      });

      // Show error message
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Failed to load profile: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _initializeControllers() {
    ProfileHelperFunctions.initializeControllers(
      userProfile: _userProfile,
      controllers: {
        'firstName': _firstNameController,
        'lastName': _lastNameController,
        'username': _usernameController,
        'email': _emailController,
        'phone': _phoneController,
        'bio': _bioController,
        'location': _locationController,
        'specialties': _specialtiesController,
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _firstNameController.dispose();
    _lastNameController.dispose();
    _usernameController.dispose();
    _emailController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _bioController.dispose();
    _locationController.dispose();
    _specialtiesController.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _toggleEdit() {
    setState(() {
      if (_isEditing) {
        // Cancel editing - revert changes
        _userProfile = _originalProfile;
        _initializeControllers();
      }
      _isEditing = !_isEditing;
    });
  }

  void _saveChanges() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate() && _userProfile != null) {
      final updatedProfile = ProfileHelperFunctions.updateProfileFromControllers(
        userProfile: _userProfile!,
        controllers: {
          'firstName': _firstNameController,
          'lastName': _lastNameController,
          'username': _usernameController,
          'email': _emailController,
          'phone': _phoneController,
          'bio': _bioController,
          'location': _locationController,
          'specialties': _specialtiesController,
        },
      );

      setState(() {
        _userProfile = updatedProfile;
        _originalProfile = updatedProfile;
        _isEditing = false;
      });

      _saveToBackend(updatedProfile);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Profile updated successfully')),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveToBackend(UserProfile profile) async {
    await ProfileHelperFunctions.saveToBackend(
      profile: profile,
      context: context,
      onError: () {
        setState(() {
          _userProfile = _originalProfile;
          _initializeControllers();
        });
      },
    );
  }

  void _deleteProfile() {
    ProfileHelperFunctions.showDeleteConfirmationDialog(
      context: context,
      onConfirm: _performDelete,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _performDelete() async {
    await ProfileHelperFunctions.performDelete(
      context: context,
      onSuccess: () {
        Navigator.popUntil(context, (route) => route.isFirst);
      },
    );
  }

  void _changeProfilePicture() {
    ProfileHelperFunctions.showImagePickerDialog(context: context);
  }

  String _getAccountType() {
    return ProfileHelperFunctions.getAccountType(_userProfile);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = User(
      id: 1,
      email: 'customer@example.com',
      role: 'customer',
      name: 'John Doe',
    );
    return Stack(
      children: [
        Positioned.fill(
          child: FadedBackground(
            startColor: const Color(0x80806C99),
            endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
          ),
        ),
        Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,

          bottomNavigationBar: CustomNavigationBar(
            user: user,
            currentRoute: ModalRoute.of(context)?.settings.name ?? '/',
          ),

          body: _isLoading
              ? ProfileWidgets.buildLoadingState()
              : CustomScrollView(
            controller: _scrollController,
            slivers: [
              GlassSliverAppBar(
                title: 'My Profile',
                showBackButton: true,
                textColor: Colors.white,
                pinned: true,
                actions: [
                  IconButton(
                    icon: Icon(_isEditing ? Icons.close : Icons.edit, color: Colors.white),
                    onPressed: _toggleEdit,
                    tooltip: _isEditing ? 'Cancel' : 'Edit',
                  ),
                  if (_isEditing)
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white),
                      onPressed: _saveChanges,
                      tooltip: 'Check',
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              SliverFillRemaining(
                hasScrollBody: false,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: ProfileWidgets.buildProfileContent(
                    formKey: _formKey,
                    userProfile: _userProfile!,
                    isEditing: _isEditing,
                    controllers: {
                      'firstName': _firstNameController,
                      'lastName': _lastNameController,
                      'username': _usernameController,
                      'email': _emailController,
                      'phone': _phoneController,
                      'bio': _bioController,
                      'location': _locationController,
                      'specialties': _specialtiesController,
                    },
                    getAccountType: _getAccountType,
                    onDeleteProfile: _deleteProfile,
                    onChangeProfilePicture: _changeProfilePicture,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/report_model.dart';
import '../../Models/staff_models.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../components/common/custom_app_bar.dart';
import '../Widgets/report_download_dialog.dart';

class ReportsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReportsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ReportsPage> createState() => _ReportsPageState();
}

class _ReportsPageState extends State<ReportsPage> {
  ReportTime _selectedTime = ReportTime.monthly;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: CustomAppBar(title: 'Reports', showBackButton: true),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          _buildReportTypeSelector(),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                _buildReportCard(
                  'Staff Performance Report',
                  'Detailed analysis of barber performance',
                  Icons.assessment,
                  AppColors.primaryColor,
                ),
                _buildReportCard(
                  'Financial Report',
                  'Revenue, expenses and profit analysis',
                  Icons.attach_money,
                  AppColors.successColor,
                ),
                _buildReportCard(
                  'Appointment Report',
                  'Booking and scheduling overview',
                  Icons.calendar_today,
                  AppColors.secondaryColor,
                ),
                _buildReportCard(
                  'Inventory Report',
                  'Product usage and stock levels',
                  Icons.inventory,
                  AppColors.warningColor,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportTypeSelector() {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text(
              'Report Period',
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<ReportTime>(
              value: _selectedTime,
              onChanged: (time) {
                setState(() {
                  _selectedTime = time!;
                });
              },
              items: ReportTime.values.map((time) {
                return DropdownMenuItem(
                  value: time,
                  child: Text(_getTimeDisplayName(time)),
                );
              }).toList(),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportCard(String title, String description, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      child: ListTile(
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        leading: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: color.withOpacity(0.1),
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
          child: Icon(icon, color: color),
        ),
        title: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        subtitle: Text(description),
        trailing: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.download),
          onPressed: () => _downloadReport(title),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _downloadReport(String reportName) async {
    final request = StaffReportRequest(
      time: _selectedTime,
      startDate: _getStartDate(),
      endDate: DateTime.now(),
      fileName: '${reportName.toLowerCase().replaceAll(' ', '_')}_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
    );

    final updatedRequest = await showDialog<StaffReportRequest>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => ReportDownloadDialog(initialRequest: request),
    );

    if (updatedRequest != null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Generating report...')),
      );

      try {
        final downloadUrl = await ApiService.generateStaffReport(updatedRequest);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Report downloaded: $downloadUrl')),
        );
      } catch (e) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Failed to generate report: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  DateTime _getStartDate() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    switch (_selectedTime) {
      case ReportTime.daily:
        return DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
      case ReportTime.weekly:
        return now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7));
      case ReportTime.monthly:
        return DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1);
      case ReportTime.annually:
        return DateTime(now.year, 1, 1);
    }
  }

  String _getTimeDisplayName(ReportTime time) {
    switch (time) {
      case ReportTime.daily:
        return 'Daily';
      case ReportTime.weekly:
        return 'Weekly';
      case ReportTime.monthly:
        return 'Monthly';
      case ReportTime.annually:
        return 'Annually';
    }
  }
}
