class SettingsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SettingsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<SettingsPage> createState() => _SettingsPageState();
}

class _SettingsPageState extends State<SettingsPage> {
  final ThemeService _themeService = ThemeService();
  final LanguageService _languageService = LanguageService();
  final LocationService _locationService = LocationService();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FadedBackground(
      startColor: const Color(0xFF806C99),
      endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        extendBody: true,
        body: CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            GlassSliverAppBar(
              title: 'Settings',
            ),
            SliverPadding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              sliver: SliverList(
                delegate: SliverChildListDelegate([
                  // User Info Section
                  const UserInfoHeader(),
                  const SectionDivider(),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Account Settings
                  _buildSectionHeader('Account Settings'),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.person,
                    title: 'Profile',
                    onTap: () => Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/profile'),
                  ),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.lock,
                    title: 'Change Password',
                    onTap: () => _showChangePasswordDialog(),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // App Preferences
                  _buildSectionHeader('App Preferences'),
                  ValueListenableBuilder<String>(
                    valueListenable: _languageService.languageNotifier,
                    builder: (context, language, child) {
                      return SettingTile(
                        icon: Icons.language,
                        title: 'Change Language',
                        subtitle: language,
                        onTap: () => _changeLanguage(),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                  ValueListenableBuilder<String>(
                    valueListenable: _themeService.themeModeNotifier,
                    builder: (context, themeMode, child) {
                      return SettingTile(
                        icon: _getThemeIcon(themeMode),
                        title: 'Change Theme',
                        subtitle: themeMode,
                        onTap: () => _changeTheme(),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                  ValueListenableBuilder<bool>(
                    valueListenable: _locationService.locationEnabledNotifier,
                    builder: (context, isEnabled, child) {
                      return CustomSwitchTile(
                        icon: Icons.location_on,
                        title: 'Location Privacy',
                        subtitle: isEnabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled',
                        value: isEnabled,
                        onChanged: (value) => _locationService.toggleLocation(value),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Support & Feedback
                  _buildSectionHeader('Support & Feedback'),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.star,
                    title: 'Rate Us',
                    onTap: () => _rateApp(),
                  ),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.feedback,
                    title: 'Send Feedback',
                    onTap: () => OverlayService.showFeedback(),
                  ),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.report_problem,
                    title: 'Report a Problem',
                    onTap: () => OverlayService.showReportProblem(),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Additional Suggested Settings
                  _buildSectionHeader('More'),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.notifications,
                    title: 'Notification Settings',
                    onTap: () => _navigateToNotifications(),
                  ),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.security,
                    title: 'Privacy & Security',
                    onTap: () => OverlayService.showPrivacyPolicy(),
                  ),
                  SettingTile(
                    icon: Icons.storage,
                    title: 'Storage Usage',
                    onTap: () => _navigateToStorage(),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Logout Button
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                    child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),
                      label: const Text(
                        'Logout',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                      ),
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                      ),
                      onPressed: () => OverlayService.showLogoutConfirmation(
                        onConfirm: () {
                          SideNavigationService.navigateToLogin();
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                ]),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(String title) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 20, 16, 8),
      child: Text(
        title,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  IconData _getThemeIcon(String themeMode) {
    switch (themeMode) {
      case 'Light':
        return Icons.light_mode;
      case 'Dark':
        return Icons.dark_mode;
      case 'System':
        return Icons.phone_iphone;
      default:
        return Icons.light_mode;
    }
  }

  void _showChangePasswordDialog() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => const ChangePasswordDialog(),
    );
  }

  void _changeLanguage() {
    DialogHelper.showLanguageBottomSheet(context, _languageService);
  }

  void _changeTheme() {
    DialogHelper.showThemeBottomSheet(context, _themeService);
  }

  void _rateApp() {
    // Open app store link
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Redirect to App Store')),
    );
  }

  void _navigateToNotifications() {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Navigate to Notification Settings')),
    );
  }

  void _navigateToStorage() {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Navigate to Storage Usage')),
    );
  }
}

class ShopDetailsPage extends StatefulWidget {

class _ShopDetailsPageState extends State<ShopDetailsPage> {
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = User(
      id: 1,
      email: 'customer@example.com',
      role: 'customer',
      name: 'John Doe',
    );

      children: [

          ),

            controller: _scrollController,

              // Expanded GlassSliverAppBar with gradient and dynamic size

                title: '$shopName',
                showBackButton: true,
                textColor: Colors.white,

                delegate: SliverChildListDelegate(
                  [

                        imageCount: 6,

                      ),
                    ),

                    // Shop Information Section
                    ShopInfoSection(shopData: widget.shopData),

                    HorizontalBarberScroll(
                      barbers: [
                        BarberData(image: "assets/mojies/image11.png", available: true, name: "John"),
                        BarberData(image: "assets/mojies/image19.png", available: false, name: "Jane"),
                        BarberData(image: "assets/mojies/image6.png", available: true, name: "Jane"),
                        BarberData(image: "assets/mojies/image8.png", available: true, name: "Jane"),
                        BarberData(image: "assets/mojies/image65.png", available: true, name: "Jane"),
                      ],
                      avatarSize: 150,
                      spacing: 12,
                    ),

                    // Services Section
                    ProductSection(shopData: widget.shopData),

                    // Services Section
                    ServicesSection(shopData: widget.shopData),

                    // Reviews Section
                    ReviewsSection(shopData: widget.shopData),

                    // Subscription Section
                    Container(
                      constraints: BoxConstraints(
                        // maxHeight: 600, // Adjust this value as needed

                          // Handle successful subscription
                          print('Subscription successful!');
                        },
                      ),

                    // Inventory Section
                    SizedBox(
                      height: 600,
                      child: InventoryManager(currentUser: user),
                    ),


                    SizedBox(height: 32),

                  ],
                ),

}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/branch_model.dart';
import '../../Models/inventory_item.dart';
import '../../Models/offer.dart';
import '../../Models/profit_data.dart';
import '../../Models/subscription.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../Widgets/subscription_card.dart';
import '../Widgets/inventory_alert_card.dart';
import '../Widgets/profit_graph.dart';
import '../Widgets/offers_card.dart';
import '../Widgets/branch_card.dart';
import 'shop_details.dart';
import 'branch_management.dart';

class ShopOwnerDashboard extends StatefulWidget {
  const ShopOwnerDashboard({super.key});

  @override
  ShopOwnerDashboardState createState() => ShopOwnerDashboardState();
}

class ShopOwnerDashboardState extends State<ShopOwnerDashboard> {
  late Future<Subscription> _subscriptionFuture;
  late Future<List<InventoryItem>> _inventoryFuture;
  late Future<ProfitReport> _profitReportFuture;
  late Future<List<Offer>> _offersFuture;
  late Future<List<Branch>> _branchesFuture;
  late Map<String, dynamic> shopData;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
    shopData = {};
  }

  // Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _fetchShopData() async {
  //   // Your logic to fetch shop data
  //   return {
  //     'shopName': 'My Shop',
  //     'location': 'City Center',
  //     'totalProducts': 150,
  //   };
  // }
  void _loadData() {
    _subscriptionFuture = ApiService.getSubscriptionDetails();
    _inventoryFuture = ApiService.getLowStockItems();
    _profitReportFuture = ApiService.getProfitReport();
    _offersFuture = ApiService.getCurrentOffers();
    _branchesFuture = ApiService.getBranches();
  }

  void _showUpgradePopup() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('Upgrade Subscription'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: Icon(Icons.star, color: AppColors.accentColor),
              title: Text('Enterprise Plan'),
              subtitle: Text('\$199.99/month'),
              trailing: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () {},
                child: Text('Upgrade'),
              ),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: Icon(Icons.workspace_premium, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
              title: Text('Premium Plan'),
              subtitle: Text('\$149.99/month'),
              trailing: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: () {},
                child: Text('Upgrade'),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: Text('Cancel'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showCreateOfferPopup() {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('Create New Offer'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextField(
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Offer Title'),
              ),
              TextField(
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Description'),
              ),
              TextField(
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Discount Percentage'),
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: TextField(
                      decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Start Date'),
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(width: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: TextField(
                      decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'End Date'),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text('Offer created successfully!'),
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.successColor,
                ),
              );
            },
            child: Text('Create Offer'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
          AppStrings.dashboard,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: AppColors.textColor,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          ),
        ),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.cardColor,
        elevation: 0,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(Icons.refresh, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
            onPressed: _loadData,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: () async => _loadData(),
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              FutureBuilder<Subscription>(
                future: _subscriptionFuture,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return _buildLoadingCard();
                  }
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return _buildErrorCard('Failed to load subscription');
                  }
                  return SubscriptionCard(
                    subscription: snapshot.data!,
                    onUpgrade: _showUpgradePopup,
                  );
                },
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
              FutureBuilder<List<InventoryItem>>(
                future: _inventoryFuture,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return _buildLoadingCard();
                  }
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return _buildErrorCard('Failed to load inventory');
                  }
                  return InventoryAlertCard(
                    lowStockItems: snapshot.data!,
                    onViewAll: () => Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => ShopDetailsPage(shopData: shopData)),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
              FutureBuilder<ProfitReport>(
                future: _profitReportFuture,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return _buildLoadingCard();
                  }
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return _buildErrorCard('Failed to load profit data');
                  }
                  return ProfitGraph(profitReport: snapshot.data!);
                },
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
              FutureBuilder<List<Offer>>(
                future: _offersFuture,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return _buildLoadingCard();
                  }
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return _buildErrorCard('Failed to load offers');
                  }
                  return OffersCard(
                    offers: snapshot.data!,
                    onCreateOffer: _showCreateOfferPopup,
                  );
                },
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
              FutureBuilder<List<Branch>>(
                future: _branchesFuture,
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return _buildLoadingCard();
                  }
                  if (snapshot.hasError) {
                    return _buildErrorCard('Failed to load branches');
                  }
                  return BranchCard(
                    branches: snapshot.data!,
                    onViewAll: () => Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => BranchManagementPage()),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLoadingCard() {
    return Card(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
        child: Center(
          child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.primaryColor),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildErrorCard(String message) {
    return Card(
      color: AppColors.errorColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.error, color: AppColors.errorColor),
            SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(child: Text(message)),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: _loadData,
              child: Text('Retry'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import '../../Theme/sphere_neomorphic.dart';

import '../Widgets/date_time_pickers.dart';
import '../Widgets/service_details_section.dart';


class ShopSchedulePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final ShopService shopService;

  const ShopSchedulePage({
    super.key,
    required this.shopService,
  });

  @override
  ShopSchedulePageState createState() => ShopSchedulePageState();
}

class ShopSchedulePageState extends State<ShopSchedulePage> {
  DateTime? _selectedDate;
  TimeOfDay? _selectedTime;
  late ServiceSelection _serviceSelection;

  bool _homeActive = false;
  bool _settingsActive = false;
  bool _profileActive = false;
  bool _infoActive = false;
  bool _searchActive = false;

  final List<ShopService> _availableAdditionalServices = [
    ShopService(name: 'Hair Cream', price: '\$8', duration: '10 min'),
    ShopService(name: 'Face Scrub', price: '\$12', duration: '15 min'),
    ShopService(name: 'Hair Wash', price: '\$10', duration: '15 min'),
    ShopService(name: 'Beard Styling', price: '\$8', duration: '10 min'),
    ShopService(name: 'Head Massage', price: '\$15', duration: '20 min'),
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _serviceSelection = ServiceSelection(mainService: widget.shopService);
  }

  void _selectDate(DateTime date) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedDate = date;
      _selectedTime = null; // Reset time when date changes
    });
  }

  void _selectTime(TimeOfDay time) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedTime = time;
    });
  }

  void _toggleService(ShopService service) {
    setState(() {
      if (_serviceSelection.containsService(service)) {
        _serviceSelection = _serviceSelection.removeService(service);
      } else {
        _serviceSelection = _serviceSelection.addService(service);
      }
      // Reset date and time when services change
      _selectedDate = null;
      _selectedTime = null;
    });
  }

  // void _confirmSchedule() {
  //   if (_selectedDate == null || _selectedTime == null) {
  //     ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
  //       SnackBar(
  //         content: Text('Please select both date and time'),
  //         backgroundColor: Colors.red,
  //       ),
  //     );
  //     return;
  //   }
  //
  //   final scheduleSlot = ShopScheduleSlot(
  //     date: _selectedDate!,
  //     time: _selectedTime!,
  //     shopService: widget.shopService,
  //   );
  //
  //   ScheduleHelper.bookSchedule(scheduleSlot).then((_) {
  //     ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
  //       SnackBar(
  //         content: Text('Schedule booked successfully!'),
  //         backgroundColor: Colors.green,
  //       ),
  //     );
  //     Navigator.pop(context);
  //   });
  // }

  void _showScheduleTypeDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext context) {
        return Dialog(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          child: Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Color(0xffffffff),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(
                color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                width: 1.0,
              ),
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                // Header
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Color(0xffffffff),
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
                      topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
                      topRight: Radius.circular(16),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.calendar_today, color: Color(0xffc25858)),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'Schedule Type',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Color(0xffc25858),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // Options
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildScheduleOption(
                        context,
                        title: 'One Time Only',
                        subtitle: 'Schedule this appointment once',
                        icon: Icons.event,
                        onTap: () {
                          Navigator.of(context).pop();
                          _confirmSchedule('one_time');
                          Provider.of<PointsProvider>(context, listen: false)
                            .addPoints(PointsType.makeAppointment);
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _buildScheduleOption(
                        context,
                        title: 'Bi-Weekly',
                        subtitle: 'Repeat every 2 weeks',
                        icon: Icons.repeat,
                        onTap: () {
                          Navigator.of(context).pop();
                          _confirmSchedule('bi_weekly');
                          Provider.of<PointsProvider>(context, listen: false)
                            .addPoints(PointsType.makeAppointment);
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      _buildScheduleOption(
                        context,
                        title: 'Monthly',
                        subtitle: 'Repeat every month',
                        icon: Icons.calendar_month,
                        onTap: () {
                          Navigator.of(context).pop();
                          _confirmSchedule('monthly');
                          Provider.of<PointsProvider>(context, listen: false)
                            .addPoints(PointsType.makeAppointment);
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // Close Button
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: Color(0xffc25858),
                        width: 1.0,
                      ),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    ),
                    child: TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      child: Text(
                        'Cancel',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Color(0xffc25858),
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildScheduleOption(BuildContext context, {
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
    required IconData icon,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
  }) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Color(0x26c25858),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(color: Color(0xffc25858)),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: Color(0xffc25858), size: 24),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    title,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Color(0xff040000),
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    subtitle,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Color(0xff040000).withOpacity(0.7),
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            // Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Color(0xffc25858)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _confirmSchedule(String scheduleType) {
    if (_selectedDate == null || _selectedTime == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Please select both date and time'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    // Your schedule confirmation logic here with scheduleType
    print('Schedule confirmed: $scheduleType for ${_serviceSelection.allServices.length} services');

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text('$scheduleType schedule booked successfully!'),
        backgroundColor: Colors.green,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FadedBackground(
      startColor: Color(0xff806C99),
      endColor: const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        body: CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            GlassSliverAppBar(
              title: 'Book a haircut',
              // backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
            ),
            SliverList(
              delegate: SliverChildListDelegate(
                [
                  Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        // Service Information
                        ServiceDetailsSection(
                          serviceSelection: _serviceSelection,
                          onServiceToggled: _toggleService,
                          availableAdditionalServices: _availableAdditionalServices,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 24),

                        // Date Picker
                        ScheduleDatePicker(
                          selectedDate: _selectedDate,
                          onDateSelected: _selectDate,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 16),

                        // Time Picker
                        ScheduleTimePicker(
                          selectedDate: _selectedDate,
                          selectedTime: _selectedTime,
                          onTimeSelected: _selectTime,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 32),

                        // Confirm Button
                        Center(
                          child: ElevatedButton(
                            onPressed: () => _showScheduleTypeDialog(context),
                            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                              backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 40, vertical: 16),
                            ),
                            child: Text(
                              'Confirm Schedule',
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        bottomNavigationBar: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
          child: Container(
            height: 90,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xFFC25858),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                  blurRadius: 10,
                  offset: const Offset(5, 5),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
              children: [
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/home_front.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/home',
                  isActive: _homeActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Home sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = true;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = true;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/flash.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/settings',
                  isActive: _settingsActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Settings sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = true;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = true;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/fire.png',
                  size: 80,
                  navigationRoute: '/profile',
                  isActive: _profileActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    print('Profile sphere tapped');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = true;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = true;
                        _infoActive = false;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/clock.png',
                  size: 80,
                  isActive: _infoActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/appointments');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = true;
                      _searchActive = false;
                    });
                  },
                  onActiveChanged: (active) {
                    if (active) {
                      setState(() {
                        _homeActive = false;
                        _settingsActive = false;
                        _profileActive = false;
                        _infoActive = true;
                        _searchActive = false;
                      });
                    }
                  },
                ),
                NeomorphicSphere(
                  activeIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male_view.png',
                  inactiveIconPath: 'assets/icons/avatar_male.png',
                  size: 80,
                  isActive: _searchActive,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/profile');
                    setState(() {
                      _homeActive = false;
                      _settingsActive = false;
                      _profileActive = false;
                      _infoActive = false;
                      _searchActive = true;
                    });
                  },
                )
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:na3eeman/Packages/Navigations/reports_page.dart';

import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../components/common/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'barber_performance_page.dart';
import 'barbers_list_page.dart';
import 'branches_page.dart';

class StaffManagementPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const StaffManagementPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.backgroundColor,
      appBar: CustomAppBar(title: AppStrings.staffManagement),
      body: GridView.count(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        crossAxisCount: 2,
        crossAxisSpacing: 16,
        mainAxisSpacing: 16,
        children: [
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Barbers',
            Icons.content_cut,
            AppColors.primaryColor,
            () => _navigateToPage(context, const BarbersListPage()),
          ),
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Performance',
            Icons.assessment,
            AppColors.secondaryColor,
            () => _navigateToPage(context, const BarberPerformancePage()),
          ),
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Reports',
            Icons.description,
            AppColors.accentColor,
            () => _navigateToPage(context, const ReportsPage()),
          ),
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Branches',
            Icons.store,
            AppColors.warningColor,
            () => _navigateToPage(context, const BranchesPage()),
          ),
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Schedule',
            Icons.calendar_today,
            Colors.blue,
            () => _showComingSoon(context),
          ),
          _buildFeatureCard(
            context,
            'Payroll',
            Icons.payment,
            Colors.green,
            () => _showComingSoon(context),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFeatureCard(
    BuildContext context,
    String title,
    IconData icon,
    Color color,
    VoidCallback onTap,
  ) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 30),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _navigateToPage(BuildContext context, Widget page) {
    Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => page));
  }

  void _showComingSoon(BuildContext context) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Feature coming soon!')),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class FeedbackDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const FeedbackDialog({super.key});

  @override
  State<FeedbackDialog> createState() => _FeedbackDialogState();
}

class _FeedbackDialogState extends State<FeedbackDialog> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _subjectController = TextEditingController();
  final _messageController = TextEditingController();
  int _rating = 5;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Send Feedback'),
      content: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextFormField(
                controller: _subjectController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Subject',
                  hintText: 'Brief description of your feedback',
                ),
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please enter a subject';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _messageController,
                maxLines: 5,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Your Feedback',
                  hintText: 'Please share your thoughts about the app...',
                  alignLabelWithHint: true,
                ),
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please enter your feedback';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  const Text('Rating:'),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: List.generate(5, (index) {
                      return IconButton(
                        icon: Icon(
                          index < _rating ? Icons.star : Icons.star_border,
                          color: Colors.amber,
                          size: 30,
                        ),
                        onPressed: () {
                          setState(() {
                            _rating = index + 1;
                          });
                        },
                      );
                    }),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: _submitFeedback,
          child: const Text('Send Feedback'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _submitFeedback() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      // Implement feedback submission logic here
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Thank you for your feedback!')),
      );
      Navigator.pop(context);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _subjectController.dispose();
    _messageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ReportProblemDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReportProblemDialog({super.key});

  @override
  State<ReportProblemDialog> createState() => _ReportProblemDialogState();
}

class _ReportProblemDialogState extends State<ReportProblemDialog> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _subjectController = TextEditingController();
  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();
  String _problemType = 'bug';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Report a Problem'),
      content: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                initialValue: _problemType,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Problem Type',
                ),
                items: const [
                  DropdownMenuItem(
                    value: 'bug',
                    child: Text('Bug Report'),
                  ),
                  DropdownMenuItem(
                    value: 'abuse',
                    child: Text('Usage Abuse'),
                  ),
                  DropdownMenuItem(
                    value: 'feature',
                    child: Text('Feature Request'),
                  ),
                  DropdownMenuItem(
                    value: 'other',
                    child: Text('Other'),
                  ),
                ],
                onChanged: (value) {
                  setState(() {
                    _problemType = value!;
                  });
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _subjectController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Subject',
                  hintText: 'Brief description of the problem',
                ),
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please enter a subject';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _descriptionController,
                maxLines: 5,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Description',
                  hintText: 'Please describe the problem in detail...',
                  alignLabelWithHint: true,
                ),
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please describe the problem';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: _submitReport,
          child: const Text('Report Problem'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _submitReport() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      // Implement problem report submission logic here
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Problem reported successfully')),
      );
      Navigator.pop(context);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _subjectController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/profile_model.dart';

class ProfileHelperFunctions {
  static void initializeControllers({
    required UserProfile? userProfile,
    required Map<String, TextEditingController> controllers,
  }) {
    if (userProfile == null) return;

    controllers['firstName']!.text = userProfile.firstName ?? '';
    controllers['lastName']!.text = userProfile.lastName ?? '';
    controllers['username']!.text = userProfile.username ?? '';
    controllers['email']!.text = userProfile.email ?? '';
    controllers['phone']!.text = userProfile.phone ?? '';
    controllers['bio']!.text = userProfile.bio ?? '';
    controllers['location']!.text = userProfile.location ?? '';
    controllers['specialties']!.text = userProfile.specialties?.join(', ') ?? '';
  }

  static UserProfile updateProfileFromControllers({
    required UserProfile userProfile,
    required Map<String, TextEditingController> controllers,
  }) {
    return userProfile.copyWith(
      firstName: controllers['firstName']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['firstName']!.text,
      lastName: controllers['lastName']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['lastName']!.text,
      username: controllers['username']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['username']!.text,
      email: controllers['email']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['email']!.text,
      phone: controllers['phone']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['phone']!.text,
      bio: controllers['bio']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['bio']!.text,
      location: controllers['location']!.text.isEmpty ? null : controllers['location']!.text,
      specialties: controllers['specialties']!.text.isEmpty
          ? null
          : controllers['specialties']!.text.split(',').map((e) => e.trim()).toList(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  static Future<void> saveToBackend({
    required UserProfile profile,
    required BuildContext context,
    required VoidCallback onError,
  }) async {
    try {
      // Implement your actual API call here
      print('Saving profile to API: $profile');

      // Simulate API call
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));

      // Replace with your actual API call:
      // await ApiService.updateProfile(profile);

      print('Profile saved successfully');
    } catch (e) {
      print('Error saving profile: $e');
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Failed to save profile: $e')),
      );
      onError();
    }
  }

  static void showDeleteConfirmationDialog({
    required BuildContext context,
    required VoidCallback onConfirm,
  }) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Delete Profile'),
        content: const Text('Are you sure you want to delete your profile? This action cannot be undone.'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              onConfirm();
            },
            child: const Text('Delete', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Future<void> performDelete({
    required BuildContext context,
    required VoidCallback onSuccess,
  }) async {
    try {
      // Implement your delete API call here
      print('Deleting profile...');

      // Simulate API call
      await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));

      // Replace with your actual API call:
      // await ApiService.deleteProfile();

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Profile deleted')),
      );

      onSuccess();
    } catch (e) {
      print('Error deleting profile: $e');
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Failed to delete profile: $e')),
      );
    }
  }

  static String getAccountType(UserProfile? userProfile) {
    if (userProfile == null) return 'User';
    if (userProfile.isOwner == true) return 'Owner';
    if (userProfile.isWorker == true) return 'Worker';
    return 'User';
  }

  static void showImagePickerDialog({required BuildContext context}) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Change Profile Picture'),
        content: const Text('Choose an option'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              _pickImageFromGallery();
            },
            child: const Text('Gallery'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              _takePhoto();
            },
            child: const Text('Camera'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static void _pickImageFromGallery() {
    // Implement gallery image picker
  }

  static void _takePhoto() {
    // Implement camera functionality
  }
}


import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/profile_model.dart';

class ProfileWidgets {
  static Widget buildLoadingState() {
    return const Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          CircularProgressIndicator(),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text('Loading profile...'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget buildProfileContent({
    required GlobalKey<FormState> formKey,
    required UserProfile userProfile,
    required bool isEditing,
    required Map<String, TextEditingController> controllers,
    required String Function() getAccountType,
    required VoidCallback onDeleteProfile,
    required VoidCallback onChangeProfilePicture,
  }) {
    return Form(
      key: formKey,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Profile Picture
          _buildProfilePicture(
            userProfile: userProfile,
            isEditing: isEditing,
            onChangeProfilePicture: onChangeProfilePicture,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          _buildProfileFieldsContainer(
            userProfile: userProfile,
            isEditing: isEditing,
            controllers: controllers,
            getAccountType: getAccountType,
            onDeleteProfile: onDeleteProfile,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildProfilePicture({
    required UserProfile userProfile,
    required bool isEditing,
    required VoidCallback onChangeProfilePicture,
  }) {
    return Center(
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          CircleAvatar(
            radius: 70,
            backgroundImage: userProfile.profilePic != null
                ? NetworkImage(userProfile.profilePic!)
                : const AssetImage('assets/mojies/image11.png') as ImageProvider,
          ),
          if (isEditing)
            Positioned(
              bottom: 0,
              right: 0,
              child: Container(
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.blue,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                  onPressed: onChangeProfilePicture,
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildProfileFieldsContainer({
    required UserProfile userProfile,
    required bool isEditing,
    required Map<String, TextEditingController> controllers,
    required String Function() getAccountType,
    required VoidCallback onDeleteProfile,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Editable Fields
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'First Name',
            value: userProfile.firstName ?? '',
            controller: controllers['firstName']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Last Name',
            value: userProfile.lastName ?? '',
            controller: controllers['lastName']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Username',
            value: userProfile.username ?? '',
            controller: controllers['username']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
            isRequired: true,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Email',
            value: userProfile.email ?? '',
            controller: controllers['email']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            isRequired: true,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Phone',
            value: userProfile.phone ?? '',
            controller: controllers['phone']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Bio',
            value: userProfile.bio ?? '',
            controller: controllers['bio']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Location',
            value: userProfile.location ?? '',
            controller: controllers['location']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
          ),
          _buildEditableField(
            label: 'Specialties',
            value: userProfile.specialties?.join(', ') ?? '',
            controller: controllers['specialties']!,
            isEditing: isEditing,
          ),

          // Read-only Fields
          _buildReadOnlyField('Role', userProfile.role),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Gender', userProfile.gender),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Email Verified', userProfile.vrfidEmail == true ? 'Yes' : 'No'),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Phone Verified', userProfile.vrfidPhone == true ? 'Yes' : 'No'),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Account Type', getAccountType()),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Subscription', userProfile.isSubscribed == true ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'),
          _buildReadOnlyField('Member Since', userProfile.createdAt?.toLocal().toString().split(' ')[0]),

          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Action Buttons
          if (!isEditing)
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: onDeleteProfile,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              ),
              child: const Text('Delete Profile'),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildEditableField({
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required bool isEditing,
    bool isRequired = false,
    TextInputType keyboardType = TextInputType.text,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              fontSize: 18,
              color: Color(0xbfffffff),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          isEditing
              ? TextFormField(
                  controller: controller,
                  keyboardType: keyboardType,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                    hintText: 'Enter $label',
                  ),
                  validator: (value) {
                    if (isRequired && (value == null || value.isEmpty)) {
                      return '$label is required';
                    }
                    return null;
                  },
                )
              : Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x80ffffff)),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    value.isEmpty ? 'Not set' : value,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: value.isEmpty ? Colors.grey : Colors.black,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget _buildReadOnlyField(String label, dynamic value) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0x80ffffff),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: Text(
              value?.toString() ?? 'Not set',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: value == null ? Colors.grey : Colors.black,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}



import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class Splash extends StatelessWidget {
  const Splash({super.key});
  static const String id = "splash";

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return const Scaffold(
      body: Column(),
    );
  }
}import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AddItemDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const AddItemDialog({Key? key}) : super(key: key);

  @override
  _AddItemDialogState createState() => _AddItemDialogState();
}

class _AddItemDialogState extends State<AddItemDialog> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _countController = TextEditingController(text: '0');
  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _countController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Add New Item'),
      content: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextFormField(
                controller: _nameController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Item Name',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                ),
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please enter item name';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _countController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Initial Count',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                ),
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                validator: (value) {
                  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                    return 'Please enter initial count';
                  }
                  final count = int.tryParse(value);
                  if (count == null || count < 0) {
                    return 'Please enter a valid number';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _descriptionController,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Description (Optional)',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                ),
                maxLines: 2,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () {
            if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
              final result = {
                'name': _nameController.text,
                'count': int.parse(_countController.text),
                'description': _descriptionController.text.isEmpty
                    ? null
                    : _descriptionController.text,
              };
              Navigator.of(context).pop(result);
            }
          },
          child: const Text('Add Item'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppointmentsList extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<dynamic> appointments;

  const AppointmentsList({super.key, required this.appointments});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (appointments.isEmpty) {
      return SliverToBoxAdapter(
        child: Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          child: const Center(
            child: Text(
              'No upcoming appointments',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.grey,
                fontSize: 16,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return SliverList(
      delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
        (context, index) {
          final appointment = appointments[index];
          return Container(
            margin: EdgeInsets.all(15),
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(15),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Color(0x66FFFFFF),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
              border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Color(0x33DFA8A4),
                  blurRadius: 4,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            // margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
            // decoration: BoxDecoration(
            //   color: Color(0x40ffffff),
            //   borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            // ),
            child: ListTile(
              leading: CircleAvatar(
                backgroundColor: Colors.blue[100],
                child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.blue),
              ),
              title: Text(
                appointment['customerName'] ?? 'Customer',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
              subtitle: Text(
                '${appointment['service'] ?? 'Haircut'} • ${appointment['time'] ?? 'Unknown time'}',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.grey[600],
                ),
              ),
              trailing: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: _getStatusColor(appointment['status']).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  appointment['status'] ?? 'Scheduled',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: _getStatusColor(appointment['status']),
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
        childCount: appointments.length,
      ),
    );
  }

  Color _getStatusColor(String? status) {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'confirmed':
        return Colors.green;
      case 'pending':
        return Colors.orange;
      case 'cancelled':
        return Colors.red;
      default:
        return Colors.blue;
    }
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cached_network_image/cached_network_image.dart';
import '../../Models/appointment_model.dart';

class AppointmentAccordion extends StatefulWidget {
  final ShopAppointment appointment;

  const AppointmentAccordion({super.key, required this.appointment});

  @override
  State<AppointmentAccordion> createState() => _AppointmentAccordionState();
}

class _AppointmentAccordionState extends State<AppointmentAccordion> {
  bool _isExpanded = false;

  Color _getStatusColor(String status) {
    switch (status.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'completed':
        return Colors.green;
      case 'pending':
        return Colors.orange;
      case 'cancelled':
        return Colors.red;
      default:
        return Colors.grey;
    }
  }

  IconData _getStatusIcon(String status) {
    switch (status.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'completed':
        return Icons.check_circle;
      case 'pending':
        return Icons.pending;
      case 'cancelled':
        return Icons.cancel;
      default:
        return Icons.help;
    }
  }

  Widget _buildStatusChip(String status) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        border: Border.all(
          color: _getStatusColor(status).withValues(alpha: 0.3),
          width: 1,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(
            _getStatusIcon(status),
            color: _getStatusColor(status),
            size: 16,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Text(
            status.toUpperCase(),
            style: TextStyle(
              color: _getStatusColor(status),
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      color: Color(0x00ffffff),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
      elevation: 2,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0x33FFFFFF),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
        ),
        child: Theme(
          data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(
            dividerColor: Colors.transparent,
            cardColor: Colors.transparent,
          ),
          child: ExpansionTile(
            collapsedBackgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
            backgroundColor:  Colors.transparent,
            key: Key(widget.appointment.id),
            initiallyExpanded: _isExpanded,
            onExpansionChanged: (expanded) {
              setState(() {
                _isExpanded = expanded;

              });
            },
            leading: CircleAvatar(
              radius: 36,
              backgroundColor: Color(0x40ffffff),
              backgroundImage: CachedNetworkImageProvider(
                widget.appointment.imagePath,
              ),
            ),

            title: Text(
              widget.appointment.title,
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                fontSize: 20,
              ),
            ),
            subtitle: _buildStatusChip(widget.appointment.status),
            trailing: Icon(
              _isExpanded ? Icons.expand_less : Icons.expand_more,
              color: Colors.grey[600],
            ),
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Color(0x33ffffff),
                  borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
                    bottomLeft: Radius.circular(12),
                    bottomRight: Radius.circular(12),
                  ),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.attach_money,
                      title: 'Total Price',
                      value: '\$${widget.appointment.totalPrice.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.access_time,
                      title: 'Service Time',
                      value: widget.appointment.serviceTime,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.calendar_today,
                      title: 'Date',
                      value: widget.appointment.date,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.person,
                      title: 'Barber',
                      value: widget.appointment.barberName,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.phone,
                      title: 'Barber Number',
                      value: widget.appointment.barberNumber,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _buildDetailRow(
                      icon: Icons.store,
                      title: 'Barber Shop',
                      value: widget.appointment.barberShopName,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDetailRow({
    required IconData icon,
    required String title,
    required String value,
  }) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(
          icon,
          color: Color(0x80ffffff),
          size: 20,
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          flex: 2,
          child: Text(
            title,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Color(0xffffffff),
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Expanded(
          flex: 3,
          child: Text(
            value,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.normal,
            ),
            textAlign: TextAlign.right,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../Models/barber.dart';


class BarberCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Barber barber;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final VoidCallback? onEdit;
  final VoidCallback? onDelete;

  const BarberCard({
    super.key,
    required this.barber,
    this.onTap,
    this.onEdit,
    this.onDelete,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 2,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 16),
      child: ListTile(
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        leading: CircleAvatar(
          radius: 25,
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          child: barber.profileImage != null
              ? ClipOval(
                  child: Image.network(barber.profileImage!),
                )
              : Text(
                  barber.name[0],
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.primaryColor,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
        ),
        title: Text(
          barber.name,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            fontSize: 16,
          ),
        ),
        subtitle: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.star, color: AppColors.warningColor, size: 16),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  barber.rating.toString(),
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text(
              '${barber.totalAppointments} appointments',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
          onSelected: (value) {
            switch (value) {
              case 'edit':
                onEdit?.call();
                break;
              case 'delete':
                onDelete?.call();
                break;
            }
          },
          itemBuilder: (BuildContext context) => [
            const PopupMenuItem(value: 'edit', child: Text('Edit')),
            const PopupMenuItem(value: 'delete', child: Text('Delete')),
          ],
        ),
        onTap: onTap,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class BarberPerformanceChart extends StatelessWidget {

      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Padding(

            const Text(
              'Appointments Performance',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 18,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            SizedBox(
              height: 200,
              child: ListView.builder(
                scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                itemCount: barbers.length,

                    width: 80,
