                            color: AppColors.primaryColor,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Text(
                          barber.name.split(' ')[0],
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/branch_model.dart';

class BranchesOverview extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Branch> branches;

  const BranchesOverview({super.key, required this.branches});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Shop Branches',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ...branches.map((branch) => _BranchCard(branch: branch)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/branch_model.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../Utils/formatters.dart';

class BranchCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Branch> branches;
  final VoidCallback onViewAll;

  const BranchCard({
    super.key,
    required this.branches,
    required this.onViewAll,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      color: AppColors.cardColor,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Branch Management',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                Chip(
                  label: Text('${branches.length} Branches'),
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  labelStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.primaryColor),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            ...branches.take(2).map((branch) => ListTile(
              leading: Container(
                width: 40,
                height: 40,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: branch.isOpen 
                    ? AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2)
                    : AppColors.errorColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  Icons.store,
                  color: branch.isOpen 
                    ? AppColors.successColor
                    : AppColors.errorColor,
                ),
              ),
              title: Text(branch.name),
              subtitle: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(branch.address),
                  SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        Icons.circle,
                        size: 8,
                        color: branch.isOpen 
                          ? AppColors.successColor
                          : AppColors.errorColor,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        branch.isOpen ? 'Open' : 'Closed',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: branch.isOpen 
                            ? AppColors.successColor
                            : AppColors.errorColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (branch.hasLowStockAlerts) ...[
                        SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Icon(Icons.warning, size: 14, color: AppColors.warningColor),
                        SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text('${branch.lowStockItems} low stock'),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              trailing: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    Formatters.formatCurrency(branch.getMonthlyRevenue),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: AppColors.successColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    '${branch.getTotalSales} sales',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            )),
            if (branches.length > 2)
              TextButton(
                onPressed: onViewAll,
                child: Text('View all ${branches.length} branches'),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class BranchCard2 extends StatelessWidget {

  final VoidCallback? onAssignManager;

  const BranchCard2({
    super.key,
    required this.branch,
    this.onTap,
    this.onAssignManager,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 2,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 16),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  branch.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                Chip(
                  label: Text(
                    branch.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive',
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                  backgroundColor: branch.isActive ? AppColors.successColor : AppColors.errorColor,
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              branch.address,
              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              children: [
                _buildInfoItem(Icons.people, '${branch.totalStaff} staff'),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                _buildInfoItem(Icons.attach_money, Formatters.formatCurrency(branch.safeMonthlyRevenue)),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            if (onAssignManager != null)
              Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                child: OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: onAssignManager,
                  child: const Text('Assign Manager'),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInfoItem(IconData icon, String text) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Icon(icon, size: 16, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        const SizedBox(width: 4),
        Text(text, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class CartItemWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final CartItem item;
  final VoidCallback onIncrease;
  final VoidCallback onDecrease;
  final VoidCallback onRemove;

  const CartItemWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.item,
    required this.onIncrease,
    required this.onDecrease,
    required this.onRemove,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 16),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            // Product Image
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              child: Image.network(
                item.imageUrl,
                width: 80,
                height: 80,
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 16),

            // Product Details and Quantity Controls
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    item.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    CartHelpers.formatPrice(item.price),
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: Colors.green,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),

                  // Quantity Controls
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      // Decrease Button
                      IconButton(
                        onPressed: onDecrease,
                        icon: Container(
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.grey[200],
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.remove, size: 16),
                        ),
                        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                      ),

                      // Quantity Display
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                        child: Text(
                          item.quantity.toString(),
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),

                      // Increase Button
                      IconButton(
                        onPressed: onIncrease,
                        icon: Container(
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.grey[200],
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 16),
                        ),
                        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                      ),

                      const Spacer(),

                      // Total Price
                      Text(
                        CartHelpers.formatPrice(item.totalPrice),
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),

            // Remove Button
            IconButton(
              onPressed: onRemove,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.red),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'dart:ui';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/user_model.dart';
import '../Navigations/navigation_config.dart';
import 'neomorphic_sphere.dart';

class CustomNavigationBar extends StatefulWidget {
  final User user;
  final String currentRoute;

  const CustomNavigationBar({
    super.key,
    required this.user,
    required this.currentRoute,
  });

  @override
  _CustomNavigationBarState createState() => _CustomNavigationBarState();
}

class _CustomNavigationBarState extends State<CustomNavigationBar> {
  String? _lastTappedId;
  bool _shouldAnimate = true;

  void _handleSphereTap(String itemId, String route) {
    setState(() {
      _lastTappedId = itemId;
      _shouldAnimate = true;
    });

    // Navigate to the route if we're not already there
    if (ModalRoute.of(context)?.settings.name != route) {
      Navigator.pushNamed(context, route);
    }
  }

  Widget _buildFrostedContainer(Widget child) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        color: const Color(0x80806C99),
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        child: BackdropFilter(
          filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 2.0, sigmaY: 2.0),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final navItems = NavigationConfig.getNavItems(widget.user, widget.currentRoute);

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20.0, 0.0, 20.0, 20.0),
      child: _buildFrostedContainer(
        Container(
          height: 90,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: const Color(0x4dC25858),
                blurRadius: 10,
                offset: const Offset(5, 5),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
            children: navItems.map((item) {
              final isActive = NavigationConfig.isItemActive(item, widget.currentRoute, widget.user);
              final shouldAnimateForThisItem = _shouldAnimate && _lastTappedId == item.id;

              return NeomorphicSphere(
                activeIconPath: item.activeIconPath,
                inactiveIconPath: item.inactiveIconPath,
                size: 80,
                isActive: isActive,
                shouldAnimate: shouldAnimateForThisItem,
                onTap: () => _handleSphereTap(item.id, item.route),
              );
            }).toList(),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Theme/profile_avatar_style.dart';


class MyProfileAvatar extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? imagePath;
  final double size;
  final String? title;
  final MyProfileAvatarStyle? style;

  const MyProfileAvatar({
    super.key,
    this.imagePath,
    required this.size,
    this.title,
    this.style,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        // Avatar Circle
        Container(
          width: size,
          height: size,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: style?.color ?? Colors.grey.shade300,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(size / 2),
            border: style?.border,
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: const Color(0x6699806C),
                blurRadius: 10,
                offset: const Offset(3, 3),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: ClipOval(
            child: _buildAvatarContent(),
          ),
        ),

        // Title (if provided)
        if (title != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            title!,
            style: style?.titleStyle ?? const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            ),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAvatarContent() {
    if (imagePath != null) {
      // Network image
      if (imagePath!.startsWith('http')) {
        return Image.network(
          imagePath!,
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
          width: size,
          height: size,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => _buildFallback(),
        );
      }
      // Asset image
      else {
        return Image.asset(
          imagePath!,
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
          width: size,
          height: size,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => _buildFallback(),
        );
      }
    }

    return _buildFallback();
  }

  Widget _buildFallback() {
    return Container(
      color: style?.color ?? Colors.blue,
      child: Icon(
        Icons.person,
        size: size * 0.6,
        color: Colors.white,
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class CustomSwitchTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final bool value;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onChanged;

  const CustomSwitchTile({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.value,
    required this.onChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(
        icon,
        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
      ),
      title: Text(title),
      subtitle: Text(subtitle),
      trailing: Switch(
        value: value,
        onChanged: onChanged,
        activeThumbColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
      ),
      onTap: () => onChanged(!value),
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Services/schedule_helper.dart';

class ScheduleDatePicker extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime? selectedDate;
  final Function(DateTime) onDateSelected;
  final Color? backgroundColor;
  final Color? selectedColor;
  final Color? availableColor;
  final Color? unavailableColor;
  final Color? textColor;
  final Color? headerColor;

  const ScheduleDatePicker({
    super.key,
    required this.selectedDate,
    required this.onDateSelected,
    this.backgroundColor,
    this.selectedColor,
    this.availableColor,
    this.unavailableColor,
    this.textColor,
    this.headerColor,
  });

  @override
  State<ScheduleDatePicker> createState() => _ScheduleDatePickerState();
}

class _ScheduleDatePickerState extends State<ScheduleDatePicker> {
  late DateTime _currentMonth;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _currentMonth = widget.selectedDate ?? DateTime.now();
  }

  void _navigateToPreviousMonth() {
    setState(() {
      _currentMonth = DateTime(_currentMonth.year, _currentMonth.month - 1, 1);
    });
  }

  void _navigateToNextMonth() {
    setState(() {
      _currentMonth = DateTime(_currentMonth.year, _currentMonth.month + 1, 1);
    });
  }

  void _selectDate(DateTime date) {
    if (ScheduleHelper.isDateAvailable(date)) {
      widget.onDateSelected(date);
    }
  }

  String _getMonthName(int month) {
    const months = [
      'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
      'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'
    ];
    return months[month - 1];
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final firstDay = DateTime(_currentMonth.year, _currentMonth.month, 1);
    final firstWeekday = firstDay.weekday;
    final daysFromPreviousMonth = firstWeekday - 1;
    final totalDays = 42; // 6 weeks

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final containerWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
        final calendarWidth = containerWidth * 0.95; // 95% of container width
        final dayCellSize = (calendarWidth - 32) / 7; // Subtract padding and divide by 7 days

        return Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Color(0x66ffffff), // Background color
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), // Rounded corners
            border: Border.all(
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF), // Border color
              width: 1.0, // Border width
            ),
          ),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Select Date',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 24,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xff040000),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),

                // Calendar container
                Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: widget.backgroundColor ?? Color(0x33C25858),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(color: Color(0xffffffff)),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      // Month header and navigation
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(
                              Icons.chevron_left,
                              color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xffc25858),
                            ),
                            onPressed: _navigateToPreviousMonth,
                          ),
                          Text(
                            '${_getMonthName(_currentMonth.month)} ${_currentMonth.year}',
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 16,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xffc25858),
                            ),
                          ),
                          IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(
                              Icons.chevron_right,
                              color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xffc25858),
                            ),
                            onPressed: _navigateToNextMonth,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Weekday headers
                      Row(
                        children: ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun']
                            .map((day) => Expanded(
                                  child: Text(
                                    day,
                                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      color: widget.textColor ?? Colors.grey[700],
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ))
                            .toList(),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),

                      // Calendar grid
                      GridView.builder(
                        shrinkWrap: true,
                        physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                        gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                          crossAxisCount: 7,
                          childAspectRatio: 1.0, // Changed to 1.0 for square cells
                          mainAxisSpacing: 4, // Reduced spacing
                          crossAxisSpacing: 4, // Reduced spacing
                        ),
                        itemCount: totalDays,
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                          final dayOffset = index - daysFromPreviousMonth;
                          final currentDate = DateTime(firstDay.year, firstDay.month, 1 + dayOffset);

                          final bool isCurrentMonth = currentDate.month == firstDay.month;
                          final bool isAvailable = isCurrentMonth && ScheduleHelper.isDateAvailable(currentDate);
                          final bool isSelected = widget.selectedDate != null &&
                              currentDate.year == widget.selectedDate!.year &&
                              currentDate.month == widget.selectedDate!.month &&
                              currentDate.day == widget.selectedDate!.day;
                          final bool isToday = currentDate.year == now.year &&
                              currentDate.month == now.month &&
                              currentDate.day == now.day;

                          return SizedBox(
                            width: dayCellSize, // Dynamic width
                            height: dayCellSize, // Dynamic height for square cells
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              onTap: isAvailable ? () => _selectDate(currentDate) : null,
                              child: Container(
                                margin: const EdgeInsets.all(2), // Reduced margin
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: isSelected
                                      ? (widget.selectedColor ?? Color(0xffc25858))
                                      : isToday
                                          ? (widget.availableColor ?? Color(0x26c25858))
                                          : Colors.transparent,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(dayCellSize / 2), // Circular based on size
                                  border: isToday
                                      ? Border.all(color: widget.availableColor ?? Color(0xffc25858))
                                      : null,
                                ),
                                child: Center(
                                  child: Text(
                                    currentDate.day.toString(),
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: !isCurrentMonth
                                          ? (widget.unavailableColor ?? Color(0xffffffff))
                                          : !isAvailable
                                              ? (widget.unavailableColor ?? Color(0xffc25858))
                                              : isSelected
                                                  ? Colors.white
                                                  : (widget.textColor ?? Colors.black),
                                      fontWeight: isSelected || isToday ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                                      fontSize: dayCellSize * 0.3, // Dynamic font size
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class ScheduleTimePicker extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime? selectedDate;
  final TimeOfDay? selectedTime;
  final Function(TimeOfDay) onTimeSelected;
  final Color? backgroundColor;
  final Color? textColor;
  final Color? headerColor;
  final Color? timeSlotColor;
  final Color? selectedTimeColor;
  final int totalDurationMinutes;

  const ScheduleTimePicker({
    super.key,
    required this.selectedDate,
    required this.selectedTime,
    required this.onTimeSelected,
    this.backgroundColor,
    this.textColor,
    this.headerColor,
    this.timeSlotColor,
    this.selectedTimeColor,
    this.totalDurationMinutes = 30,

  });

  @override
  State<ScheduleTimePicker> createState() => _ScheduleTimePickerState();
}

class _ScheduleTimePickerState extends State<ScheduleTimePicker> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final availableTimes = widget.selectedDate != null
        ? ScheduleHelper.getAvailableTimesForDate(widget.selectedDate!)
        : [];

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(
          color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
          width: 1.0,
        ),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Available Times',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 24,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xff040000),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            if (widget.selectedDate == null) ...[
              // Show message when no date is selected
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Color(0x4dffffff),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: Color(0xffffffff)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      Icons.access_time,
                      color: widget.headerColor ?? Color(0xffc25858),
                      size: 20,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Text(
                      'Select a date first to see available times',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: widget.textColor ?? Colors.black87,
                        fontSize: 16,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ]
            else ...[
              // Show available time slots in a dynamic grid
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: widget.backgroundColor ?? Color(0x33C25858),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: Color(0xffffffff)),
                ),
                child: LayoutBuilder(
                  builder: (context, constraints) {
                    final screenWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
                    final itemWidth = (screenWidth * 0.95) / 3; // 95% width divided by 3 columns

                    return Wrap(
                      spacing: 8, // Horizontal spacing between items
                      runSpacing: 10, // Vertical spacing between lines
                      alignment: WrapAlignment.start,
                      children: availableTimes.map((time) {
                        final isSelected = widget.selectedTime != null &&
                            widget.selectedTime!.hour == time.hour &&
                            widget.selectedTime!.minute == time.minute;

                        return SizedBox(
                          width: itemWidth, // Dynamic width based on screen
                          child: GestureDetector(
                            onTap: () => widget.onTimeSelected(time),
                            child: Container(
                              height: 40, // Fixed height for each time slot
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isSelected
                                    ? (widget.selectedTimeColor ?? Color(0x40ffffff))
                                    : (widget.timeSlotColor ?? Color(0x26c25858)),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                                border: Border.all(
                                  color: isSelected
                                      ? Color(0xffffffff)
                                      : Color(0xffc25858),
                                  width: 1,
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Center(
                                child: Text(
                                  time.format(context),
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: isSelected ? Color(0xffc25858) : Color(0xffffffff),
                                    fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              )
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class EarningsChart extends StatelessWidget {
  final dynamic earningsData;

  const EarningsChart({super.key, required this.earningsData});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Handle List data (which is what your API returns)
    if (earningsData is List) {
      final List<dynamic> earningsList = earningsData as List;
      return _buildWeeklyChart(earningsList);
    } else {
      return _buildEmptyChart();
    }
  }

  Widget _buildWeeklyChart(List<dynamic> earningsList) {
    if (earningsList.isEmpty) {
      return _buildEmptyChart();
    }

    // Process the data
    final List<Map<String, dynamic>> chartData = [];
    double maxAmount = 0;

    for (var item in earningsList) {
      if (item is Map<String, dynamic>) {
        final amount = (item['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
        chartData.add(item);
        if (amount > maxAmount) maxAmount = amount;
      }
    }

    if (chartData.isEmpty) {
      return _buildEmptyChart();
    }

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const Text(
            "Weekly Earnings Trend",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          SizedBox(
            height: 200,
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
              children: chartData.map((day) {
                final amount = (day['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble();
                final date = DateTime.parse(day['date'] ?? DateTime.now().toIso8601String());

                return Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        '\$${amount.toInt()}',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Container(
                          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.6),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                          ),
                          height: maxAmount > 0 ? (amount / maxAmount) * 150 : 0,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        _getDayAbbreviation(date),
                        style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              }).toList(),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          _buildStats(chartData),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStats(List<Map<String, dynamic>> chartData) {
    final totalEarnings = chartData.map((e) => (e['amount'] ?? 0).toDouble()).reduce((a, b) => a + b);
    final totalAppointments = chartData.map((e) => (e['appointments'] ?? 0) as int).reduce((a, b) => a + b);
    final averagePerAppointment = totalAppointments > 0 ? totalEarnings / totalAppointments : 0;

    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
      children: [
        _StatItem(
          label: 'Total',
          value: '\$${totalEarnings.toStringAsFixed(0)}',
          color: Colors.green,
        ),
        _StatItem(
          label: 'Appointments',
          value: totalAppointments.toString(),
          color: Colors.blue,
        ),
        _StatItem(
          label: 'Average',
          value: '\$${averagePerAppointment.toStringAsFixed(0)}',
          color: Colors.orange,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEmptyChart() {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: const Center(
        child: Text(
          'No earnings data available',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            color: Colors.grey,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _getDayAbbreviation(DateTime date) {
    final days = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
    return days[date.weekday % 7];
  }
}

class _StatItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Color color;

  const _StatItem({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(
          value,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: color,
          ),
        ),
        Text(
          label,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: Colors.grey,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/inventory_item.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../Utils/formatters.dart';

class InventoryAlertCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<InventoryItem> lowStockItems;
  final VoidCallback onViewAll;

  const InventoryAlertCard({
    super.key,
    required this.lowStockItems,
    required this.onViewAll,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      color: AppColors.cardColor,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Inventory Alerts',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                Badge(
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.errorColor,
                  label: Text(lowStockItems.length.toString()),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            if (lowStockItems.isEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20.0),
                child: Text(
                  'No low stock items',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: AppColors.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                  ),
                ),
              )
            else
              ...lowStockItems.take(3).map((item) => ListTile(
                leading: Container(
                  width: 40,
                  height: 40,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(Icons.inventory_2, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
                ),
                title: Text(item.name),
                subtitle: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Stock: ${item.currentStock} (Min: ${item.minStockLevel ?? 3})',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: item.isCriticalStock 
                          ? AppColors.errorColor
                          : AppColors.warningColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (item.category != null)
                      Text(
                        'Category: ${item.category}',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                trailing: item.price != null ? Text(
                  Formatters.formatCurrency(item.price!),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.primaryColor,
                  ),
                ) : null,
              )),
            if (lowStockItems.length > 3)
              TextButton(
                onPressed: onViewAll,
                child: Text('View all ${lowStockItems.length} items'),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Helpers/inventory_helpers.dart';
import '../../Models/inventory_item.dart';

class InventoryListItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final InventoryItem item;
  final VoidCallback onIncreaseCount;
  final VoidCallback? onDelete;

  const InventoryListItem({
    super.key,
    required this.item,
    required this.onIncreaseCount,
    this.onDelete,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0x4dffffff),
        border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x80ffffff)),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: ListTile(
        leading: CircleAvatar(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          child: Text(
            item.count.toString(),
            style: TextStyle(
              color: item.isLowStock ? Colors.orange : Colors.green,
              fontSize: 24,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        title: Text(
          item.name,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            color: item.isLowStock ? Colors.orange[800] : null,
          ),
        ),
        subtitle: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              '${InventoryHelpers.getStockStatusMessage(item.count)} ${InventoryHelpers.getStockEmoji(item.count)}',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: item.isLowStock ? Colors.orange[700] : Colors.grey[600],
                fontSize: 12,
              ),
            ),
            if (item.description != null)
              Text(
                item.description!,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              ),
          ],
        ),
        trailing: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            IconButton(
              icon: Image.asset('assets/icons/plus.png', width: 24, height: 24),
              onPressed: onIncreaseCount,
              tooltip: 'Increase count',
            ),
            if (onDelete != null)
              IconButton(
                icon: Image.asset('assets/icons/trash.png', width: 24, height: 24),
                onPressed: onDelete,
                tooltip: 'Delete item',
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/inventory_item.dart';
import '../../Models/user_model.dart';
import '../../Services/inventory_service.dart';
import 'inventory_list_item.dart';
import 'low_stock_alert.dart';
import 'add_item_dialog.dart';

class InventoryManager extends StatefulWidget {
  final User currentUser;

  const InventoryManager({super.key, required this.currentUser});

  @override
  InventoryManagerState createState() => InventoryManagerState();
}

class InventoryManagerState extends State<InventoryManager> {
  final InventoryService _inventoryService = InventoryService();
  late Future<List<InventoryItem>> _inventoryFuture;
  List<InventoryItem> _inventoryItems = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadInventory();
  }

  void _loadInventory() {
    setState(() {
      _inventoryFuture = _inventoryService.getInventoryItems();
    });
  }

  Future<void> _increaseItemCount(InventoryItem item) async {
    try {
      final updatedItem = await _inventoryService.updateItemCount(
        item.id,
        item.count + 1
      );

      setState(() {
        final index = _inventoryItems.indexWhere((i) => i.id == item.id);
        if (index != -1) {
          _inventoryItems[index] = updatedItem;
        }
      });

      // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Increased ${item.name} count to ${updatedItem.count}'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Failed to update ${item.name}'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _addNewItem() async {
    final result = await showDialog<Map<String, dynamic>>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => const AddItemDialog(),
    );

    if (result != null) {
      try {
        final newItem = await _inventoryService.addItem(
          result['name']!,
          initialCount: result['count'],
          description: result['description'],
        );

        setState(() {
          _inventoryItems.add(newItem);
        });

        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Added ${newItem.name} to inventory'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.green,
          ),
        );
      } catch (e) {
        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Failed to add item: $e'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.red,
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteItem(InventoryItem item) async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Delete Item'),
        content: Text('Are you sure you want to delete ${item.name}?'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
            child: const Text('Delete', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirmed == true) {
      try {
        await _inventoryService.deleteItem(item.id);
        setState(() {
          _inventoryItems.removeWhere((i) => i.id == item.id);
        });

        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Deleted ${item.name} from inventory'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.green,
          ),
        );
      } catch (e) {
        // ignore: use_build_context_synchronously
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Failed to delete ${item.name}'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.red,
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (!widget.currentUser.isCustomer) {
      return const Center(
        child: Text(
          'Inventory management is only available for owners',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, color: Colors.grey),
        ),
      );
    }


    return Stack(
      children: [
        Center(
          child: Container(
            width: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.90,
            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0x4dffffff),
              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x80ffffff)),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: FutureBuilder<List<InventoryItem>>(
              future: _inventoryFuture,
              builder: (context, snapshot) {
                if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                  return const SizedBox(
                    height: 200, // Fixed height for loading state
                    child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  );
                }

                if (snapshot.hasError) {
                  return SizedBox(
                    height: 300, // Fixed height for error state
                    child: Center(
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          Image.asset('assets/icons/error_icon.png', width: 64, height: 64),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          Text(
                            'Failed to load inventory',
                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                            child: Text(
                              snapshot.error.toString(),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                                    color: Colors.grey,
                                  ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          ElevatedButton(
                            onPressed: _loadInventory,
                            child: const Text('Retry'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }

                _inventoryItems = snapshot.data ?? [];

                return Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    // Header with title and refresh
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: const Color(0x80ffffff))),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            'Inventory Management',
                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                          ),
                          IconButton(
                            icon: Image.asset('assets/icons/moon.png', width: 24, height: 24),
                            onPressed: _loadInventory,
                            tooltip: 'Refresh Inventory',
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),

                    LowStockAlert(items: _inventoryItems),

                    // Use Flexible instead of ConstrainedBox for better layout control
                    if (_inventoryItems.isNotEmpty)
                      Flexible(
                        child: ListView.builder(
                          shrinkWrap: true,
                          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                          itemCount: _inventoryItems.length,
                          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                            final item = _inventoryItems[index];
                            return InventoryListItem(
                              item: item,
                              onIncreaseCount: () => _increaseItemCount(item),
                              onDelete: () => _deleteItem(item),
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                      )
                    else
                      SizedBox(
                        height: 200, // Fixed height for empty state
                        child: Center(
                          child: Column(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                            children: [
                              Image.asset('assets/icons/inventory_icon.png', width: 64, height: 64),
                              const SizedBox(height: 16),
                              Text(
                                'No items in inventory',
                                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: Colors.grey),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned(
          bottom: 20,
          right: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.10, // Adjust based on your container width
          child: Container(
            width: 56,
            height: 56,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [Color(0xFF806C99), Color(0x80DFA8A4)],
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              ),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              // boxShadow: [
              //   BoxShadow(
              //     color: Color(0x80000000),
              //     blurRadius: 4,
              //     offset: const Offset(0, 2),
              //   ),
              // ],
            ),
            child: IconButton(
              onPressed: _addNewItem,
              icon: Image.asset('assets/icons/plus.png', width: 40, height: 40),
              style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Helpers/inventory_helpers.dart';
import '../../Models/inventory_item.dart';

class LowStockAlert extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<InventoryItem> items;

  const LowStockAlert({super.key, required this.items});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final lowStockItems = items.where((item) => item.isLowStock).toList();

    if (lowStockItems.isEmpty) {
      return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }

    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.orange[100],
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.orange),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.orange),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              const Text(
                'Low Stock Alert',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.orange,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            InventoryHelpers.formatLowStockAlert(lowStockItems),
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.orange[800]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(
            '${lowStockItems.length} item${lowStockItems.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} need${lowStockItems.length > 1 ? '' : 's'} restocking',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: Colors.orange[700],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class NeomorphicSphere extends StatefulWidget {
  final String activeIconPath;
  final String inactiveIconPath;
  final double size;
  final Color sphereColor;
  final Color shadowColor;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final double iconSize;
  final bool isActive;
  final bool shouldAnimate;

  const NeomorphicSphere({
    super.key,
    required this.activeIconPath,
    required this.inactiveIconPath,
    this.size = 70,
    this.sphereColor = const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
    this.shadowColor = const Color(0x80242424),
    required this.onTap,
    this.iconSize = 30,
    required this.isActive,
    this.shouldAnimate = false,
  });

  @override
  _NeomorphicSphereState createState() => _NeomorphicSphereState();
}

class _NeomorphicSphereState extends State<NeomorphicSphere>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  bool _isPressed = false;
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> _scaleAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _animationController = AnimationController(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      vsync: this,
    );

    _scaleAnimation = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0.0,
      end: 1.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeOutBack,
    ));

    if (widget.isActive) {
      _animationController.forward();
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(NeomorphicSphere oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);

    if (widget.shouldAnimate) {
      if (widget.isActive && !oldWidget.isActive) {
        _animationController.forward();
      } else if (!widget.isActive && oldWidget.isActive) {
        _animationController.reverse();
      }
    } else {
      if (widget.isActive) {
        _animationController.value = 1.0;
      } else {
        _animationController.value = 0.0;
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isActive = widget.isActive;
    final currentIconPath = isActive ? widget.activeIconPath : widget.inactiveIconPath;
    final currentIconSize = isActive ? widget.iconSize + 10 : widget.iconSize;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = true),
      onTapUp: (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTapCancel: () => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTap: widget.onTap,
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          // Neomorphic Sphere (only visible when active)
          if (isActive)
            AnimatedBuilder(
              animation: _animationController,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return Transform.scale(
                  scale: _scaleAnimation.value,
                  child: child,
                );
              },
              child: Container(
                width: widget.size,
                height: widget.size,
                decoration: ShapeDecoration(
                  color: widget.sphereColor,
                  shape: const OvalBorder(),
                  shadows: _isPressed
                      ? [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: widget.shadowColor.withOpacity(0.2),
                            blurRadius: 5,
                            offset: const Offset(2, 2),
                            spreadRadius: -2,
                          ),
                        ]
                      : [
                          BoxShadow(
                            color: widget.shadowColor,
                            blurRadius: 15,
                            offset: const Offset(4, 4),
                            spreadRadius: 1,
                          ),
                        ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

          // Icon (always visible)
          Image.asset(
            currentIconPath,
            width: currentIconSize,
            height: currentIconSize,
            fit: BoxFit.contain,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/offer.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';

class OffersCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Offer> offers;
  final VoidCallback onCreateOffer;

  const OffersCard({
    super.key,
    required this.offers,
    required this.onCreateOffer,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      color: AppColors.cardColor,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Current Offers & Ads',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: onCreateOffer,
                  icon: Icon(Icons.add),
                  label: Text('Create'),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.secondaryColor,
                    foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            if (offers.isEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20.0),
                child: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    'No active offers',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              )
            else
              ...offers.map((offer) => ListTile(
                leading: Container(
                  width: 50,
                  height: 50,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.secondaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(25),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(
                    Icons.local_offer,
                    color: AppColors.secondaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
                title: Text(offer.title),
                subtitle: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(offer.description),
                    SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      '${offer.daysRemaining} days remaining',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: offer.daysRemaining < 3
                          ? AppColors.errorColor
                          : AppColors.successColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                trailing: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      Formatters.formatPercent(offer.discountPercentage / 100),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 18,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: AppColors.secondaryColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              )),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../../Models/payment_method.dart';
import 'cliq_payment_page.dart'; // Add this import
import 'visa_payment_page.dart'; // Add this import

class PaymentMethodService {
  static Future<PaymentMethod?> showPaymentMethodDialog(BuildContext context) {
    return showDialog<PaymentMethod>(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext context) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Select Payment Method'),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: PaymentMethod.values.map((method) {
              return ListTile(
                leading: Icon(method.icon),
                title: Text(method.displayName),
                onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(method),
              );
            }).toList(),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  static void handlePaymentSelection({
    required BuildContext context,
    required PaymentMethod method,
    required String scheduleType,
    required Function(String, String) onPaymentComplete,
  }) {
    switch (method) {
      case PaymentMethod.cash:
        _showCashConfirmation(context, scheduleType, onPaymentComplete);
        break;
      case PaymentMethod.cliq:
        _navigateToCliqPayment(context, scheduleType, onPaymentComplete);
        break;
      case PaymentMethod.visa:
        _navigateToVisaPayment(context, scheduleType, onPaymentComplete);
        break;
    }
  }

  static void _showCashConfirmation(
    BuildContext context,
    String scheduleType,
    Function(String, String) onPaymentComplete,
  ) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext context) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Cash Payment'),
          content: const Text('Please bring cash at the time of your appointment.'),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
              child: const Text('Cancel'),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                Navigator.of(context).pop();
                onPaymentComplete(scheduleType, 'cash');
              },
              child: const Text('Confirm'),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  static void _navigateToCliqPayment(
    BuildContext context,
    String scheduleType,
    Function(String, String) onPaymentComplete,
  ) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (context) => CliqPaymentPage(
          scheduleType: scheduleType,
          onPaymentComplete: () => onPaymentComplete(scheduleType, 'cliq'),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  static void _navigateToVisaPayment(
    BuildContext context,
    String scheduleType,
    Function(String, String) onPaymentComplete,
  ) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (context) => VisaPaymentPage(
          scheduleType: scheduleType,
          onPaymentComplete: () => onPaymentComplete(scheduleType, 'visa'),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'payment_methods.dart';

class ReceiptPopup {
  static void show({
    required BuildContext context,
    required String scheduleType,
    required String selectedDate,
    required String selectedTime,
    required double amount,
    required Function(String, String) onPaymentComplete,
  }) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext context) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Appointment Confirmation'),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text('Schedule Type: ${_getScheduleTypeText(scheduleType)}'),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('Date: $selectedDate'),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text('Time: $selectedTime'),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Text('Total Amount: \$${amount.toStringAsFixed(2)}'),
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
              child: const Text('Cancel'),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () async {
                Navigator.of(context).pop();
                final paymentMethod = await PaymentMethodService.showPaymentMethodDialog(context);

                if (paymentMethod != null) {
                  PaymentMethodService.handlePaymentSelection(
                    context: context,
                    method: paymentMethod,
                    scheduleType: scheduleType,
                    onPaymentComplete: onPaymentComplete,
                  );
                }
              },
              child: const Text('Confirm & Pay'),
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  static String _getScheduleTypeText(String type) {
    switch (type) {
      case 'one_time':
        return 'One Time Only';
      case 'bi_weekly':
        return 'Bi-Weekly';
      case 'monthly':
        return 'Monthly';
      default:
        return 'One Time Only';
    }
  }
}

          children: [

              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/pending_confirmation_model.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';

class PendingConfirmations extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<PendingConfirmation> pendingConfirmations;

  const PendingConfirmations({super.key, required this.pendingConfirmations});

  @override
  State<PendingConfirmations> createState() => _PendingConfirmationsState();
}

class _PendingConfirmationsState extends State<PendingConfirmations> {
  void _confirmPayment(int appointmentId) async {
    try {
      await ApiService.confirmCashPayment(appointmentId);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('Payment confirmed successfully')),
        );
        setState(() {});
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('Error confirming payment: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _showConfirmationDialog(PendingConfirmation confirmation) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Confirm Cash Payment'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('Customer: ${confirmation.customerName}'),
            Text('Service: ${confirmation.service}'),
            Text('Amount: \$${confirmation.amount.toStringAsFixed(2)}'),
            if (confirmation.notes != null) Text('Notes: ${confirmation.notes}'),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              _confirmPayment(confirmation.id);
            },
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green),
            child: const Text('Confirm Payment'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (widget.pendingConfirmations.isEmpty) {
      return Container();
    }

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Pending Cash Confirmations',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.orange,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ...widget.pendingConfirmations.map(
            (confirmation) => _ConfirmationCard(
              confirmation: confirmation,
              onConfirm: () => _showConfirmationDialog(confirmation),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ConfirmationCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final PendingConfirmation confirmation;
  final VoidCallback onConfirm;

  const _ConfirmationCard({
    required this.confirmation,
    required this.onConfirm,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.orange[50],
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.orange[300]!),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  confirmation.customerName,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    fontSize: 16,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  confirmation.service,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.grey[700],
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  'Time: ${TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(confirmation.appointmentTime).format(context)}',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.grey[600],
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                if (confirmation.notes != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    'Notes: ${confirmation.notes}',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.grey[600],
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Column(
            children: [
              Text(
                '\$${confirmation.amount.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  fontSize: 16,
                  color: Colors.green,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: onConfirm,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: Colors.green,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
                ),
                child: const Text('Confirm'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
