class PointsTrackerWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  const PointsTrackerWidget({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<PointsProvider>(
      builder: (context, pointsProvider, child) {
        final availableActions = PointsType.values
            .where((type) => pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(type))
            .toList();

        return Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment.topCenter,
              end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              colors: [
                Color(0x99FFFFFF),
                Color(0x4DFFFFFF),
              ],
            ),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            // boxShadow: [
            //   BoxShadow(
            //     color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha:0.15),
            //     blurRadius: 12,
            //     offset: const Offset(0, 4),
            //   ),
            // ],
            border: Border.all(
              color: Color(0x80ffffff),
              width: 1,
            ),
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Header with points and progress
              _buildHeader(context, pointsProvider),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Available actions
              if (availableActions.isNotEmpty) ...[
                _buildAvailableActions(context, pointsProvider, availableActions),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ],

              // Quick actions
              _buildQuickActions(context, pointsProvider),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader(BuildContext context, PointsProvider pointsProvider) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: [
        Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Rewards Points',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: Color(0xFF000000),
                    fontSize: 20,
                  ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              'Earn points and get rewards!',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Color(0x80000000),
                fontSize: 12,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              colors: [Color(0x66c25858), Color(0xCCC25858)],
            ),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(25),
            border: Border.all(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
            // boxShadow: [
            //   BoxShadow(
            //     color: Color(0x80806C99),
            //     blurRadius: 8,
            //     offset: const Offset(0, 2),
            //   ),
            // ],
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.workspace_premium,
                color: Colors.white,
                size: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text(
                '${pointsProvider.points.totalPoints} pts',
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAvailableActions(BuildContext context, PointsProvider pointsProvider, List<PointsType> availableActions) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Text(
              'Available Rewards',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: Colors.grey.shade800,

                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.green.shade100,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  '${availableActions.length} available',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.green.shade800,
                    fontSize: 10,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showAvailableActionsPopup(BuildContext context, PointsProvider pointsProvider, List<PointsType> availableActions) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => Dialog(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        child: Container(
          width: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.9,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.2),
                blurRadius: 20,
                offset: const Offset(0, 10),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'Available Rewards',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.blue.shade900,
                        ),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                    icon: Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
                    padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    constraints: const BoxConstraints(),
                    iconSize: 20,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                'Complete these actions to earn points:',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                  fontSize: 14,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ...availableActions.map((type) => _buildPopupActionCard(context, pointsProvider, type)),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.blue.shade600,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                  ),
                  child: const Text(
                    'Got it!',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPopupActionCard(BuildContext context, PointsProvider pointsProvider, PointsType type) {
    final pointsValue = PointsService.getPointsValue(type);
    final description = PointsService.pointsDescriptions[type]!;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.grey.shade50,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(
          color: Colors.grey.shade200,
          width: 1,
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 40,
            height: 40,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionColor(type).withOpacity(0.1),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionIcon(type),
              color: PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionColor(type),
              size: 22,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  description,
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    color: Colors.black87,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionSubtitle(type),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [
                  PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionColor(type),
                  PointsTrackerHelpers.getActionColor(type).withOpacity(0.8),
                ],
              ),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
            ),
            child: Text(
              '+$pointsValue',
              style: const TextStyle(
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildQuickActions(BuildContext context, PointsProvider pointsProvider) {
    final hasQuickActions = pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.rateApp) ||
                           pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.appFeedback);
    if (!hasQuickActions) return const SizedBox();
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Wrap(
          spacing: 8,
          runSpacing: 8,
          children: [
            if (pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.rateApp))
              PointsTrackerHelpers.buildQuickActionChip(
                context,
                'Rate App',
                Icons.star_rate_rounded,
                Colors.orange,
                () => PointsTrackerDialogs.showRateAppDialog(context, pointsProvider),
              ),
            if (pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.appFeedback))
              PointsTrackerHelpers.buildQuickActionChip(
                context,
                'Feedback',
                Icons.feedback_rounded,
                Colors.purple,
                () => PointsTrackerDialogs.showAppFeedbackDialog(context, pointsProvider),
              ),
            if (pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.appFeedback))
              PointsTrackerHelpers.buildQuickActionChip(
                context,
                'Invite a friend',
                Icons.feedback_rounded,
                Colors.green,
                () => PointsTrackerDialogs.inviteFriend(context, pointsProvider),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ProductSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> shopData;

  const ProductSection({
    super.key,
    required this.shopData,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Use shop-specific services if available, otherwise use default
    final products = shopData['products'] as List<Map<String, String>>? ?? [
      {'name': 'Haircut', 'price': '\$25', 'made': 'CK'},
      {'name': 'Beard Trim', 'price': '\$15', 'made': 'Tommy'},
      {'name': 'Haircut & Beard', 'price': '\$35', 'made': 'Elegance'},
      {'name': 'Hair Wash', 'price': '\$10', 'made': 'Home Made'},
    ];

    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x33FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Products & Prices',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...products.map((service) => _buildServiceItem(
            name: service['name'] as String,
            price: service['price'] as String,
            made: service['made'] as String,
          )).toList(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // main_file.dart
 // Import the animated button

  Widget _buildServiceItem({
    required String name,
    required String price,
    required String made,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  name,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  made,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(width: 10),
          Text(
            price,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(width: 10),
          CustomAnimatedIcon(
            icon: Icons.add_shopping_cart,
            onTap: () {
              print('Added $name to cart');
            },
            backgroundColor: Color(0x804CAF50),
            size: 20,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';

import '../../Models/profit_data.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';
import '../../Utils/formatters.dart';


class ProfitGraph extends StatefulWidget {
  final ProfitReport profitReport;

  const ProfitGraph({super.key, required this.profitReport});

  @override
  ProfitGraphState createState() => ProfitGraphState();
}

class ProfitGraphState extends State<ProfitGraph> {
  bool _isMonthly = true;
  late List<ProfitData> _currentData;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _currentData = widget.profitReport.monthlyData;
  }

  void _togglePeriod() {
    setState(() {
      _isMonthly = !_isMonthly;
      _currentData = _isMonthly
          ? widget.profitReport.monthlyData
          : widget.profitReport.yearlyData;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      color: AppColors.cardColor,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Profit Analytics',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: _togglePeriod,
                      child: Text(
                        _isMonthly ? 'Monthly' : 'Yearly',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.primaryColor,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    PopupMenuButton<String>(
                      onSelected: (value) => _showDownloadDialog(context, value),
                      itemBuilder: (context) => [
                        PopupMenuItem(value: 'pdf', child: Text('Download PDF')),
                        PopupMenuItem(value: 'csv', child: Text('Download CSV')),
                      ],
                      icon: Icon(Icons.download, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              height: 200,
              child: LineChart(
                LineChartData(
                  gridData: FlGridData(show: false),
                  titlesData: FlTitlesData(show: false),
                  borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
                  lineBarsData: [
                    LineChartBarData(
                      spots: _currentData.asMap().entries.map((e) {
                        return FlSpot(
                          e.key.toDouble(),
                          e.value.profit,
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                      isCurved: true,
                      color: AppColors.primaryColor,
                      barWidth: 3,
                      belowBarData: BarAreaData(
                        show: true,
                        color: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 16),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
              children: [
                _buildStatItem('Total Revenue', widget.profitReport.totalRevenue),
                _buildStatItem('Total Profit', widget.profitReport.totalProfit),
                _buildStatItem('Total Expenses', widget.profitReport.totalExpenses),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatItem(String label, double value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(
          Formatters.formatCurrency(value),
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: AppColors.primaryColor,
          ),
        ),
        Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            color: AppColors.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _showDownloadDialog(BuildContext context, String format) {
    TextEditingController fileNameController = TextEditingController(
      text: 'profit_report_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
    );

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('Download Report'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Text('Choose file name:'),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextField(
              controller: fileNameController,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                labelText: 'File Name',
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              // Implement download logic
              Navigator.pop(context);
              _showDownloadSuccess(context);
            },
            child: Text('Download $format'.toUpperCase()),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showDownloadSuccess(BuildContext context) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text('Report downloaded successfully!'),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.successColor,
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart'; // Add this import
import '../../Providers/points_provider.dart'; // Make sure this path is correct
import '../../Models/points_model.dart'; // Make sure this path is correct

class RatingWithPointsWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> shopData;
  final double initialRating;
  final Function(double)? onRatingChanged;

  const RatingWithPointsWidget({
    Key? key,
    required this.shopData,
    this.initialRating = 0.0,
    this.onRatingChanged,
  }) : super(key: key);

  @override
  State<RatingWithPointsWidget> createState() => _RatingWithPointsWidgetState();
}

class _RatingWithPointsWidgetState extends State<RatingWithPointsWidget> {
  late double _userRating;
  bool _hasAwardedPoints = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _userRating = widget.initialRating;
  }

  void _handleRatingTap(int rating) {
    setState(() {
      _userRating = rating.toDouble();
    });

    // Call the callback if provided
    widget.onRatingChanged?.call(_userRating);

    // Award points only if they haven't been awarded yet and rating is valid
    if (!_hasAwardedPoints && rating > 0) {
      final pointsProvider = Provider.of<PointsProvider>(context, listen: false);

      if (pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.rateShop)) {
        pointsProvider.addPoints(
          PointsType.rateShop,
          shopName: widget.shopData['title'] as String? ?? 'Shop'
        );
        _hasAwardedPoints = true;

        // Show success message
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Thanks for rating ${widget.shopData['title']}! +15 points'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.green,
            duration: Duration(seconds: 2),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Consumer<PointsProvider>(
      builder: (context, pointsProvider, child) {
        final canEarnPoints = pointsProvider.canEarnPoints(PointsType.rateShop) && !_hasAwardedPoints;

        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Rating: ',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                ...List.generate(5, (index) {
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => _handleRatingTap(index + 1),
                    child: Icon(
                      index < _userRating ? Icons.star : Icons.star_border,
                      color: Color(0xFFFFD700),
                      size: 24,
                    ),
                  );
                }),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  _userRating.toStringAsFixed(1),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
                if (canEarnPoints) ...[
                  SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.2),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.green),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      '+15 pts',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.green,
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),

            // Optional: Show message if points were already earned
            if (_hasAwardedPoints) ...[
              SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                'Points earned for rating this shop!',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Colors.green,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/report_model.dart';
import '../../Models/staff_models.dart';
import '../../Utils/constants.dart';

class ReportDownloadDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final StaffReportRequest initialRequest;

  const ReportDownloadDialog({super.key, required this.initialRequest});

  @override
  State<ReportDownloadDialog> createState() => _ReportDownloadDialogState();
}

class _ReportDownloadDialogState extends State<ReportDownloadDialog> {
  late StaffReportRequest _request;
  final _fileNameController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _request = widget.initialRequest;
    _fileNameController.text = _request.fileName;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Download Report'),
      content: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text('File Format: PDF', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _fileNameController,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'File Name',
                hintText: 'Enter file name',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
              onChanged: (value) {
                setState(() {
                  _request = _copyWith(fileName: value);
                });
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'Report Period: ${_getTimeDisplayName(_request.time)}',
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey),
            ),
            Text(
              'Date Range: ${_formatDate(_request.startDate)} - ${_formatDate(_request.endDate)}',
              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.grey, fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: const Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(_request),
          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
            backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
          ),
          child: const Text('Download', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  StaffReportRequest _copyWith({String? fileName}) {
    return StaffReportRequest(
      time: _request.time,
      startDate: _request.startDate,
      endDate: _request.endDate,
      branchId: _request.branchId,
      staffId: _request.staffId,
      fileName: fileName ?? _request.fileName,
    );
  }

  String _getTimeDisplayName(ReportTime time) {
    switch (time) {
      case ReportTime.daily:
        return 'Daily';
      case ReportTime.weekly:
        return 'Weekly';
      case ReportTime.monthly:
        return 'Monthly';
      case ReportTime.annually:
        return 'Annually';
    }
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return '${date.year}-${date.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${date.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class ReviewsSection extends StatefulWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> shopData;

  const ReviewsSection({
    Key? key,
    required this.shopData, // Add this parameter
  }) : super(key: key);


  @override
  ReviewsSectionState createState() => ReviewsSectionState();
}

class ReviewsSectionState extends State<ReviewsSection> {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> reviews = [
    {'name': 'John Doe', 'rating': 5.0, 'comment': 'Great service! Very professional.'},
    {'name': 'Jane Smith', 'rating': 4.5, 'comment': 'Good haircut, friendly staff.'},
    {'name': 'Mike Johnson', 'rating': 4.0, 'comment': 'Nice atmosphere, will come again.'},
  ];

  final TextEditingController _reviewController = TextEditingController();
  double _userRating = 5.0;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _reviewController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _addReview() {
    if (_reviewController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      reviews.insert(0, {
        'name': 'You', // You can replace this with actual user name
        'rating': _userRating,
        'comment': _reviewController.text.trim(),
      });
    });
      Provider.of<PointsProvider>(context, listen: false)
        .addPoints(PointsType.shopFeedback, shopName: 'Barber Shop Name');

    _reviewController.clear();
    _userRating = 5.0;

    // Optional: Hide keyboard after submission
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x33FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                'Customer Reviews',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                'See All',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFF2196F3),
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...reviews.map((review) => _buildReviewItem(
            name: review['name'] as String,
            rating: review['rating'] as double,
            comment: review['comment'] as String,
          )),

          // Add Review Section
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          Container(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Add Your Review',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 12),

                // Rating Section
               Row(
                children: [
                  Flexible(
                    child: RatingWithPointsWidget(
                      shopData: widget.shopData,
                      initialRating: _userRating,
                      onRatingChanged: (newRating) {
                        print('Rating changed to: $newRating');
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),

                SizedBox(height: 12),

                // Comment TextField
                TextField(
                  controller: _reviewController,
                  maxLines: 3,
                  style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: 'Write your review here...',
                    hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC)),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      borderSide: BorderSide(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
                    ),
                    focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      borderSide: BorderSide(color: Color(0xFF2196F3)),
                    ),
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
                  ),
                ),

                SizedBox(height: 12),

                // Submit Button
                Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _addReview,
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Color(0xFF2196F3),
                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Text('Submit Review'),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReviewItem({
    required String name,
    required double rating,
    required String comment,
  }) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                name,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.star, color: Color(0xFFFFD700), size: 16),
                  SizedBox(width: 4),
                  Text(
                    rating.toString(),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                      fontSize: 14,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            comment,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
              fontSize: 14,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/shop_service_model.dart';
import '../Navigations/shop_schedule_page.dart';

class ServicesSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<ShopService> services;

  const ServicesSection({
    super.key,
    required this.services,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x33FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Services & Prices',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...services.map((service) => _buildServiceItem(
            context: context,
            service: service,
          )),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildServiceItem({
    required BuildContext context,
    required ShopService service,
  }) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () {
        Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (context) => ShopSchedulePage(shopService: service),
          ),
        );
      },
      child: Container(
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  service.name,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  service.duration,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            Text(
              service.price,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                fontSize: 18,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/service_selection_model.dart';
import '../../Models/shop_service_model.dart';

class ServiceDetailsSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final ServiceSelection serviceSelection;
  final Function(ShopService) onServiceToggled;
  final List<ShopService> availableAdditionalServices;

  const ServiceDetailsSection({
    super.key,
    required this.serviceSelection,
    required this.onServiceToggled,
    this.availableAdditionalServices = const [],
  });

  void _showAddServiceDialog(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (BuildContext context) {
        return Dialog(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          child: Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0xffffffff),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(
                color: const Color(0xFFC25858),
                width: 1.0,
              ),
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                // Header
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    // color: const Color(0xffffffff),
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
                      topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
                      topRight: Radius.circular(16),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.add_circle, color: Color(0xffc25858)),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'Add Additional Services',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Color(0xffc25858),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                // Services List
                Container(
                  // color: Color(0x80806c99),
                  constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 400),
                  child: ListView.builder(
                    shrinkWrap: true,
                    itemCount: availableAdditionalServices.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final service = availableAdditionalServices[index];
                      final isSelected = serviceSelection.containsService(service);

                      return ListTile(
                        leading: Icon(
                          isSelected ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.radio_button_unchecked,
                          color: isSelected ? Color(0xffc25858) : Colors.grey,
                        ),
                        title: Text(
                          service.name,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xff040000),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                        subtitle: Text(
                          '${service.duration} • ${service.price}',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xff040000).withValues(alpha: 0.7),
                          ),
                        ),
                        trailing: Text(
                          service.price,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xffc25858),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontSize: 16,
                          ),
                        ),
                        onTap: () {
                          onServiceToggled(service);
                          Navigator.of(context).pop();
                        },
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),

                // Close Button
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 5, 20, 5),
                  child: Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: Color(0xffc25858), // Same color as the text
                        width: 1.0,
                      ),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(50),
                    ),
                    child: TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                      child: Text(
                        'Close',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Color(0xffc25858),
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final allServices = serviceSelection.allServices;

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0x4dffffff),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(
          color: const Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
          width: 1.0,
        ),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Service Details',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 24,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: Color(0xff040000),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Main Selected Service
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Color(0x26c25858),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                border: Border.all(color: Color(0xffc25858)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.celebration, color: Color(0xffc25858), size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Main Service',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: Color(0xffc25858),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          '${serviceSelection.mainService.name} - ${serviceSelection.mainService.price}',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xff040000),
                            fontSize: 16,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Selected additional services list
            if (serviceSelection.additionalServices.isNotEmpty) ...[
              Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'Additional Services:',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Color(0xff040000),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ...serviceSelection.additionalServices.map((service) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                    child: Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Color(0x1Ac25858),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Color(0xffc25858), size: 20),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              '${service.name} - ${service.price}',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: Color(0xff040000),
                                fontSize: 14,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(Icons.remove_circle, color: Color(0xffc25858), size: 20),
                            onPressed: () => onServiceToggled(service),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  )),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Divider(color: Color(0xffffffff)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                ],
              ),
            ],

            // Total summary
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Color(0x33C25858),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              ),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Total Duration:',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Color(0xff040000),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        ServiceCalculator.formatTotalDuration(allServices),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Color(0xff040000),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Total Price:',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Color(0xff040000),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        ServiceCalculator.formatTotalPrice(allServices),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: Color(0xff040000),
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 18,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Add more services button
            if (availableAdditionalServices.isNotEmpty) ...[
              Center(
                child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () => _showAddServiceDialog(context),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Color(0x4dffffff),
                    foregroundColor: Color(0xffc25858),
                    shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24, vertical: 12),
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(25),
                    ),
                  ),
                  icon: Icon(Icons.add, size: 20),
                  label: Text(
                    'Add More Services',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class SettingTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final Widget? trailing;
  final Color? backgroundColor;
  final Color? borderColor;
  final double borderRadius;

  const SettingTile({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    this.subtitle,
    required this.onTap,
    this.trailing,
    this.backgroundColor,
    this.borderColor,
    this.borderRadius = 12.0,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: backgroundColor ?? Color(0x4dffffff),
        border: Border.all(
          color: borderColor ?? Color(0x80ffffff),
          width: 1.0,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(borderRadius),
      ),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4), // Add some vertical spacing
      child: ListTile(
        leading: Icon(
          icon,
          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
        ),
        title: Text(title),
        subtitle: subtitle != null ? Text(subtitle!) : null,
        trailing: trailing ?? const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 16),
        onTap: onTap,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ShopInfoSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> shopData;

  const ShopInfoSection({
    super.key,
    required this.shopData,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final rating = shopData['shopRating'] ?? 0.0;
    final location = shopData['shopLocation'] ?? '';
    final workingHours = shopData['workingHours'] ?? '';

    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x33FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Shop Information',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          _buildInfoRow(
            icon: Icons.star,
            title: 'Rating',
            value: '$rating/5.0',
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 12),
          _buildInfoRow(
            icon: Icons.location_on,
            title: 'Location',
            value: location,
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 12),
          _buildInfoRow(
            icon: Icons.access_time,
            title: 'Working Hours',
            value: workingHours,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInfoRow({
    required IconData icon,
    required String title,
    required String value,
  }) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF), size: 20),
        SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
                  fontSize: 14,
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                value,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/shop_service_model.dart';
import '../Navigations/shop_schedule_page.dart'; // Correct import

class ServicesSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final Map<String, dynamic> shopData;

  const ServicesSection({
    super.key,
    required this.shopData,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // FIX: Properly handle the service data conversion
    final List<ShopService> services;

    if (shopData['services'] != null) {
      services = (shopData['services'] as List).map((service) {
        if (service is ShopService) {
          return service;
        } else if (service is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          return ShopService.fromMap(service);
        } else {
          // Fallback for unexpected types
          return ShopService(
            name: 'Unknown Service',
            price: '\$0',
            duration: '0 min',
          );
        }
      }).toList();
    } else {
      // Default mock data
      services = [
        {'name': 'Haircut', 'price': '\$25', 'duration': '30 min'},
        {'name': 'Beard Trim', 'price': '\$15', 'duration': '20 min'},
        {'name': 'Haircut & Beard', 'price': '\$35', 'duration': '45 min'},
        {'name': 'Hair Wash', 'price': '\$10', 'duration': '15 min'},
      ].map((service) => ShopService.fromMap(service)).toList();
    }

    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x33FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Services & Prices',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 20,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...services.map((shopService) => _buildServiceItem(
            context: context,
            service: shopService,
          )),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildServiceItem({
    required BuildContext context,
    required ShopService service,
  }) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () {
        // FIX: Use ShopSchedulePage instead of ScheduleAppointmentPage
        Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (context) => ShopSchedulePage(shopService: service),
          ),
        );
      },
      child: Container(
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  service.name,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  service.duration,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFCCCCCC),
                    fontSize: 14,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            Text(
              service.price,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
                fontSize: 18,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/soonest_appointment_model.dart';
import '../../Services/soonest_appointment_helpers.dart';


class SoonestAppointmentCard extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<SoonestAppointment> appointments;
  final Function(SoonestAppointment)? onAppointmentDelayed;
  final Function(SoonestAppointment)? onAppointmentRescheduleNeeded;

  const SoonestAppointmentCard({
    super.key,
    required this.appointments,
    this.onAppointmentDelayed,
    this.onAppointmentRescheduleNeeded,
  });

  @override
  State<SoonestAppointmentCard> createState() => _SoonestAppointmentCardState();
}

class _SoonestAppointmentCardState extends State<SoonestAppointmentCard> {
  SoonestAppointment? _soonestAppointment;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _updateSoonestAppointment();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(SoonestAppointmentCard oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.appointments != widget.appointments) {
      _updateSoonestAppointment();
    }
  }

  void _updateSoonestAppointment() {
    setState(() {
      _soonestAppointment = SoonestAppointmentData.getSoonestAppointment(widget.appointments);
    });
  }

  void _handleDelayAppointment() async {
    if (_soonestAppointment == null) return;

    final result = await showDialog<Duration>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _DelayAppointmentDialog(
        currentAppointment: _soonestAppointment!,
      ),
    );

    if (result != null && mounted) {
      _processDelay(result);
    }
  }

  void _processDelay(Duration delay) {
    try {
      final delayedAppointment = SoonestAppointmentHelpers.delayAppointment(
        _soonestAppointment!,
        delay,
      );

      widget.onAppointmentDelayed?.call(delayedAppointment);

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Appointment delayed by ${delay.inMinutes} minutes'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.green,
        ),
      );

      setState(() {
        _soonestAppointment = delayedAppointment;
      });

    } catch (e) {
      // Delay not possible, need to reschedule
      widget.onAppointmentRescheduleNeeded?.call(_soonestAppointment!);

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Cannot delay. Please reschedule your appointment.'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.orange,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  void _callShop() {
    if (_soonestAppointment != null) {
      final phoneNumber = _soonestAppointment!.shopPhoneNumber;
      // You would typically use url_launcher here
      print('Calling: $phoneNumber');
      // Example: launch('tel:$phoneNumber');
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_soonestAppointment == null) {
      return _buildNoAppointmentCard();
    }

    return _buildAppointmentCard(_soonestAppointment!);
  }

  Widget _buildNoAppointmentCard() {
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      elevation: 4,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.calendar_today,
              size: 48,
              color: Colors.grey.shade400,
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              'No Upcoming Appointments',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: Colors.grey.shade600,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'Book an appointment to see it here',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.grey.shade500,
              ),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAppointmentCard(SoonestAppointment appointment) {
    return Card(
      color: Color(0x40ffffff),
      shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      // elevation: 0,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            // Header
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Next Appointment',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: Color(0xff000000),
                  ),
                ),
                Icon(
                  Icons.access_time,
                  color: Color(0xffc25858),
                ),
              ],
            ),

            SizedBox(height: 16),
            // Appointment Details
            _buildDetailRow(
              Icons.calendar_today,
              SoonestAppointmentHelpers.formatAppointmentDateTime(appointment.dateTime),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            _buildDetailRow(
              Icons.attach_money,
              'Total: ${SoonestAppointmentHelpers.formatPrice(appointment.totalPrice)}',
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            _buildDetailRow(
              Icons.store,
              appointment.shopName,
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            GestureDetector(
              onTap: _callShop,
              child: _buildDetailRow(
                Icons.phone,
                appointment.shopPhoneNumber,
                isClickable: true,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Services
            if (appointment.services.isNotEmpty) ...[
              Text(
                'Services:',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: Color(0xff000000),
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 4),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                runSpacing: 6,
                children: appointment.services
                    .map((service) => Container(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Color(0x4dffffff), // Your background color
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                            border: Border.all(
                              color: Color(0x4dc25858),
                              width: 1,
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            service,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: Color(0xff000000),
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ))
                    .toList(),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 16),
            ],

            // Delay Button
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
              child: Container(
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                ),
                child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _handleDelayAppointment,
                  icon: Icon(Icons.schedule, size: 18),
                  label: Text('Delay Appointment'),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
                    backgroundColor: Color(0x4dffffff),
                    foregroundColor: Color(0xffc25858),
                    elevation: 0,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      side: BorderSide(
                        color: Color(0x4dc25858),
                        width: 1,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDetailRow(IconData icon, String text, {bool isClickable = false}) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(
          icon,
          size: 20,
          color: Color(0xffc25858),
        ),
        SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            text,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 15,
              color: isClickable ? Color(0xff18458d) : Color(0xff000000),
              fontWeight: isClickable ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.normal,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _DelayAppointmentDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final SoonestAppointment currentAppointment;

  const _DelayAppointmentDialog({required this.currentAppointment});

  @override
  State<_DelayAppointmentDialog> createState() => _DelayAppointmentDialogState();
}

class _DelayAppointmentDialogState extends State<_DelayAppointmentDialog> {
  int _selectedMinutes = 5;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      backgroundColor: Color(0xffffffff),
      title: Text('Delay Appointment'),
      content: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text('Current time: ${SoonestAppointmentHelpers.formatAppointmentDateTime(widget.currentAppointment.dateTime)}'),
          SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text('Select delay duration:'),
          SizedBox(height: 12),
          DropdownButton<int>(
            value: _selectedMinutes,
            isExpanded: true,
            items: [5, 10, 15]
                .map((minutes) => DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: minutes,
                      child: Text('$minutes minutes'),
                    ))
                .toList(),
            onChanged: (value) {
              setState(() {
                _selectedMinutes = value!;
              });
            },
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 8),
          if (_selectedMinutes == 15)
            Text(
              'Note: Delays longer than 15 minutes require rescheduling',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Color(0xfff57c00),
                fontSize: 12,
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: Text('Cancel'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, Duration(minutes: _selectedMinutes)),
          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
            // backgroundColor: Color(0x4dffffff), // Background color
            foregroundColor: Color(0xffc25858),
            // elevation: 0, // Shadow intensity
            shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(50),
              side: BorderSide(
                color: Color(0x4dc25858), // Border color
                width: 1, // Border width
              ),
            ),
          ),
          child: Text(
            'Delay Appointment',
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Color(0xffc25858),
            ),
          ),
        )
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _StatCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final int count;
  final Color color;
  final IconData icon;

  const _StatCard({
    required this.title,
    required this.count,
    required this.color,
    required this.icon,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Color(0x80FFFFFF),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
        border: Border.all(color: Color(0x33FFFFFF)),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Color(0x66DFA8A4),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              Text(
                count.toString(),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 24,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: Colors.black87,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            title,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 14,
              color: Colors.grey[600],
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SubscriptionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Subscription subscription;
  final VoidCallback onUpgrade;

  const SubscriptionCard({
    super.key,
    required this.subscription,
    required this.onUpgrade,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      color: AppColors.cardColor,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Subscription',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: subscription.status == "Active"
                      ? AppColors.successColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2)
                      : AppColors.warningColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    subscription.status,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: subscription.status == "Active"
                        ? AppColors.successColor
                        : AppColors.warningColor,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              subscription.planName,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 24,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: AppColors.primaryColor,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              '${Formatters.formatCurrency(subscription.price)}/month',
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 16,
                color: AppColors.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.7),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size: 16, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
                SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  '${subscription.daysRemaining} days remaining',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: subscription.daysRemaining < 7
                      ? AppColors.errorColor
                      : AppColors.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 12),
            Wrap(
              spacing: 8,
              runSpacing: 4,
              children: subscription.features.map((feature) => Chip(
                label: Text(feature),
                backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                labelStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.primaryColor),
              )).toList(),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 16),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: onUpgrade,
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppColors.accentColor,
                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 50),
              ),
              child: Text('Upgrade Plan'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
