class SubscriptionItemWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final ShopSubscription subscription;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const SubscriptionItemWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.subscription,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;
    final isSmallScreen = size.width < 600;
    return Card(
      shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 2,
      color: Color(0x4dffffff),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 16),
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        side: subscription.isPopular
            ? BorderSide(color: Color(0xffc25858), width: 2)
            : BorderSide(color: Color(0x80ffffff)),
      ),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // Header with name and popular badge
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      subscription.name,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 22,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Color(0xccffffff)
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (subscription.isPopular)
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 8,
                        vertical: 4,
                      ),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: LinearGradient(
                          colors: [Color(0x66c25858), Color(0xCCC25858)],
                        ),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        'Popular',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),

              // Description
              Text(
                subscription.description,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  color: Color(0xFF463954),
                ),
              ),

              // Price and duration
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    subscription.formattedPrice,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 20,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Color(0x99ffffff),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    subscription.formattedDuration,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: Color(0xFF463954),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),

              // Monthly price for longer durations
              if (subscription.duration.inDays > 30)
                Text(
                  SubscriptionHelper.getPricePerMonth(
                    subscription.price,
                    subscription.duration,
                  ),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: Color(0xffffffff),
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../Models/subscription_model.dart';
import '../../Services/subscription_details_dialog.dart';
import '../../Services/subscription_helper.dart';
import 'subscription_item_widget.dart';

class SubscriptionListWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback onSubscriptionSuccess;

  const SubscriptionListWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.onSubscriptionSuccess,
  });

  @override
  State<SubscriptionListWidget> createState() => _SubscriptionListWidgetState();
}

class _SubscriptionListWidgetState extends State<SubscriptionListWidget> {
  final List<ShopSubscription> _allSubscriptions =
      SubscriptionHelper.getSubscriptions();
  int _visibleCount = 3;
  final int _loadMoreCount = 3;

  void _showSubscriptionDetails(ShopSubscription subscription) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => SubscriptionDetailsDialog(
        subscription: subscription,
        onSubscribe: () {
          _handleSubscription(subscription);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  void _handleSubscription(ShopSubscription subscription) {
    // Here you would typically call your subscription API
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text('Successfully subscribed to ${subscription.name}!'),
        backgroundColor: Colors.green,
      ),
    );
    widget.onSubscriptionSuccess();
  }

  void _loadMoreSubscriptions() {
    setState(() {
      _visibleCount += _loadMoreCount;
      if (_visibleCount > _allSubscriptions.length) {
        _visibleCount = _allSubscriptions.length;
      }
    });
  }

  List<ShopSubscription> get _visibleSubscriptions =>
      _allSubscriptions.take(_visibleCount).toList();

  bool get _canLoadMore => _visibleCount < _allSubscriptions.length;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;
    final isSmallScreen = size.width < 600;

    return Column(
      children: [
        // Header
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
          child: Text(
            'Subscribe to the Shop',
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                color: Color(0xffffffff)
            ),
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
        ),

        // Subscription List
        ListView.builder(
          shrinkWrap: true,
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          itemCount: _visibleSubscriptions.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
            final subscription = _visibleSubscriptions[index];
            return SubscriptionItemWidget(
              subscription: subscription,
              onTap: () => _showSubscriptionDetails(subscription),
            );
          },
        ),

        // Load More Button
        if (_canLoadMore)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: OutlinedButton(
              onPressed: _loadMoreSubscriptions,
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                foregroundColor: Color(0xff806C99),
                side: BorderSide(color: Color(0x80806C99)),
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 12,
                  vertical: 12,
                ),
              ),
              child: const Text('Show More'),
            ),
          ),

        // Showing X of Y text
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
          child: Text(
            'Showing ${_visibleSubscriptions.length} of ${_allSubscriptions.length} plans',
            style: TextStyle(
              color: Colors.grey[600],
              fontSize: 14,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class SuggestedProductWidget extends StatelessWidget {

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../Models/working_hours_model.dart';
import '../../Services/api_service.dart';

class WorkingHoursTracker extends StatefulWidget {
  final WorkingHours workingHours;

  const WorkingHoursTracker({super.key, required this.workingHours});

  @override
  State<WorkingHoursTracker> createState() => _WorkingHoursTrackerState();
}

class _WorkingHoursTrackerState extends State<WorkingHoursTracker> {
  late WorkingHours _workingHours;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _workingHours = widget.workingHours;
  }

  void _startWorking() async {
    try {
      await ApiService.startWorkingHours();
      setState(() {
        _workingHours = WorkingHours(
          date: _workingHours.date,
          scheduledStart: _workingHours.scheduledStart,
          scheduledEnd: _workingHours.scheduledEnd,
          actualStart: DateTime.now(),
          actualEnd: null,
          isWorking: true,
        );
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Error starting work: $e')),
      );
    }
  }

  void _endWorking() async {
    try {
      await ApiService.endWorkingHours();
      setState(() {
        _workingHours = WorkingHours(
          date: _workingHours.date,
          scheduledStart: _workingHours.scheduledStart,
          scheduledEnd: _workingHours.scheduledEnd,
          actualStart: _workingHours.actualStart,
          actualEnd: DateTime.now(),
          isWorking: false,
        );
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Error ending work: $e')),
      );
    }
  }

  String _formatDuration(Duration duration) {
    final hours = duration.inHours;
    final minutes = duration.inMinutes.remainder(60);
    return '${hours}h ${minutes}m';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            "Today's Working Hours",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    _WorkingHoursItem(
                      label: 'Scheduled',
                      value: _formatDuration(_workingHours.scheduledDuration),
                      color: Colors.blue,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    _WorkingHoursItem(
                      label: 'Worked',
                      value: _formatDuration(_workingHours.workedDuration),
                      color: Colors.green,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    _WorkingHoursItem(
                      label: 'Remaining',
                      value: _workingHours.isWorking
                          ? _formatDuration(_workingHours.remainingTime)
                          : 'Completed',
                      color: _workingHours.isWorking ? Colors.orange : Colors.grey,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Column(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 80,
                    height: 80,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      color: _workingHours.isWorking ? Colors.green : Colors.grey[300],
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      _workingHours.isWorking ? Icons.work : Icons.work_off,
                      color: Colors.white,
                      size: 40,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  if (!_workingHours.isWorking && _workingHours.actualStart == null)
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _startWorking,
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green),
                      child: const Text('Start Work'),
                    )
                  else if (_workingHours.isWorking)
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: _endWorking,
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
                      child: const Text('End Work'),
                    )
                  else
                    Text(
                      'Work Completed',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.green[700],
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (_workingHours.actualStart != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(
              'Started: ${TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(_workingHours.actualStart!).format(context)}',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600]),
            ),
          ],
          if (_workingHours.actualEnd != null)
            Text(
              'Ended: ${TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(_workingHours.actualEnd!).format(context)}',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600]),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _WorkingHoursItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Color color;

  const _WorkingHoursItem({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 12,
          height: 12,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: color,
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Text(
          '$label: ',
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
        ),
        Text(
          value,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            color: color,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../Models/points_model.dart';
import '../Services/points_service.dart';

class PointsProvider with ChangeNotifier {
  PointsModel _points = PointsModel(
    totalPoints: 0,
    transactions: [],
    completedOneTimeActions: {},
  );

  PointsModel get points => _points;

  void addPoints(PointsType type, {String? shopName}) {
    _points = PointsService.addPoints(
      currentPoints: _points,
      type: type,
      shopName: shopName,
    );
    notifyListeners();
    _saveToStorage();
  }

  bool canEarnPoints(PointsType type) {
    return PointsService.canEarnPoints(_points, type);
  }

  void loadPoints(PointsModel points) {
    _points = points;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _saveToStorage() {
    // Implement your storage logic here (SharedPreferences, Hive, etc.)
    // For now, we'll just print to console
    if (kDebugMode) {
      print('Points saved: ${_points.toJson()}');
    }
  }
}

class AppRoutes {
  static Map<String, WidgetBuilder> routes = {
    '/': (context) => LoginPage(),
    '/login': (context) => LoginPage(),
    '/signup': (context) => const SignupPage(),
    '/customer_home': (context) => const CustomerHomePage(),
    '/barber_home': (context) => const BarberHomePage(),
    // '/owner_home': (context) => const OwnerHomePage(),
    '/admin_home': (context) => const AdminHomePage(),
    '/history': (context) => const HistoryPage(),
    '/forgot-password': (context) => const ForgotPasswordPage(),
    '/otp-verification': (context) {
      final email = ModalRoute.of(context)!.settings.arguments as String;
      return OtpVerificationPage(email: email);
    },
    '/reset-password': (context) {
      final token = ModalRoute.of(context)!.settings.arguments as String;
      return ResetPasswordPage(resetToken: token);
    },
    '/shops': (context) => const ShopListPage(),
    '/profile': (context) => const ProfilePage(),
    '/appointments': (context) => const AppointmentsPage(),
    '/cart': (context) => const CartPage(),
    '/logout': (context) => const LogoutPage(),
    '/settings': (context) => const SettingsPage(),
    '/favorites': (context) => const FavoritesPage(),
    '/report': (context) => const ReportPage(),
    '/owner': (context) => const ShopOwnerDashboard(),
    '/staff': (context) => const StaffManagementPage(),
    // '/favorites': (context) => ChangeNotifierProvider(
    //   create: (_) => FavoritesProvider(FavoritesService()),
    //   child: const FavoritesPage(),),
  };
}

  static Future<List<Barber>> getBarbers({String? branchId}) async {
    final staff = await getStaffMembers(branchId: branchId);
    final barbers = staff.where((member) => member.role == StaffRole.barber).toList();

    // Convert to Barber with performance data
    return barbers.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
      final index = entry.key;
      final staffMember = entry.value;
      return Barber.fromStaffMember(
        staffMember,
        rating: 4.5 + (index * 0.1),
        totalAppointments: 100 + (index * 50),
        totalEarnings: 5000 + (index * 2500).toDouble(),
        specialties: staffMember.specialties ?? ['Haircut', 'Beard Trim'],
        servicesCount: {'Haircut': 70 + index * 20, 'Beard Trim': 30 + index * 10},
        lastActive: DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(hours: index * 2)),
      );
    }).toList();
  }

  static Future<List<BarberPerformance>> getBarberPerformance(String barberId, DateTime startDate, DateTime endDate) async {
    await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 500));

    // Mock performance data
    return List.generate(30, (index) {
      final date = DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(days: 29 - index));
      return BarberPerformance(
        staffId: barberId,
        date: date,
        appointmentsCompleted: 5 + (index % 10),
        totalRevenue: 200.0 + (index * 10.0),
        tips: 20.0 + (index * 2.0),
        rating: 4.0 + (index % 5 * 0.2),
        servicesBreakdown: {'Haircut': 3, 'Beard Trim': 2},
      );
    });
  }

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../Models/appointment_model.dart';

class AppointmentService {
  static const String baseUrl = 'https://your-api-url.com/api/appointments';

  static Future<List<ShopAppointment>> fetchAppointments() async {
    try {
      final response = await http.get(Uri.parse(baseUrl));

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        final List<dynamic> data = json.decode(response.body);
        return data.map((json) => ShopAppointment.fromJson(json)).toList();
      } else {
        throw Exception('Failed to load appointments: ${response.statusCode}');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // For demo purposes, return mock data
      print('Error fetching appointments: $e');
      return _getMockAppointments();
    }
  }

  static List<ShopAppointment> _getMockAppointments() {
    return [
      ShopAppointment(
        id: '1',
        imagePath: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1562322140-8baeececf3df?w=400',
        title: 'Haircut & Styling',
        status: 'completed',
        totalPrice: 45.00,
        serviceTime: '30 mins',
        date: '2024-01-15',
        barberName: 'John Doe',
        barberNumber: '+1234567890',
        barberShopName: 'Modern Cuts',
      ),
      ShopAppointment(
        id: '2',
        imagePath: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1599351431202-1e0f0137899a?w=400',
        title: 'Beard Trim',
        status: 'pending',
        totalPrice: 25.00,
        serviceTime: '20 mins',
        date: '2024-01-16',
        barberName: 'Mike Smith',
        barberNumber: '+1234567891',
        barberShopName: 'Elite Barbers',

      ShopAppointment(

}

import 'package:logger/logger.dart';

class AuthService {
  final Logger _logger = Logger();
  static const String baseUrl = 'https://your-api.com/api/v1/auth'; // Update with your actual API URL

  // static void logout() {
  //   // Implement your logout logic here
  //   print('User logged out');
  // }
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> login(String email, String password) async {
    // Replace with your actual API call
    try {
      // Mock API response - replace with real API call
      final response = await _mockLoginApi(email, password);
      return response;
    } catch (e) {
      _logger.e('Login error: $e');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _mockLoginApi(String email, String password) async {
    // Simulate API delay
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));

    // Replace this with your actual API response structure
    return {
      'token': 'your_jwt_token',
      'user': {
        'id': 1,
        'email': email,
        'role': 'customer', // This would come from your API
        'name': 'John Doe',
      }
    };
  }

  // Signup method - ADD THIS INSIDE THE CLASS
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> signup(
      String firstName,
      String secondName,
      String email,
      String phoneNumber,
      String gender,
      String password,
      String role,
      )  async {
    try {
      // Replace with your actual signup API call
      final response = await _mockSignupApi(
        firstName,
        secondName,
        email,
        phoneNumber,
        gender,
        password,

      );
      return response;

    // Simulate API delay
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));

    // Mock response - in real app, this would send SMS/WhatsApp OTP
    return {
      'success': true,
      'message': 'OTP sent to your registered phone number',
      'otpLength': 6, // For UI to know how many OTP digits to expect
    };
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> verifyPasswordResetOtp({
    required String email,
    required String otp,
  })  async {
    try {
      _logger.i('Verifying OTP for email: $email');

      final response = await _mockVerifyOtpApi(email: email, otp: otp);

      _logger.i('OTP verification: ${response['success']}');
      return response;
    } catch (e) {
      _logger.e('Verify OTP error: $e');
      return {
        'success': false,
        'message': 'OTP verification failed. Please try again.',
      };
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _mockVerifyOtpApi({
    required String email,
    required String otp,
  })  async {
    // Simulate API delay
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));

    // Mock verification - in real app, verify against what was sent
    // For demo purposes, any 6-digit OTP starting with '123' will work
    final isValid = otp.length == 6 && otp.startsWith('123');

    return {
      'success': isValid,
      'message': isValid ? 'OTP verified successfully' : 'Invalid OTP',
      'resetToken': isValid ? 'reset_token_after_otp_verification' : null,
    };
  }
}


import '../Models/cart_model.dart';

class CartHelpers {
  static double calculateTotalPrice(List<CartItem> cartItems) {
    return cartItems.fold(0, (total, item) => total + item.totalPrice);
  }

  static String formatPrice(double price) {
    return '\$${price.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }

  static void increaseQuantity(CartItem item) {
    item.quantity++;
  }

  static void decreaseQuantity(CartItem item) {
    if (item.quantity > 1) {
      item.quantity--;
    }
  }

  static void removeItem(List<CartItem> cartItems, String itemId) {
    cartItems.removeWhere((item) => item.id == itemId);
  }

  static void addToCart(List<CartItem> cartItems, Product product) {
    final existingItem = cartItems.firstWhere(
      (item) => item.id == product.id,
      orElse: () => CartItem(
        id: '',
        name: '',
        price: 0,
        imageUrl: '',
        quantity: 0,
      ),
    );

    if (existingItem.id.isNotEmpty) {
      existingItem.quantity++;
    } else {
      cartItems.add(CartItem(
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        price: product.price,
        imageUrl: product.imageUrl,
        quantity: 1,
      ));
    }
  }
}
// import 'dart:convert';
// import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../Models/inventory_item.dart';

class InventoryService {
  // Use a list instead of final to allow modification
  static final List<InventoryItem> _mockInventory = [
    InventoryItem(
      id: '1',
      name: 'Shampoo',
      count: 5,
      category: 'Hair Care',
      minStockLevel: 3,
      price: 15.99,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
    InventoryItem(
      id: '2',
      name: 'Conditioner',
      count: 2,
      category: 'Hair Care',
      minStockLevel: 3,
      price: 12.99,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
    InventoryItem(
      id: '3',
      name: 'Hair Gel',
      count: 8,
      category: 'Styling',
      minStockLevel: 5,
      price: 8.99,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
    InventoryItem(
      id: '4',
      name: 'Scissors',
      count: 1,
      category: 'Tools',
      minStockLevel: 2,
      price: 25.99,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
    InventoryItem(
      id: '5',
      name: 'Razors',
      count: 4,
      category: 'Tools',
      minStockLevel: 5,
      price: 12.99,
      imageUrl: '',
    ),
  ];

  Future<List<InventoryItem>> getInventoryItems() async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
    return List<InventoryItem>.from(_mockInventory);
  }

  Future<InventoryItem> updateItemCount(String itemId, int newCount) async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));

    final item = _mockInventory.firstWhere((item) => item.id == itemId);
    final updatedItem = item.copyWith(count: newCount, lastUpdated: DateTime.now());

    final index = _mockInventory.indexWhere((item) => item.id == itemId);
    _mockInventory[index] = updatedItem;

    return updatedItem;
  }

  Future<InventoryItem> addItem(
    String name, {
    int initialCount = 0,
    String? description,
    String? category,
    int? minStockLevel,
    double? price,
    String? imageUrl,
  }) async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));

    final newItem = InventoryItem(
      id: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
      name: name,
      count: initialCount,
      description: description,
      lastUpdated: DateTime.now(),
      category: category,
      minStockLevel: minStockLevel,
      price: price,
      imageUrl: imageUrl,
    );

    _mockInventory.add(newItem);
    return newItem;
  }

  Future<void> deleteItem(String itemId) async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    _mockInventory.removeWhere((item) => item.id == itemId);
  }

  List<InventoryItem> getLowStockItems(List<InventoryItem> items) {
    return items.where((item) => item.isLowStock).toList();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class LanguageService {
  final ValueNotifier<Locale?> localeNotifier = ValueNotifier(null);
  final ValueNotifier<String> languageNotifier = ValueNotifier('English');

  Future<void> init() async {
    // Load saved language preference
    // For demo, we'll use English
    localeNotifier.value = const Locale('en', 'US');
    languageNotifier.value = 'English';
  }

  void changeLanguage(Locale locale) {
    localeNotifier.value = locale;
    if (locale.languageCode == 'ar') {
      languageNotifier.value = 'العربية';
    } else {
      languageNotifier.value = 'English';
    }
    // Save preference to storage
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../Packages/HomePages/customer_home.dart';
import '../Packages/HomePages/barber_home.dart';
import '../Packages/HomePages/admin_home.dart';

class NavigationService {
  static String getHomeRouteBasedOnRole(String role) {
    switch (role.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'customer':
        return '/customer_home';
      case 'barber':
        return '/barber_home';
      case 'owner':
        return '/owner_home';
      case 'admin':
        return '/admin_home';
      default:
        throw Exception('Unknown role: $role');
    }
  }

  static void navigateToHomeBasedOnRole(BuildContext context, String role) {
    try {
      final routeName = getHomeRouteBasedOnRole(role);
      Navigator.pushReplacementNamed(context, routeName);
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Navigation error: ${e.toString()}')),
      );
    }
  }

  static Widget getHomeWidgetBasedOnRole(String role) {
    switch (role.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'customer':
        return const CustomerHomePage();
      case 'barber':
        return const BarberHomePage();
      case 'owner':
        return const BarberHomePage();
      case 'admin':
        return const AdminHomePage();
      default:
        throw Exception('Unknown role: $role');
    }
  }
}

      content: LogoutConfirmationOverlay(onConfirm: onConfirm),
    );
  }

  static void _showDialog({
    required String title,
    required String icon,
    required Widget content,
    bool isForm = false,
  }) {
    final context = SideNavigationService.navigatorKey.currentContext;
    if (context == null) return;

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => Dialog(
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              colors: [
                Color(0x4dc25858),
                Color(0x80806C99),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, // Key property - hug content
              children: [
                // Header
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Image.asset(
                      icon,
                      width: 30,
                      height: 30,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Text(
                      title,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 20,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Colors.black, // Added for better contrast
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    IconButton(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.black), // Added black color
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                const Divider(color: Colors.black54), // Added color for better visibility
                const SizedBox(height: 4),

                // Content - no fixed height constraints
                isForm ? SingleChildScrollView(child: content) : content,
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // static Widget _buildLogoutConfirmationContent({

import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;
import 'package:printing/printing.dart';
import '../Utils/formatters.dart';
import '../Models/profit_data.dart';
import '../Models/staff_models.dart';
import '../Models/barber.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

class PdfService {
  // Your existing method
  static Future<void> generateProfitReport(
    List<ProfitData> data,
    String period,
    String fileName
  ) async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();

    pdf.addPage(
      pw.Page(
        pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
        build: (pw.Context context) {
          return pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Header(level: 0, child: pw.Text('Profit Report - $period')),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 20),
              _buildProfitTable(data),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );

    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (PdfPageFormat format) async => pdf.save(),
    );
  }

  // NEW: Staff performance report
  static Future<void> generateStaffPerformanceReport(
    List<Barber> barbers,
    StaffReportRequest request,
  ) async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();

    pdf.addPage(
      pw.Page(
        pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
        build: (pw.Context context) {
          return pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Header(
                level: 0,
                child: pw.Text(
                  'Staff Performance Report',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 24,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
              pw.Text(
                'Period: ${Formatters.formatDate(request.startDate)} - ${Formatters.formatDate(request.endDate)}',
                style: const pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 20),
              _buildStaffPerformanceTable(barbers),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 30),
              _buildPerformanceSummary(barbers),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );

    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (PdfPageFormat format) async => pdf.save(),
    );
  }

  // NEW: Staff payroll report
  static Future<void> generatePayrollReport(
    List<StaffMember> staff,
    DateTime period,
  ) async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();

    pdf.addPage(
      pw.Page(
        pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
        build: (pw.Context context) {
          return pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Header(
                level: 0,
                child: pw.Text(
                  'Payroll Report - ${DateFormat('MMMM yyyy').format(period)}',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 24,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 20),
              _buildPayrollTable(staff),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );

    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (PdfPageFormat format) async => pdf.save(),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildProfitTable(List<ProfitData> data) {
    return pw.Table(
      border: pw.TableBorder.all(),
      children: [
        pw.TableRow(
          children: [
            pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
              child: pw.Text('Date', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
            pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
              child: pw.Text('Revenue', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
            pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
              child: pw.Text('Profit', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
            pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
              child: pw.Text('Expenses', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        for (var item in data)
          pw.TableRow(
            children: [
              pw.Padding(
                padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
                child: pw.Text(Formatters.formatDate(item.date)),
              ),
              pw.Padding(
                padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
                child: pw.Text(Formatters.formatCurrency(item.revenue)),
              ),
              pw.Padding(
                padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
                child: pw.Text(Formatters.formatCurrency(item.profit)),
              ),
              pw.Padding(
                padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
                child: pw.Text(Formatters.formatCurrency(item.expenses)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildStaffPerformanceTable(List<Barber> barbers) {
    return pw.Table(
      border: pw.TableBorder.all(),
      children: [
        pw.TableRow(
          children: [
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Name', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Appointments', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Earnings', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Rating', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Specialties', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
          ],
        ),
        for (var barber in barbers)
          pw.TableRow(
            children: [
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(barber.name)),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(barber.totalAppointments.toString())),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(Formatters.formatCurrency(barber.totalEarnings))),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(barber.rating.toStringAsFixed(1))),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(barber.specialties.join(', '))),
            ],
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildPerformanceSummary(List<Barber> barbers) {
    final totalAppointments = barbers.fold(0, (sum, barber) => sum + barber.totalAppointments);
    final totalEarnings = barbers.fold(0.0, (sum, barber) => sum + barber.totalEarnings);
    final averageRating = barbers.fold(0.0, (sum, barber) => sum + barber.rating) / barbers.length;

    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border.all(),
        borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: pw.Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          pw.Text('Performance Summary', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          pw.Text('Total Appointments: $totalAppointments'),
          pw.Text('Total Earnings: ${Formatters.formatCurrency(totalEarnings)}'),
          pw.Text('Average Rating: ${averageRating.toStringAsFixed(1)}'),
          pw.Text('Number of Barbers: ${barbers.length}'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildPayrollTable(List<StaffMember> staff) {
    return pw.Table(
      border: pw.TableBorder.all(),
      children: [
        pw.TableRow(
          children: [
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Name', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Role', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Hourly Rate', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Estimated Hours', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
            pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('Monthly Pay', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold))),
          ],
        ),
        for (var member in staff)
          pw.TableRow(
            children: [
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(member.name)),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(member.roleString)),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(member.hourlyRate != null ? Formatters.formatCurrency(member.hourlyRate!) : 'N/A')),
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text('160')), // Assuming 160 hours/month
              pw.Padding(padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(8.0), child: pw.Text(member.hourlyRate != null ? Formatters.formatCurrency(member.hourlyRate! * 160) : 'N/A')),
            ],
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import '../Models/points_model.dart';

class PointsService {
  static const Map<PointsType, int> pointsValues = {
    PointsType.inviteFriend: 25,
    PointsType.rateShop: 15,
    PointsType.arriveOnTime: 10,
    PointsType.shopFeedback: 20,
    PointsType.rateApp: 50,
    PointsType.appFeedback: 50,
    PointsType.makeAppointment: 10,
  };

  static const Map<PointsType, String> pointsDescriptions = {
    PointsType.inviteFriend: 'Invited a friend',
    PointsType.rateShop: 'Rated a shop',
    PointsType.arriveOnTime: 'Arrived on time for appointment',
    PointsType.shopFeedback: 'Added shop feedback',
    PointsType.rateApp: 'Rated the app',
    PointsType.appFeedback: 'Added app feedback',
    PointsType.makeAppointment: 'Make Appointment',
  };

  static PointsModel addPoints({
    required PointsModel currentPoints,
    required PointsType type,
    String? shopName,
  }) {
    // Check if it's a one-time action that's already completed
    if (_isOneTimeAction(type) &&
        currentPoints.completedOneTimeActions.contains(type)) {
      return currentPoints;
    }

    final points = pointsValues[type] ?? 0;
    final description = _buildDescription(type, pointsDescriptions[type]!, shopName);

    final newTransaction = PointsTransaction(
      type: type,
      points: points,
      date: DateTime.now(),
      description: description,
    );

    final newCompletedActions = {...currentPoints.completedOneTimeActions};
    if (_isOneTimeAction(type)) {
      newCompletedActions.add(type);
    }

    return currentPoints.copyWith(
      totalPoints: currentPoints.totalPoints + points,
      transactions: [...currentPoints.transactions, newTransaction],
      completedOneTimeActions: newCompletedActions,
    );
  }

  static bool _isOneTimeAction(PointsType type) {
    return type == PointsType.rateApp || type == PointsType.appFeedback;
  }

  static String _buildDescription(PointsType type, String baseDescription, String? shopName) {
    switch (type) {
      case PointsType.rateShop:
      case PointsType.shopFeedback:
        return '$baseDescription${shopName != null ? ' - $shopName' : ''}';
      default:
        return baseDescription;
    }
  }

  static bool canEarnPoints(PointsModel points, PointsType type) {
    if (_isOneTimeAction(type)) {
      return !points.completedOneTimeActions.contains(type);
    }
    return true;
  }

  static int getPointsValue(PointsType type) {
    return pointsValues[type] ?? 0;
  }
}

class PointsTrackerHelpers {
  // Action styling helpers
  static Color getActionColor(PointsType type) {

        return Colors.green;
      case PointsType.rateShop:
        return Colors.blue;
      case PointsType.arriveOnTime:
        return Colors.orange;
      case PointsType.shopFeedback:
        return Colors.purple;
      case PointsType.rateApp:
        return Colors.orange;
      case PointsType.appFeedback:
        return Colors.purple;
      case PointsType.makeAppointment:
        return Colors.teal;
    }
  }

  static IconData getActionIcon(PointsType type) {
    switch (type) {
      case PointsType.inviteFriend:
        return Icons.person_add_alt_1_rounded;
      case PointsType.rateShop:
        return Icons.star_rate_rounded;
      case PointsType.arriveOnTime:
        return Icons.access_time_rounded;
      case PointsType.shopFeedback:
        return Icons.reviews_rounded;
      case PointsType.rateApp:
        return Icons.star_rate_rounded;
      case PointsType.appFeedback:
        return Icons.feedback_rounded;
      case PointsType.makeAppointment:
        return Icons.calendar_today_rounded;
    }
  }

  static String getActionSubtitle(PointsType type) {
    switch (type) {
      case PointsType.inviteFriend:
        return 'Share with friends';
      case PointsType.rateShop:
        return 'After service completion';
      case PointsType.arriveOnTime:
        return 'For each appointment';
      case PointsType.shopFeedback:
        return 'Share your experience';
      case PointsType.rateApp:
        return 'One-time reward';
      case PointsType.appFeedback:
        return 'One-time reward';
      case PointsType.makeAppointment:
        return 'For each booking';
    }
  }

  // UI component builders
  static Widget buildQuickActionChip(
    BuildContext context,
    String text,
    IconData icon,
    Color color,
    VoidCallback onTap,
  ) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            colors: [color.withOpacity(0.1), color.withOpacity(0.05)],
          ),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.3)),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(icon, size: 16, color: color),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              text,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: color,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  static Widget buildPointsRule(String title, String points, String description) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 16, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      title,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.green.shade100,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        points,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.green.shade800,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Text(
                  description,
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // Success message helper
  static void showSuccessMessage(BuildContext context, String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Row(
          children: [

        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../Models/shop_schedule_model.dart';
// import '../Models/shop_service_model.dart';

class ScheduleHelper {
  // Mock data - replace with actual API calls
  static final List<DateTime> _unavailableDates = [
    DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 1)),
    DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 3)),
    DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 5)),
  ];

  static final List<TimeOfDay> _availableTimeSlots = [
    TimeOfDay(hour: 9, minute: 0),
    TimeOfDay(hour: 10, minute: 0),
    TimeOfDay(hour: 11, minute: 0),
    TimeOfDay(hour: 14, minute: 0),
    TimeOfDay(hour: 15, minute: 0),
    TimeOfDay(hour: 16, minute: 0),
  ];

  static final Map<DateTime, List<TimeOfDay>> _bookedSlots = {
    DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 2)): [
      TimeOfDay(hour: 10, minute: 0),
      TimeOfDay(hour: 14, minute: 0),
    ],
    DateTime.now().add(Duration(days: 4)): [
      TimeOfDay(hour: 9, minute: 0),
      TimeOfDay(hour: 15, minute: 0),
    ],
  };

  static List<DateTime> get unavailableDates => _unavailableDates;
  static List<TimeOfDay> get availableTimeSlots => _availableTimeSlots;

  static bool isDateAvailable(DateTime date) {
    if (date.isBefore(DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(days: 1)))) {
      return false;
    }

    for (DateTime unavailableDate in _unavailableDates) {
      if (date.year == unavailableDate.year &&
          date.month == unavailableDate.month &&
          date.day == unavailableDate.day) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  static bool isTimeAvailable(DateTime date, TimeOfDay time) {
    final bookedTimes = _bookedSlots[date];
    if (bookedTimes == null) return true;

    return !bookedTimes.any((bookedTime) =>
        bookedTime.hour == time.hour && bookedTime.minute == time.minute);
  }

  static List<TimeOfDay> getAvailableTimesForDate(DateTime date) {
    return _availableTimeSlots
        .where((time) => isTimeAvailable(date, time))
        .toList();
  }

  static Future<void> bookSchedule(ShopScheduleSlot scheduleSlot) async {
    // Implement actual booking logic here
    await Future.delayed(Duration(seconds: 1)); // Simulate API call
    print('Schedule booked: ${scheduleSlot.dateTime}');
  }

  static Future<List<ShopScheduleSlot>> getUserSchedules() async {
    // Implement actual fetch logic here
    await Future.delayed(Duration(seconds: 1));
    return [];
  }
}

class SideNavigationService {

    print('Current route: ${navigatorKey.currentState?.widget.initialRoute}');
  }
}



import '../Models/soonest_appointment_model.dart';

class SoonestAppointmentHelpers {
  static bool canDelayAppointment(SoonestAppointment appointment, Duration delay) {
    DateTime newDateTime = appointment.dateTime.add(delay);
    DateTime originalDate = appointment.dateTime;

    // Check if the delay would move the appointment to another day
    bool isSameDay = newDateTime.year == originalDate.year &&
        newDateTime.month == originalDate.month &&
        newDateTime.day == originalDate.day;

    // Allow delay only if it's within the same day and doesn't exceed business hours
    return isSameDay && delay.inMinutes <= 15;
  }

  static SoonestAppointment delayAppointment(SoonestAppointment appointment, Duration delay) {
    if (canDelayAppointment(appointment, delay)) {
      return appointment.copyWith(
        dateTime: appointment.dateTime.add(delay),
      );
    } else {
      // For delays > 15 min or crossing days, we need to reschedule
      // This would typically involve showing a rescheduling dialog
      throw Exception('Cannot delay appointment. Please reschedule.');
    }
  }

  static String formatAppointmentDateTime(DateTime dateTime) {
    return '${_formatDate(dateTime)} at ${_formatTime(dateTime)}';
  }

  static String _formatDate(DateTime dateTime) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final today = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final appointmentDate = DateTime(dateTime.year, dateTime.month, dateTime.day);

    if (appointmentDate == today) {
      return 'Today';
    } else if (appointmentDate == today.add(Duration(days: 1))) {
      return 'Tomorrow';
    } else {
      return '${dateTime.month}/${dateTime.day}/${dateTime.year}';
    }
  }

  static String _formatTime(DateTime dateTime) {
    final hour = dateTime.hour % 12;
    final minute = dateTime.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final period = dateTime.hour < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM';
    return '${hour == 0 ? 12 : hour}:$minute $period';
  }

  static String formatPrice(double price) {
    return '\$${price.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:na3eeman/Services/subscription_helper.dart';
import '../Models/subscription_model.dart';

class SubscriptionDetailsDialog extends StatelessWidget {
  final ShopSubscription subscription;
  final VoidCallback onSubscribe;

  const SubscriptionDetailsDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.subscription,
    required this.onSubscribe,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final size = MediaQuery.of(context).size;
    final isSmallScreen = size.width < 600;

    return Dialog(
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
      child: Container(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(
          maxWidth: isSmallScreen ? size.width * 0.9 : 500,
          maxHeight: size.height * 0.8,
        ),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          gradient: _getBackgroundGradient(subscription),
        ),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              // color: Color(0xffffffff),
              // Header
              _buildHeader(context),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),

              // Price Section
              _buildPriceSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),

              // Features
              _buildFeaturesSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 32),

              // Subscribe Button
              _buildSubscribeButton(context),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  LinearGradient _getBackgroundGradient(ShopSubscription subscription) {
    if (subscription.isPopular) {
      return const LinearGradient(
        begin: Alignment.topCenter,
        end: Alignment.bottomLeft,
        colors: [
          Color(0x80806C99),
          Color(0x1affffff),
        ],
      );
    } else if (subscription.price > 20) {
      return const LinearGradient(
        begin: Alignment.topLeft,
        end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        colors: [
          Color(0x80806C99),
          Color(0x4dc25858),
        ],
      );
    } else {
      return const LinearGradient(
        begin: Alignment.topLeft,
        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
        colors: [
          Color(0x4dc25858),
          Color(0x1affffff),
        ],
      );
    }
  }

  Widget _buildHeader(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                subscription.name,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                subscription.description,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                  color: Colors.grey[600],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        if (subscription.isPopular)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Color(0xB3C25858),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            ),
            child: Text(
              'POPULAR',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelSmall?.copyWith(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPriceSection() {
    return Container(
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border.all(
          color: Color(0xb33b7b44),
        ),
        color: Color(0x4dffffff),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(
            subscription.formattedPrice,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 32,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Color(0xb33b7b44),
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            'for ${subscription.formattedDuration}',
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 16,
              color: Colors.grey[600],
            ),
          ),
          if (subscription.duration.inDays > 30)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
              child: Text(
                SubscriptionHelper.getPricePerMonth(
                  subscription.price,
                  subscription.duration,
                ),
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  color: Color(0x80806C99),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFeaturesSection() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          'What\'s included:',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 18,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.grey[800],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        Column(
          children: subscription.features
              .map(
                (feature) => Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        Icons.check_circle,
                        color: Colors.green[400],
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          feature,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16,
                            color: Colors.grey[700],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              )
              .toList(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSubscribeButton(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      child: ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: () {
          Navigator.of(context).pop();
          onSubscribe();
        },
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: Color(0x4dffffff),
          shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            side: BorderSide(
              color: Color(0x80C25858),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        child: const Text(
          'Subscribe Now',
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 18,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Color(0xB3C25858),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

        price: 7.99,
        duration: const Duration(days: 30),
        features: [
          '15% student discount',
          'Free shipping on orders over \$30',
          'Extended return period',
        ],
      ),
    ];
  }

  static String formatPrice(double price) {
    return '\$${price.toStringAsFixed(2)}';
  }

  static String getPricePerMonth(double price, Duration duration) {
    final months = duration.inDays / 30;
    final monthlyPrice = price / months;
    return '\$${monthlyPrice.toStringAsFixed(2)}/month';
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class ThemeService {
  final ValueNotifier<ThemeMode> themeNotifier = ValueNotifier(ThemeMode.system);
  final ValueNotifier<String> themeModeNotifier = ValueNotifier('System');

  Future<void> init() async {
    // Load saved theme preference
    // For demo, we'll use system theme
    themeNotifier.value = ThemeMode.system;
    themeModeNotifier.value = 'System';
  }

  void changeTheme(ThemeMode themeMode) {
    themeNotifier.value = themeMode;
    switch (themeMode) {
      case ThemeMode.light:
        themeModeNotifier.value = 'Light';
        break;
      case ThemeMode.dark:
        themeModeNotifier.value = 'Dark';
        break;
      case ThemeMode.system:
        themeModeNotifier.value = 'System';
        break;
    }
    // Save preference to storage
  }
}

// custom_animated_icon.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class CustomAnimatedIcon extends StatefulWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final Color iconColor;
  final double size;
  final Duration duration;

  const CustomAnimatedIcon({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onTap,
    this.backgroundColor = const Color(0xFF4CAF50),
    this.iconColor = Colors.white,
    this.size = 20.0,
    this.duration = const Duration(milliseconds: 1000),
  });

  @override
  State<CustomAnimatedIcon> createState() => _CustomAnimatedIconState();
}

class _CustomAnimatedIconState extends State<CustomAnimatedIcon>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<double> _animation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: widget.duration,
    );

    _animation = TweenSequence<double>(
      [
        TweenSequenceItem(tween: Tween(begin: 1.0, end: 1.3), weight: 50),
        TweenSequenceItem(tween: Tween(begin: 1.3, end: 1.0), weight: 50),
      ],
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _controller,
      curve: Curves.elasticOut,
    ));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleTap() {
    _controller.forward(from: 0);
    widget.onTap();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ScaleTransition(
      scale: _animation,
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: _handleTap,
        child: Container(
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(8),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: widget.backgroundColor,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          ),
          child: Icon(
            widget.icon,
            color: widget.iconColor,
            size: widget.size,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class AppColor {
  // * Convert Color from Hex to type Flutter 0xFFdddddd
  static Color _colorFromHex(String hexColor) {
    final color = hexColor.replaceAll('#', '');
    return Color(int.parse('FF$color', radix: 16));
  }

  //* bacground
  static Color white = _colorFromHex('#e6e7f0');

}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class CustomNeumorphicIcon extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final double size;
  final Color iconColor;
  final double depth;

  const CustomNeumorphicIcon({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.size,
    required this.iconColor,
    this.depth = 2,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: size + 8,
      height: size + 8,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.70),
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        boxShadow: [
          // Dark shadow
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
            blurRadius: depth * 2,
            offset: Offset(depth, depth),
          ),
          // Light shadow
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.8),
            blurRadius: depth * 2,
            offset: Offset(-depth, -depth),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Icon(
          icon,
          size: size,
          color: iconColor,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class CustomNeumorphicContainer extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final double width;
  final double height;
  final BorderRadiusGeometry borderRadius;
  final double depth;

  const CustomNeumorphicContainer({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.width = double.infinity,
    this.height = double.infinity,
    this.borderRadius = BorderRadius.zero,
    this.depth = 3,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: width,
      height: height,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: borderRadius,
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.70),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: const Color(0x6699806C),
            blurRadius: depth * 2,
            offset: Offset(depth, depth),
            spreadRadius: 0,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:ui';

class GlassSliverAppBar extends StatefulWidget {
  final String title;
  final bool showBackButton;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final Color textColor;
  final List<Color>? gradientColors;
  final VoidCallback? onBackPressed;
  final List<Widget>? actions;
  final bool pinned;
  final bool floating;
  final bool snap;
  final double expandedHeight;
  final double collapsedHeight;
  final Widget? leading;

  const GlassSliverAppBar({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.showBackButton = true,
    this.backgroundColor = Colors.transparent,
    this.textColor = Colors.white,
    this.gradientColors,
    this.onBackPressed,
    this.actions,
    this.pinned = true,
    this.floating = false,
    this.snap = false,
    this.expandedHeight = 200,
    this.collapsedHeight = kToolbarHeight,
    this.leading,
  });

  @override
  State<GlassSliverAppBar> createState() => _GlassSliverAppBarState();
}

class _GlassSliverAppBarState extends State<GlassSliverAppBar> {

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final gradientColors = widget.gradientColors ?? [
      const Color(0x3fffffff),
      const Color(0xE6DFA8A4),
    ];

    return SliverAppBar(
      // title: _buildCollapsedTitle(),
      centerTitle: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 0,
      leading: widget.leading ?? (widget.showBackButton ? _buildBackButton() : null),
      actions: widget.actions,
      pinned: widget.pinned,
      floating: widget.floating,
      snap: widget.snap,
      expandedHeight: widget.expandedHeight,
      collapsedHeight: widget.collapsedHeight,
      flexibleSpace: _buildFlexibleSpace(gradientColors),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBackButton() {
    return IconButton(
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
      onPressed: widget.onBackPressed ?? () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFlexibleSpace(List<Color> gradientColors) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [
            gradientColors[0],
            gradientColors.length > 1 ? gradientColors[1] : gradientColors[0],
          ],
          begin: Alignment.topCenter,
          end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
        ),
      ),
      child: ClipRect(
        child: BackdropFilter(
          filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 10, sigmaY: 10),
          child: Container(
            color: Colors.transparent,
            child: Align(
              alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: Text(
                  widget.title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 32,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: widget.textColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  // Color _getColorWithSafeOpacity(Color color, double opacity) {
  //   final safeOpacity = opacity.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
  //   return color.withValues(alpha: safeOpacity);
  // }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

// For more customizable inner shadows
class CustomInnerShadowPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<ShadowConfig> shadows;
  final double borderRadius;

  CustomInnerShadowPainter({
    required this.shadows,
    this.borderRadius = 0,
  });

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rect = Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, size.width, size.height);
    final radius = Radius.circular(borderRadius);

    for (final shadow in shadows) {
      final paint = Paint()
        ..color = shadow.color
        ..maskFilter = MaskFilter.blur(
          BlurStyle.normal,
          shadow.blurRadius,
        );

      canvas.drawRRect(
        RRect.fromRectAndRadius(rect, radius).deflate(shadow.spread),
        paint,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}

class ShadowConfig {
  final Color color;
  final double blurRadius;
  final double spread;

  const ShadowConfig({
    required this.color,
    required this.blurRadius,
    this.spread = 0,
  });
}

// For outer shadows with custom shapes
class CustomOuterShadowPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<BoxShadow> shadows;
  final double borderRadius;

  const CustomOuterShadowPainter({
    required this.shadows,
    required this.borderRadius,
  });

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rect = Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, size.width, size.height);
    final rrect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(rect, Radius.circular(borderRadius));

    for (final shadow in shadows) {
      final paint = Paint()
        ..color = shadow.color
        ..maskFilter = MaskFilter.blur(
          BlurStyle.normal,
          shadow.blurRadius,
        );

      canvas.drawRRect(
        rrect.shift(shadow.offset),
        paint,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class NeomorphicSphere extends StatefulWidget {
  final String activeIconPath;
  final String inactiveIconPath;
  final double size;
  final Color sphereColor;
  final Color shadowColor;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final String? navigationRoute;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? navigationArguments;
  final double iconSize;
  final bool isActive;
  final ValueChanged<bool>? onActiveChanged; // Callback when active state changes

  const NeomorphicSphere({
    super.key,
    required this.activeIconPath,
    required this.inactiveIconPath,
    this.size = 70,
    this.sphereColor = const Color(0xFFDFA8A4),
    this.shadowColor = const Color(0x80242424),
    this.onTap,
    this.navigationRoute,
    this.navigationArguments,
    this.iconSize = 30,
    this.isActive = false,
    this.onActiveChanged,
  });

  @override
  _NeomorphicSphereState createState() => _NeomorphicSphereState();
}

class _NeomorphicSphereState extends State<NeomorphicSphere> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  bool _isPressed = false;
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<Offset> _slideAnimation;
  late Animation<double> _opacityAnimation;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _animationController = AnimationController(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      vsync: this,
    );

    _slideAnimation = Tween<Offset>(
      begin: const Offset(0, 1.0), // Slide up from bottom
      end: Offset.zero,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
    ));

    _opacityAnimation = Tween<double>(
      begin: 0.0,
      end: 1.0,
    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
      parent: _animationController,
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    ));

    // Start animation if initially active
    if (widget.isActive) {
      _animationController.forward();
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(NeomorphicSphere oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);

    // Animate when active state changes
    if (widget.isActive && !oldWidget.isActive) {
      _animationController.forward();
    } else if (!widget.isActive && oldWidget.isActive) {
      _animationController.reverse();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleTap(BuildContext context) {
    if (widget.isActive) {
      // If already active, just show press feedback and refresh/redirect
      setState(() => _isPressed = true);
      Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 150), () {
        setState(() => _isPressed = false);
      });

      // Execute custom onTap callback if provided
      widget.onTap?.call();

      // Refresh current page or navigate to same route
      if (widget.navigationRoute != null) {
        Navigator.pushReplacementNamed(
          context,
          widget.navigationRoute!,
          arguments: widget.navigationArguments,
        );
      }
    } else {
      // If not active, become active and navigate
      widget.onActiveChanged?.call(true);

      // Execute custom onTap callback if provided
      widget.onTap?.call();

      // Navigate to new page if route provided
      if (widget.navigationRoute != null) {
        if (widget.navigationArguments != null) {
          Navigator.pushNamed(
            context,
            widget.navigationRoute!,
            arguments: widget.navigationArguments,
          );
        } else {
          Navigator.pushNamed(context, widget.navigationRoute!);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isActive = widget.isActive;
    final currentIconPath = isActive ? widget.activeIconPath : widget.inactiveIconPath;
    final currentIconSize = isActive ? widget.iconSize + 20 : widget.iconSize; // +20 when active

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = true),
      onTapUp: (_) => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTapCancel: () => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      onTap: () => _handleTap(context),
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          // Neomorphic Sphere (only visible when active)
          if (isActive) ...[
            AnimatedBuilder(
              animation: _animationController,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return Transform.translate(
                  offset: _slideAnimation.value,
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: _opacityAnimation.value,
                    child: child,
                  ),
                );
              },
              child: Container(
                width: widget.size,
                height: widget.size,
                decoration: ShapeDecoration(
                  color: widget.sphereColor,
                  shape: const OvalBorder(),
                  shadows: _isPressed
                      ? [
                    // Inset shadow effect when pressed
                    BoxShadow(
                      color: widget.shadowColor.withValues(alpha:0.2),
                      blurRadius: 5,
                      offset: const Offset(2, 2),
                      spreadRadius: -2,
                    ),
                  ]
                      : [
                    // Outer shadow for neomorphic effect
                    BoxShadow(
                      color: widget.shadowColor,
                      blurRadius: 15,
                      offset: const Offset(4, 4),
                      spreadRadius: 1,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],

          // Icon (always visible)
          Image.asset(
            currentIconPath,
            width: currentIconSize,
            height: currentIconSize,
            fit: BoxFit.contain,
            // No color filter applied - original colors
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
